=================== FINDINGS from workspace b01
FINDINGS - batch 01 (main image ROM 0x1000-0x875C, 143 targets; see results.txt)

0. TOOLING I ADDED (in wip/, not in tools/): chkx.py = tools/chk.py with per-line mode tokens, fchkx.sh/ck.ps1 (fast loop, ~3 s),
   vv.py (variant search over bodies or whole files, ONE chk call), try.py (tries every mode x -mcpu for a file), pub.py (rebuilds
   results.txt + wip/out/*.c), gen.py (types_spec.txt -> include/b01_gen.h with explicit padding), side-by-side asm diff via SIDE=1
   (mips-linux-gnu-objdump of ours vs ROM, marks differing words).  Tokens in a chk line (after the flags):
     @C  = gcc 2.8.1 + -msplit-addresses + "unfilled epilogue" fixup          (canonical LOW-REGION mode, with -mcpu=r4000)
     @D  = gcc 2.8.1 + unfilled epilogue fixup only
     @H  = gcc 2.7.2 (-mcpu=r4000) + lu assembler where `addu sp ; j $31` is NOT slot-filled
     @B/@E/@F/@G/@A variants with -fno-delayed-branch / game assembler (rarely needed), @gcc=<ver>, @isa=-mips1,...
   "unfilled epilogue" fixup = rewrite gcc's  `.set noreorder; j $31; addu $sp,$sp,N`  into  `addu $sp,$sp,N; j $31; nop`.
   (rule of thumb in wip/epifix.py).  The shell tool needs MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 for `wsl -- bash /mnt/d/...` calls.

1. THE LOW PART OF THE MAIN IMAGE IS A DIFFERENT COMPILE SETUP (ROM 0x1000 .. ~0x3900; first normal function seen: func_00203848 at 0x4448)
   Functions of that part (the os/pi/io/tlb layer: func_00200500 .. func_00203908) show:
   * epilogue NOT slot-filled: `lw ra; lw s0; addiu sp,sp,N; jr ra; nop`   (gcc 2.8.1 emits `jr ra ; addiu sp` -> never matches as is).
     Leaf functions are filled normally (`jr ra ; andi v0,v0,0x200`), calls are filled by gcc (`jal f ; sw s0,0x10(sp)`).
   * -msplit-addresses style globals: `lui v1,%hi(D) ; lw v0,%lo(D)(v1) ; ... sw v0,%lo(D)(v1)` (hi part shared / explicit temp reg),
     e.g. func_002005DC (volatile counter loop), func_00201ACC, func_00201DB0 (`lui v0,hi; lw v1,lo(v0)` placed BEFORE `addiu sp,sp,-N`).
   * -mcpu=r4000 scheduling (NOT r4300): with r4000 func_00201B50/00201C80 match (global load hoisted above the `move s0,v0` that
     follows the first call); with -mcpu=r4300 they are 2-3 words off.
   * $fp (s8) is used as an allocatable callee-saved register (func_00201E94, 00202018, 0020095C use s0-s8)  -> gcc 2.8.x register set,
     but gcc 2.7.2 style slot filling in some spots (beqzl that copies the target insn into the slot appears with 2.7.2 only;
     func_00201E94/00202018 miss exactly that one word with 2.8.1; func_002018A0 ends in an endless loop and the ROM still has its
     8-word epilogue after it: gcc 2.8.1 deletes it, gcc 2.7.2 keeps it).
   Best explanation: an unknown gcc (between 2.7.2 and 2.8.0, with -msplit-addresses) for this part; exact config used here is
   `-O2 -mcpu=r4000 -msplit-addresses` + unfilled-epilogue assembler behaviour (token @C), or gcc 2.7.2 (@H) for functions without
   globals (func_002005A0, 00200800, 00200840, 00200AD8 only matched there).  Per-function mode is in results.txt notes ("needs ...").
   Functions above ~0x4400 (func_00203848 .. func_00207B5C) all match the normal setup (gcc 2.8.1 -O2 -mcpu=r4300), as in p1-p4.

2. HAND-WRITTEN ASM IN THE LOW PART (no C possible, listed NONE): func_00200400 (boot/TLB init), 002023C4/002023D0 (mfc0/mtc0 $12),
   0020238C/002023A8 (cache loops), 002023DC (TLB dump), 00202268/002022E0/002028D0/00202994 (exception vector stubs, installer and
   context save), 00202C98 (2 stray words).  They use `addi` (trapping) and `break`, mark the exception layer.

3. CODEGEN LESSONS (all verified EXACT unless noted)
   * Stack-passed struct arguments: a Vec3f parameter that starts in a2 is spilled `sw a2,0x50(sp); sw a3,0x54(sp)` and copied with lw/sw
     to the callee frame; declare `Vec3f` params in both function and callee prototypes (func_002063EC, 002064A0, 002062E0, 002054E0:
     `func_00216288(actor, id, actor->pos, 0)` gives lw a2,8 / lw a3,0xC / lw+sw for z, while f32 params give lwc1/swc1 for the stack word).
   * 16-byte struct (x,y,z,w) copied with 4 lw/sw: `typedef struct {f32 x,y,z,w;} Vec4` (func_002064A0).
   * `x*3` next to a literal-1 argument: `sllv a3,v0,a2` with a2==1 is produced by `&obj->seg[idx]` (24 byte elems) in func_0020694C and
     func_00205628 (this resolves the open "sllv" issue of p2 for this pattern: it appears when the constant 1 is a call argument).
   * `if ((x ^ 1) == 0) A else B` (func_00204BD0, 00206018) is the only form that gives `xori v0,v0,1 ; bnez`; `x == 1` gives li/bne.
   * `u32 f = flags & 0x10000; ok = f != 0;` (lw ; lui ; and ; sltu) (func_00207ABC, as in p1) and `state->unk40 >= info->unk4C` to get the
     ROM's load order (field first, constant second); `if (ok == 0) {x = 0.0f;} else if (...)`.
   * jump table switch with shared return blocks (func_00207738): list the cases in the order of the code blocks in the ROM
     (3 ; 1,2 ; 0 {inner switch: 0,2 ; 1,3 ; default -> break} ; 4), inner default = `r = 0x526C; break;` and `return r;` after the switch.
   * Float literal pool: when asm shows `lui at,%hi(D_800C6B60) ; lwc1` and the word is 4.0/30.0/8.5... in rodata, write the literal
     (30.0f) instead of an extern: chk prints ro=0xC7760:4 and everything else is identical (pool order = first use per object).
     func_00206080: u32->f32 `(f32)(u32)(100 - (n-2)*25)` needs the 2^32 double literal (ro=...:16 = 0.01f + 2^32 double).
   * Struct base kept in a register with negative offset (`lbu 0x1D(v1)` / `lw -0x1258(v1)` after addiu v1,&D_801462C8): write
     `u8 *g = D_801462C8; g[0x1D]; *(u32*)(g - 0x1258)` (a local copy of the base; using the array name twice folds the offsets).
   * Same address, two symbols: when the ROM recomputes `lui/addiu` for the same address twice (func_002016FC: D_800F6CB0 as msg buffer
     and as thread stack end) the original used two different objects: use `D_800F6CAC + 4` for one of them to stop CSE.
   * `while (n != 0 && (dst & 3) != 0)` produces the ROM's `beqz s0; andi v0,s1,3; L: beqz v0 ...; bnez s0,L; andi` double test (func_00200F98,
     00200D18, 00200B14).  `if (n >= 16 && ((((u32)dst & 0xF) == 0) & (dmaOk != -1)))` (bitwise & of two boolean compares) reproduces
     `nor ; sltu ; and` (func_0020095C/00200E04; `~dmaOk != 0` gave different register numbering).
   * `for (i = 0; i < (len >> 2); i++)` with the bound written inline gives `srl v1,s1,2` in a delay slot + `move s3,v1` (func_002012D8);
     a separate `n = len >> 2` local gives different registers.
   * Inline asm `__asm__ volatile("break 1")` compiles to the ROM's `break 1` (func_002017E8).
   * Unused/dead locals are kept: func_00203F04 first copies state->vec130 into a local that is overwritten by the next statement.

4. OPEN PROBLEMS (kept in wip/hard, all structurally identical to the ROM):
   * func_00200C28/00200CA0 (identical duplicates), func_00201064: register numbering of two callee-saved temporaries (sh vs val).
   * func_00203F04: struct-copy temporaries t0,t1,t2 in the ROM versus v1,t0,t1 here (ROM looks as if v1 were reserved).
   * func_002043E0, func_00204FC4, func_00204A68, func_00205F18: callee-saved register numbering / early-return layout (the inlined copy in
     func_00204A68 shows the original used a single result variable `var_v0` with `goto` into a shared `r = 0` block).
   * func_00201E94/00202018 (beqzl), func_002018A0 (epilogue after endless loop): see section 1.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b02
FINDINGS - batch 02 (100 targets, main image ROM 0x87B8-0x10000: AI/steering controller + waypoint-graph manager)
Result: 82 EXACT, 13 PARTIAL, 5 NONE (2 stray padding fragments, 1 probable hand-written stub, 2 giants not attempted). See results.txt.
Header: include/b02_types.h (generated from wip/types_spec.txt by wip/gen_types.py; includes p1_types.h but defines its own B2* superset structs
because p1's Actor/ActorState are too sparse; type names are provisional).

TOOLING
- tools/fchk.ps1 is ~1 s per call.  Wrappers in wip/: vv.py (N full-file variants in one chk call), side.py (side-by-side; marks register-level
  differences with <<<<; lu_side.py's != only compares mnemonics and hides register diffs), done.py (check + copy to out/ + results line).
- tools/asmfn.py only scans asm/entry.s and asm/16E000.s; most main-image functions live in other asm/*.s files: scan all asm/*.s.
- Stray junk words: some functions are preceded by 3 junk words (tail of the previous object's alignment padding), e.g. func_0020D364
  (0xDF64..0xDF6F), func_0020EF54 (0xFB54..0xFB5F), func_0020DDD4 (0xE9D4..0xE9DF); some "functions" are just a stray word
  (func_00207FFC = 0x4500000C, func_0020DC04 = lw/lw/jr fragment).  Use the real start (rom+0xC) in the chk line (results.txt has the real rom).
- Game code that calls sqrtf via 'jal sqrtf' (func_002095F8, 00208AAC, 00208EB0, 00208410) only matches with -fno-builtin added to the flags (gcc otherwise
  expands sqrt.s inline).  There is no inline sqrt.s anywhere in the ROM, so the whole game was probably built with -fno-builtin; the lead should
  check whether the global game flags need it (struct copies / memset were unaffected in my tests).
- Jump tables sit at ROM = VA - 0x80000000 + 0xC00 (read them with python to map switch cases).

CODEGEN LESSONS (gcc 2.8.1 -O2 -mcpu=r4300)
- The original code uses many STATIC INLINE helpers that were also emitted standalone: getRec0 (=func_0020C994: pool0->data[idx*elemSize]), getEdge (=C9B0),
  isBi (=C9CC style switch 1,4,7), find node by key (=CFE0), adjacency collection (=CCE8), min-distance loops.  An inlined result pointer is copied to a
  second pseudo ('addu t1,v1,zero' right after the pointer is computed): reproduce it by calling a static inline that returns the pointer instead of
  writing the address expression inline - this fixed register naming in six functions.
- Early-return form beats nested ifs when the ROM puts 'return 1' blocks in branch delay slots: 'if (m == NULL) return 1; ...' (func_0020D364, 0020EAE0),
  'if (call()) return cmp; return 1;' (002099B4), 'if (goal == -1) return cnt;' (0020B95C).
- Collecting into an output array: write 'out[cnt] = x; cnt++;' (array index + separate counter).  gcc strength-reduces it into the walking pointer plus
  counter exactly like the ROM (pointer init lands in the loop preheader); an explicit 'po++' pointer variable gives different allocation.  Conversely,
  storing through an explicit pointer variable (*lp++ = a) is what makes gcc reload globals inside the loop (func_0020ABF0), while an array-index store
  lets gcc hoist global loads.
- 'extern const f32 D_x;' makes gcc hoist a float constant load out of a loop that contains calls (callee-saved FP reg + sdc1 in the prologue);
  a non-const global is reloaded each iteration (func_0020EDCC, 0020EEA4, 0020AA40, 0020C2E8, 0020C5A0).
- f32 zero trick: 'f32 zero = 0.0f; v.z = zero; v.y = zero; v.x = zero;' gives mtc1 + swc1 x3 (plain 'v.x = 0.0f' gives 'sw $zero'); see func_0020D9C0.
- One variable used for two different loops (first loop counter in a callee-saved reg because the same variable is reused in a later loop with calls)
  vs two variables (temp reg): the ROM tells (func_0020BE7C, 0020B95C).  A count returned from an inline fn and copied ('n = adj(...)') puts the count in a
  temp and the loop bound in an s-reg.
- Parameter 'u8 kind' used in compares and passed on: 'andi 0xFF' at each use and the register stays callee-saved; '(u8)kind' casts on an s32 param spilled
  it to the stack instead (func_0020BE7C, 0020C078, 0020CC0C, 0020B95C).
- 'vel.x += acc.x*tt; vel.y += acc.x*tt; vel.z += acc.x*tt;' (product CSE'd) matches; a named temp changed FP allocation (func_0020E674).
- Evaluation order: if the ROM computes the call first write 'call() + k > (f32)x' (func_0020A458); a value held in a callee-saved int reg across a call
  means a named local assigned before it (func_0020A458: t = cfg[1].unk8 before func_002745D4).
- 'nv = a; nv += rand*b' must use ONE local (func_0020A028), not two.
- Sequential beq chains on adjacent constants (11,12 ; 13,14) need the goto form 'if (t==11) goto A; if (t==12) { A: ... }' (func_00208158); unmerged
  duplicate arms (li v0,0x12C twice) could not be reproduced because gcc cross-jumps them (4 words left).
- switch with default in the middle: 'default: reset; case 0: ...; case 1: ...; case 2: ...' gives the ROM layout (func_00209DAC); a final
  'default: return;' sends out-of-range values straight to the epilogue (func_00209E80).
- A global used as base for fields at several offsets, or whose address-minus-constant is passed on (D_801468A0->unk78 and D_801468A0-0x1860), must be
  declared as an array 'extern T D_x[]' so gcc keeps one base register (func_0020EA10).
- Vec3f by value args reproduce the a1-a3 + stack words; 0x1C-byte option blocks and the 0x50-byte block save/restore are plain struct assignments.
- Unresolved: func_0020BC50 (ROM keeps 'mult' before a loop-top beq with a nop slot, gcc puts it in the slot); func_0020D6E4 (first loop compares with
  (f32)<copy of 0> via mtc1/cvt.s.w); func_00209CD8 and func_0020AA40 (pure register-number rotations of 2-3 callee-saved variables, many variants).

DATA STRUCTURE HINTS (names are guesses; see include/b02_types.h)
- B2Ctl (size >= 0x330): AI/steering controller: owner Actor at +0, target Actor at +0x64, 4-entry node queue q[] at +0x14, candidate array ent[10]
  {a,b,w} at +0xC0, Vec3f array at +0x138, anim entry cur +0x218 / id +0x21C, aim timers +0x2E4/2E8/2EC, state +0x314.
- B2Mgr = D_8013B364 (waypoint graph): pool0 (node records, elem size at pool+0, data at +8) at +0, pool8 (edge records {u16 a,u16 b,u8 kind}) at +8, edge
  count +0xC, 64 open slots at +0x38, node list head +0x24 (B2Node: id, f32 costs 0x18-0x24, state +0x28, parent +0x30, next +0x10).
- B2Obj (stride 0x2E8) arrays D_8011FFC0 and D_8011FE88->actors (+0x138, count +0x140): type u16 at +0xE4, pos at +8, def at +0x18.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b03
FINDINGS batch 03 (append-style)
- Stray words: many "functions" in targets are 1-3 words of stale ROM padding in front of the next 16-byte aligned entry
  (func_002106DC, 00211014, 00212664, 0021333C, 002138E8: not functions).  Several others START with 2-3 stray words
  (func_00212D58, 002131D8, 00212E04, 0020F6B8, 00210178, 00210228...): the real entry is at the first `addiu $sp,$sp,-N`, so chk with
  rom = real entry (list line uses that offset) is EXACT; asm shows `sw $t2,..; jal ...` junk before the prologue.
- Counting/zero loops `for (i=0;i<30;i++) a[i]=0;` compile to the `li v1,29 ... bgez v1; addiu v0,-4` countdown form; `= {0}` calls bzero.
- Loop `while(i<10){...; if (c) break;}` -> top-test + `j top` shape needs `for(;;){ if (i>=10) break; ...; i++; if (id != x) break; continue; }`
  (trailing `continue;` or goto-to-exit gives bne-exit + j; plain while gives rotated loop with beq top).
- `if (a == -1) { if (c) {..} else {..} x = -1; }` : put the common store after the if/else, or -1 is hoisted into a callee-saved reg.
- A value tested then reused as call arg (`sel = L->sel; if (sel == -1) ... use sel`) vs reloading from memory changes the delay-slot filling.
- `if (p != -1) {A} else {B} return 1` (positive test first) matched where early-return versions did not (func_0020F2A8);
  while for two tail-merged calls, write the call twice (once per arm) instead of goto (func_00212828).
- Return-value `return 1` in early-return arms: ROM has `beqz x,end ; addiu v0,zero,1` (v0 set in the slot): write explicit early `return 1;` statements.
- rand() % K + C: write literally, gcc generates the exact mult/mfhi magic (K=4,2,100,32400,250000 seen).
- Callee returning s16 used only for a compare: declare s32 (else sll/sra appears).
- Hoisted invariant: `for (...) if (x == s->target ...)` re-reading the field in the loop gave the ROM's hoisted `move a1,v0` copy; a local copy did not.
- Single global used as several blocks: EntList D_8013B364 (head +0x24, count +4, sel +0x18) is ONE struct: access as array `extern T D[]; L = D;`
  and keep `L` in a local gives the saved-register base (s2/s3).
- BIG ONE: `while (1) { if (i >= 10) break; ...; if (x) break; continue; }` and `for (;;) { same }` compile DIFFERENTLY in gcc 2.8.1:
  the while(1) form keeps the unrotated top-test loop (compare result in a reg, `beql`, `j top; slti` in the slot) and does NOT
  constant-fold the entry test even when i = 0 was just assigned; the for(;;) form gets rotated/folded.  The ROM's 10-try random
  picker loops (func_00213810/002136EC/00213500/00213340) all need while(1).
- Stack-passed pointer to a local (5th+ arg) in a loop gets hoisted into a callee-saved reg (verified on a tiny testcase); if the ROM
  recomputes `addiu v0,sp,N` inside the loop the real code did something else (open: func_00210964).
- Calls with a 5th int arg of 0 (`sw zero,0x10($sp)` before jal, m2c drops it) change the frame (arr at 0x18) - check frame offsets.
- `x = D[idx]` with 12-byte struct from a symbol array compiles to three `lui $at / addu $at,$at,idx / lw` triples (assembler macro): normal.
- Tree-switch (sparse cases): block order in the ROM = source order of the case bodies; matched func_00216108 by keeping bodies in
  numeric case order and writing the complex case as nested positive ifs ending in a single `return 4;`.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b05
FINDINGS batch 05 (24 targets, 11 EXACT, 11 PARTIAL, 2 NONE; all -O2 -mcpu=r4300)

TOOLING
- tools/asmfn.py only read asm/entry.s and asm/16E000.s; patched to glob asm/*.s.
- wip/vv.py: full-file variants separated by "//====" lines, one fchk call (files wip/vx_N.c; fchk rsyncs wip but excludes _v_*).
- Statement-order permutation search (6 statements -> 120 orders in ONE chk call) solved func_002227D0 (7 words off).
- A side-by-side diff with structural normalisation (ignore regs/relocs/branch offsets) shows the true diffs when a register
  assignment mismatch cascades over the whole function.
- Prefix-only compiles (early return) do NOT work for matching: prologue/saved regs depend on the whole function.

SOURCE SHAPE -> CODEGEN
- m2c prints `x * K` operand order unreliably; trust the ROM load order. Write `p->f * K` when f is loaded first, `p->f > K`
  instead of `K < p->f` when the variable is loaded before the constant.
- extern const f32 K: gcc CSEs it across calls into a callee-saved fp reg (needed whenever the ROM holds a constant in $fsN).
- Separate fp variables per m2c temp (var_fv0, var_fv0_2 ...) matter for register numbers; a shared temp changed fp regs.
- Cross-jumping explains many "weird" layouts: write the call twice (once per arm), or a direct store `p->st = K` in every arm of
  an if/else chain (no temp variable); gcc merges the identical tails (shared sw / jal) and fills delay slots with the li.
  A temp variable instead gave li; ...; sw in one place and a different register (a0 vs v0).
- bool result of an inlined predicate: `if (a && b && ...) { call(); ok = 1; } else { ok = 0; }` keeps ok in $v0 (nested ifs with
  repeated ok=0 put it in a0 and leave nop delay slots).
- switch statements: cases emitted in SOURCE order; the compare tree (pivot, <, ==) is generated by gcc. For ROM layouts with
  case blocks in odd order (7,2,1/0x12,default) just write the cases in that order (func_0021D750).
- Calls without argument setup in ROM (arg regs still hold the value) = K&R declaration `s32 f();` and a call with no args.
- sqrtf: gcc expands the builtin inline (sqrt.s + c.eq.s + call fallback). ROM calls sqrtf: declare it through an asm label
  (`f32 b05_sqrtf(f32) __asm__("sqrtf");`) to suppress the builtin.
- struct of 0x38 bytes copied with lw/sw loop: struct of s32 w[14] (u8[] struct gives lwl/lwr).
- Vec3f locals are 16 bytes apart (8 byte alignment); first declared = lowest address; address-taken scalars are placed after
  arrays; spilled pseudos (no free callee-saved reg) become stack slots after them (func_00220EB0 spC0..spCC).
- Struct passed by value (Vec3f / 4-word Quat) in the arg list reproduces the lw/sw copies; f32 args after int args travel in int regs.
- Ternary with two separate fp arms: `(t < 0 ? -t : t) * lim` with `m = t * K0` hoisted into a local BEFORE the if gave the ROM order
  (func_002233CC, func_00224078); `dst = expr` directly in each arm instead of via a temp changed fp reg allocation (func_002233CC).
- `while (0.0f < step) { step = 0.0f; ... }` is the source of the "do {...} while (0.0f<0.0f)" m2c output (func_00220EB0).
- A u16 loaded then sign-extended (lhu+sll+sra) vs lh: keep a local as s32, or s16 temp t = (u16)x + n; v = t.
- value = D[idx] style globals: `gm = D_801462D5` (local) keeps the global in a register across later compares.
- `s32 *tb = D_X; tb[0], tb[-2]` gives one base register for sym and sym-8 accesses.

OPEN
- func_00220EB0: p lands in s1 and the loop temporaries in s2, ROM has the reverse; everything else is structurally close.
- func_00222D40 (inline limit helper result copy lw a1 -> addu v1,a1), func_00222FBC (bnel vs bne, 1 word),
  func_002246E8 (re-materialised li 0x7DA in a cross-jumped arm, 1 word), func_002238BC (6 words, zero-const register).

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b08
FINDINGS batch 08 (main image 0x34028-0x3B3E0, func_00233428..func_0023A4D0; 74 targets, see results.txt)
Flags everywhere: -O2 -mcpu=r4300.  Workflow: wip/ck.sh (fchk wrapper), wip/vv.py (variant checker on fchk, no 40 s sync),
wip/side.sh (side-by-side with game flags), wip/perm.py (statement permutations), wip/a.py (asm of any function, all asm/*.s).

0. TOOLING / ASSEMBLER (needs lead action)
 - tools/gas_compat.py patch (copy: wip/out/gas_compat.py, 3 added lines): the nop that old gas inserts between an FP compare
   (c.lt.s etc.) and bc1t/bc1f must also be inserted when a LABEL sits between them (gcc cross-jumping makes `c.lt; j L; ...; L: bc1tl`
   and the ROM has `L: nop; bc1tl`, i.e. the nop goes AFTER the label).  Add a `lastcmp` flag that survives labels (like `lastmul`) and
   emit the nop when `prev is None and lastcmp`.  Needed for func_0023A284 (EXACT only with the patch); harmless otherwise.
 - GCC 2.8 -O2 expands a plain sqrtf() call inline (sqrt.s + NaN check + call).  The ROM always CALLS sqrtf.  Declaring the callee under
   another C name with the same asm symbol reproduces the call:  extern f32 b08_sqrtf(f32) __asm__("sqrtf");  (include/b08_types.h).
   (sinf/cosf are not expanded inline.)
 - Hex printing of a function's m2c `D_800Cxxxx` float globals: most of them are literal-pool floats of the original object, not real
   globals (chk prints `ro=0x...` when we compile with literals; the ROM pool address is listed).  tools: wip/f32lit.py D_800C82F0 prints
   the shortest literal (e.g. 0.008726647f).  Using literals is needed when the original used them in loops/inlines (see 3).
 - Unused/aliasing: all shell quoting of \n inside C char constants is lost through some heredocs: write C files with the Write tool.

1. SOURCE SHAPES THAT PRODUCED THE ROM CODE
 - struct passed BY VALUE as stack args (Vec3f at the end of the arg list): func_00239DD8 / 00239314 / 002394AC: `o->v = v;` is lw,lw,lw/sw,sw,sw;
   components copied one by one does not match.  Also `f32 a,b,c,d` before it come in a1..a3 + stack.
 - A struct param copied into the callee frame twice (sw home slot ... lw ... sw dest) = the same function INLINED (`static inline void set(Obj *o, ..., Vec3f v)`)
   with a nested struct member access `o->co.ch[0].v = v` (declare the base object with pad[0xA0] + ChanObj member).
 - gcc inlines `inline`/`static inline` functions at -O2 only if defined BEFORE use; non-static `inline void f()` ALSO emits the out-of-line
   body at that place (func_00239AE4 + its user func_00239B54, EXACT in one file).  Several ROM function pairs are "helper + function that
   inlines it" (233920 inlines the wave function 00239D0C three times via a statement macro, 0238D20 inlines 00239234 (world->screen),
   002394AC / 00239314 / 00239DD8 share one chan-setter, 00239760 / 0023A334 / 00237E70 / 002398F8 share a text-spawn helper, 002365FC inlines
   func_0023A284's "approach" 4 times).
 - Float literals in ROM pools are NOT merged per function in these inlined bodies: the ROM has one pool entry per use
   (e.g. 16.0f, 30.0f, 30.0f, 16.0f ...).  `ro=0xC8FE0:48` in func_002365FC is 12 floats.  Writing the inline helper with literals reproduces it.
 - Scalar global loads are hoisted above stores to struct members of another pointer (no alias between a struct member and a scalar global);
   the ROM kept `o->unk40 = D_800D2B40` last, i.e. the global was an ARRAY/aggregate: `extern s32 D_800D2B40[]; ... D_800D2B40[0]` (func_00238EA8).
 - Display-list pushes `dl = D_80110634++; dl->w0 = ..; dl->w1 = ..;`: when 5+ pushes are written with ONE reused `dl` variable gcc
   chains `move`s and batches the stores to D_80110634; the ROM (base-relative offsets from one register, D stored after each push) needs a
   SEPARATE local pointer variable for every push (or at least for the last one, after the call): func_002362D8.  The gbi macro order
   `0xF6000000 | (x << 14) | (y << 2)` (constant first) is needed for FILLRECT words.  float->u32 casts use the generated 2^31 literal pool.
 - Loops that scan strings (func_00239760 family): the ROM shape is `do { p++; if (*p != '\n' && *p != 0) continue; if (start != p) {..} } while (*p != 0);`
   with the early exit written as `n = NULL; if (b == NULL) return n; {...}`; with this the loop-invariant float literals appear at the loop top exactly
   like the ROM, and the `addu v0,a0,zero` ends up before the epilogue.
 - switch with sequential compare chain (1,0,2,3) in the ROM = if-chain with gotos (func_00233B04), `case 1: ... case 0: ... default:` as a
   switch gives a compare tree instead.  switch (c) {case 1: sin..; case 0: rand..; default: 0} gives the block order sin, rand, zero (func_00239D0C).
 - Two compare variants that gave the ROM's FP register numbering: `p->unk18 = (s32)(sinf(x) * 255.0f);` BEFORE `p->unk28 += step` (func_00238040).
 - Parameter (f32 returned by int-typed first arg + stack f32) : float param after an int param travels in an int reg (mtc1) as documented earlier.
 - `s32 id = table[i].s16field;` (not `s16 id`) to get lh instead of lhu + sll/sra at the call (func_00233428).
 - u32 return of an 'is zero' test: `return c->v.z == 0.0f;` matched (func_002337C0).

2. STRUCTURES (names are guesses)
 - Chan (0x14 bytes: mode, Vec3f v, f32 d) / ChanObj {s32; Chan ch[4] at +4,+0x18,+0x2C,+0x40}: look like envelope/emitter channels (wave type 0 = random in
   [-x,x] (func_00274B00), 1 = sin(d*pi/180)*x).  func_00233920 applies such an emitter (radius ch[0].d, strength falloff func_00265734) to a force vector.
 - TextNode / text system (func_00237E70 spawn, 002398F8, 0023A334, 00239760 = text-line spawners from a free list at +0xF24 into list +0xE40; 00238040 / 00238304 update
   and draw them; node: next0, next4, state 0x14 (0 dead,1 wait,2 fade in,3 hold,4 fade out), alpha 0x18, timer 0x28...).
 - Player/pawn objects: pawn+0x29C..0x2A8 = HUD rectangle (x,y,w,h as f32) approached with 4 inlined approach(x,target,step=16) calls (func_002365FC).
 - func_00233B04 is a colour fade state machine (state 0x544, timer 0x538, levels bytes 0x54A-0x54D, current alpha 0x551).

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b09
FINDINGS batch 09 (main image 0x3BBE0-0x4258C, loader/collision code, 85 target symbols)
(append-style; see results.txt for the per-function status)

TOOLING / ASSEMBLER
- Added a chk token `GAME` (tools/chk.py + tools/gas_compat.py mode 'luret'): lu behaviour + a bare `j $31` keeps a `nop` delay slot.
  Needed when gcc ends a function with a struct block-move (movstrsi): ROM has `jr ra ; nop`, modern gas fills the slot with the last sw
  (func_0023C8C4, func_0023E8A4).  Everything else matched with the normal 'lu' mode.
- sqrtf: with -O2 (no -fno-builtin) gcc 2.8.1 inlines sqrtf as `sqrt.s; c.eq.s; bc1t; jal sqrtf`.  Every function that calls sqrtf in the ROM
  (func_002417B4, func_00241830, func_0023E6DC, func_0023E168, ...) is EXACT only with `-fno-builtin` appended to the chk flags.
  (sinf/cosf are not affected.)  I recorded this as "needs gcc -fno-builtin" in results.txt.
- Several "targets" are not single functions: func_0023C914 is three functions (loader thread starter, thread entry D_0023CA14 at 0x3D614,
  list unlink at 0x3D6FC); 7 targets are plain alignment padding words (0023B994, 0023CFB8, 0023CFC8, 0023EDE8, 0024064C, 002406BC,
  0024193C, 0023F734): garbage tail words of the previous object, not code.
- tools/asmfn.py only read asm/entry.s + asm/16E000.s; it now globs asm/*.s.

CODEGEN LESSONS
- In-place accumulate matters for FP register allocation: `up = e->f208; up += x * y;` (one pseudo, add.s f1,f1,f0) matched where
  `up = e->f208 + x * y` gave a different destination register (func_0023B708, func_0023CFD0 `c = d->f14; c *= K;`).
  Likewise a clamp written as ternary `dn = (dn < 0.0f) ? 0.0f : dn;` matched where the if-form changed the register of the loaded value.
- Two arms that end in identical code are cross-jumped by gcc: write the call TWICE (once per arm).  func_0023C074 (osMapTLB / osUnmapTLB
  in both arms) only matched this way; with a shared `goto unmap` the argument registers differ.  Same for inlined helpers: an inline
  with a single return-statement tail after the if/else produced a post-join store; early `return` per arm reproduced the ROM.
- Inlining: Acclaim used `static inline` helpers (func_0023ED54 body is inlined into 0023E8C4/0023EA34/0023DF60/0023E168; the plane-height
  helper is inlined twice into 00240A88; the ray-vs-plane func_002412C0 into 002413B8).  A static inline in the same .c file reproduces it;
  the helper's own copy is a separate global function in the ROM.
- Non-struct stores/loads and CSE: gcc 2.8.1 does NOT invalidate a cached global scalar load after a store through a struct pointer
  (n->prev = g; if (g) ...) and does not reload node fields after a store to a scalar global.  A BLKmode struct copy (movstrsi) however
  invalidates all cached memory: after `D->v8 = a->v180;` the global `D` is reloaded (func_0023E44C).  Offset-0 stores through a plain
  non-struct pointer (`*(int*)n = ..`) also kill cached global loads, offset-4 ones do not (tested with a tiny harness).
  When the ROM reloads a node field after a store to a global list head, store the global through a struct view
  `((B09List *)&D_80103E20)->head = x` (func_0023CBF8) - but using the global ONLY through that view in many places makes gcc hoist its address.
- Loops: `while (n--)` with n a copy of the count gives `addu v0,v1 ; bnez ; addiu v1,-1`; an up-counting `for` over a pointer
  plus `if (cnt != 0) { i = cnt - 1; do {..} while (i--); }` was needed for func_0023CDC4's count loop.
  `for (i = n - 1; i != -1; i--)` (explicit -1 test) reproduced a `bne s0, -1` back edge in func_002414E4.
- Array decl trick again: `extern B09World D_8011FE88[]; D_8011FE88->count` makes gcc keep the base in a register and walk pointers
  (func_0023F42C, func_00240250); `extern T D[]` plus `D->field` for a flag byte hoisted the base out of the loop (func_00240250).
- One struct object spanning two symbols: D_80103254 (load queue) and D_80103D08 (+0xAB4) are the same global; gcc derives one from the
  other (`addiu a0, s1, -0xAB4`) when both are members of ONE extern struct (func_0023CA14).  A thread block + its stack (D_80103950 = base+0x230)
  likewise.
- Switch with jump table (func_0023E44C): `case 1: case 2: case 3: case 4:` with separate arms and identical tails was enough;
  gcc merges the identical `D->unk14 = -1` tails itself.  chk reports ro=0xC9390:40.
- Float compare operand order controls the load order: `h->t >= m->qcopy.t` loads h first, `m->qcopy.t <= h->t` loads m first.
- Dead stores into a local struct are kept (B09V3 p; p.z = ...; unused later) and give the 0x10 frame of func_0023DDB4 / DA90.
- Struct-copy of an f32 triple `B09V3 n = *(B09V3 *)&q->nx;` gives the lw/lw/lw + sw/sw/sw to the stack that the ROM has; later loads are lwc1 from the stack copy.

OPEN (see wip/hard): func_0023CDC4 (loop count hoisting), func_0023CE8C (store/reload scheduling), func_0023E168 (extra callee-saved reg
for &q->hit), func_00240A88 (clamp constant register), func_00241830/00241940/002414E4 (callee-saved FP/int register permutations),
func_00240D10..00241170 (extra callee-saved reg for &local2 although func_00240C9C of identical shape matched).

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b12
FINDINGS - batch 12 (main image 0x50CA4-0x57E10, 122 targets: list/heap/cache "resource manager" module + a few audio-thread helpers)
(append-style; see results.txt for per-function status)

TOOLING
- tools/gas_compat.py fix (copy in wip/out/gas_compat.py): an `$at` macro expansion (`op reg,sym(idx)` or `op reg,BIG(base)`) that is the LAST insn before a
  `jr $ra` must keep a nop delay slot (old gas never moved the last insn of a macro into the slot; modern gas does).  Without the fix func_00254CE4 shows 2 words off.
  Patch = set prev='MACRO' after the symidx_re / bigoff_re expansions and add `prev == 'MACRO'` to the "return keeps nop" condition (2 lines).
- `memcpy(d,s,n)` is turned by gcc 2.8 -O2 into `bcopy(s,d,n)` (swapped args).  The ROM calls memcpy as a plain function; declare/call it via another name
  (e.g. prototype `void *func_002C2490(void*,void*,u32)`) or pass -fno-builtin.  memset stays memset.
- helper scripts that were useful: wip/side.sh (side-by-side with game flags), wip/vv.py (variant search: many full-file variants in ONE fchk call, ~3 s),
  wip/af.py (asm of a function from asm/2BD34.s; NOTE tools/asmfn.py only knows entry.s/16E000.s and prints nothing for this region).
  When reading asm dumps: a `nop` filtered out by grep hides every delay slot; delay-slot insns of non-likely branches print one space narrower.
- the m2c skeleton is good for field offsets/call args but turns delay-slot style `bnez a2,L; <nop>; lw s0,x` into fake `if`s, and drops pass-through args.

COMPILE-UNIT FACTS (module at ROM 0x50CA4..0x57E10)
- Orphan fragments exist between functions: func_00255C3C (addiu a2,0x2800 falling into 255C40), func_002555C8 (jal osWritebackDCache; no epilogue),
  func_00256214 (lw v0,-0x4960(v0); addiu sp; sw s1..), func_002567E8 (addiu a1,1; jal func_0028FD94 + next fn's first insn as delay slot), and the 2 leading words
  of func_00256708 / trailing 2 words of func_00255220 (addiu a1,0x18; jal func_00255C40).  They look like pieces of scheduled prologues/stubs; not matchable as functions.
  func_00257210 and func_00251448 DO start with `lui/lw` before the `addiu sp` (scheduled prologue); func_00256FE0 matched with that default behaviour.
- Literal pool (ro=): func_002500A4 uses the u32<-f32 constant 2147483648.0 at ROM 0xC9B40, func_0025021C uses 255.0f twice (0xC9B44, 0xC9B48): the original
  assembler keeps one pool entry per use.  They live in one object (ro=0xC9B40:4 and 0xC9B44:8 are adjacent).
- Intrusive doubly linked list package (func_00255xxx/func_00256xxx): list header {head,tail,prevOff,nextOff,count}, element links addressed by byte offsets
  (push_front 255C58, push_back 255CB4, remove 255E78, insert_before 255D10, insert_after 255DC4, move_to_front 255F58 = remove+push_front inline,
  move_to_back 256044, pop_head 255F10, pop_tail 255F34, init 255C40).  255F58/256044 compile only with the two helpers as `inline` functions in the file.
- Heap package (0x255854 init, 0x2558C0 alloc-from-front, 0x2559D4 alloc-from-back, 0x255ACC free+coalesce, 0x2555D0 claim range, 0x255720 free-all, 0x2554E0 next pow2).
- The whole module's globals are ONE struct at D_80103F70 (D_80104560, D_80104570, D_80104598, D_8010513C, D_80105180, ... are members).
  Declaring it as a struct (include/b12_types.h B12Mgr, generated by wip/mgr.py) reproduces the base-register CSE of functions that touch many members; but several
  functions (251448, 2512C8, 252F2C ...) only matched with the members declared as separate scalars (`lui/lw` each time): decide per function by looking at
  whether the ROM keeps one `lui/addiu` base register.
- Lock = P3Lock at D_80105140 (queue + buf + count at D_8010515C): the lock/unlock halves are always written as
  `m = __osDisableInt(); if (++cnt != 1) { __osRestoreInt(m); osRecvMesg(&q,0,1); } else __osRestoreInt(m);` and the unlock half with a NEW local (m2) -
  using the same local for both halves changes where the `addu a0,v0,zero` lands (delay slot of the beqz vs of the jal).

SOURCE-STYLE LESSONS
- `n->refs--; if (n->refs == 0) ...` (two statements) vs `if (--n->refs == 0)`: only the two-statement form reproduced the jump-threaded refcount drain loop of func_00254930.
- Return slot shape: `if (x) { return call(...); } return 0;` matched where `if (!x) return 0; return call(...);` put the `return 0` block at the end (func_00250754).
- Two-reg sort: `for (...; ...) { if (p->key > n->key) ...}` operand order decides which load comes first (func_00251590).
- Keep an array/pointer scalar and its store through `B12Node **` consistent: `tab = D_80104564; *(B12Node **)((u8*)tab + cnt*4) = n;` gave `addu v0,v0,v1` (index first) and the reload of cnt after the store (func_002512C8);
  `D_80104564[cnt] = n` gave the operands swapped and no reload.
- Scheduling of loads before stores: a load of a global scalar cannot move above a store through a struct pointer only if gcc thinks they may alias; making the store
  `*(u32*)&n->flags = 0` (not a struct member access) restored the ROM order in func_002512C8; `*(&D_80105134 + type) = 0` (scalar + index, not an array member) restored the
  ROM order of the epilogue loads in func_00254F3C.
- Struct by value: a 12-byte Vec3 followed by an 8-byte pair as the last stack args gives `lw v1,t0,t1 / sw ... ; lw v1,t0 / sw ...` copies (func_002505CC: P3Vec3 then B12Pair).
- Hoisted arg `blk + len`: compute `src = (u8*)blk + len` into a local BEFORE the `if (o->unkDA != X)` so the add lands in the beq delay slot (func_00250458/00250C2C); also load both
  words (off=blk[1], len=blk[2]) before the test.
- `x = y = z = 0` chains give `addu s6,zero,zero; addu s5,s6,zero; addu s0,s6,zero`; initialise OSTime locals AFTER them (func_00256CB0).
- A `for (i=0;i<n;i++)` after an `if (n>0)` emits two guards; drop the outer if (func_002505CC).
- 64-bit OSTime math: `(u32)(((now-last)<<6)/3000)` produces the __udivdi3 call with a2=0,a3=3000 (func_00256CB0).
- Loop-invariant hoisting: constants passed as u64 args (osSetTimer) are NOT hoisted in the ROM but are in ours (func_0025651C unmatched); local base pointers (`B12Cache *c = &...`)
  assigned only inside the if body reproduce the ROM's hoisted s2/s3/s4 (func_00251590).
- Jump-into-loop layout (`j test; body; test: bnez body`) of func_002555D0 could not be produced from while/for/for(;;) (always the peeled form with a top test).

UNMATCHED / PARTIAL (details in results.txt notes and wip/hard/): 0025021C 0025651C 00250E10 00251448 00251754 002508E0 002555D0 00255854 00254E70 00256708 002570E0 00257210 00252FFC

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b13
FINDINGS batch 13 (append-style; sound manager / voice pool code ROM 0x57F80-0x5F500)
- memcpy with a CONSTANT size: gcc expands the builtin inline (struct move loop, or a call to bcopy with swapped args when the
  alignment is unknown).  The ROM calls memcpy(dst, src, 0xCC) as a plain jal.  Matching trick without -fno-builtin:
  `void *memcpy_call(void *, const void *, u32) __asm__("memcpy");` then call memcpy_call(...) (asm-label alias, gcc 2.8 supports it).
  (-fno-builtin also gives the call, but then flags differ from the other game code.)
- Counting mutex inlined everywhere (OSMesgQueue + counter at +0x1C): lock = {mask = __osDisableInt(); if (++l->count != 1) {__osRestoreInt(mask); osRecvMesg(&l->q, 0, 1);} else __osRestoreInt(mask);}
  unlock = {mask = __osDisableInt(); if (--l->count != 0) {__osRestoreInt(mask); osSendMesg(&l->q, 0, 1);} else __osRestoreInt(mask);}
  with a function-level `SndLock *l;` assigned `l = &m->lock;` as a separate statement before EACH macro expansion (the ROM recomputes
  addiu s0,s1,0x110 in the delay slot of every __osDisableInt).  A macro-local pointer or expressions without a local do not match.
- Walking a table of 0xCC byte records in a loop: write TWO pointers: `v = p->v` (used for the field accesses, v++ at loop end) and a
  second `w = p->v` (w++) used for a field at offset 0; array indexing p->v[i].x in the body gives a different induction-variable layout.
  A single `v++` pointer gives the biased base register (first IV = &v[0].lastfield).
- `&p->v[idx]` with s16 idx: ROM computes (idx*0xCC + p) FIRST and adds the array offset 4 as a separate `addiu`.  To get it write
  `v = (SndVoice *)(idx * 0xCC + (u32)p); v = (SndVoice *)((u8 *)v + 4);` (macro SND_VOICE_AT).  `&p->v[idx]` gives (mult+4)+p.
- Unlinking a node `l` of a doubly linked list and then using it as `n`: write the unlink on `l`, THEN `n = (T *)l;` (otherwise the
  copy is propagated and the register numbering differs).  Insert-before-head: `n->prev = c->prev; n->next = c; c->prev->next = n; c->prev = n;`
  (load first) matches; the reverse store order gets delay-slot copies.
- Struct by value out of a pointer: `Vec3f *p = &o->pos; f(m, id, *p, 0, -1);` (pointer local) matched; `o->pos` directly did not.
- 12 byte Vec3f cleared as `v.x = v.y = v.z = 0.0f;` gives swc1 z,y,x order like the ROM.
- KEY (mutex layout): the inline counting mutex must be written as two `static inline void SndMgr_Lock(SndMgr *m)` / `SndMgr_Unlock(m)`
  functions (local `SndLock *l = &m->lock; u32 mask = __osDisableInt(); if (++l->count != 1) {__osRestoreInt(mask); osRecvMesg(&l->q,0,1);} else {__osRestoreInt(mask);}`;
  unlock: `if (--l->count != 0) {__osRestoreInt(mask); osSendMesg(&l->q,0,1);} else {...}`).  With CPP macros gcc 2.8 (jump.c) parks the
  out-of-line mutex arms behind the first unconditional jump (an early `return -1;`, a second return, ...) and flips the branch polarity
  (bne to an arm placed at the top of the function); with `static inline` functions the arms stay inline exactly like the ROM.
  Only matters when the function has more than one return; with a single return both forms give the same code.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b14
FINDINGS - batch 14 (ROM 0x5FB54-0x66B20, 142 targets: bit-stream / keyframe-track decoder, controller+rumble slots, small helpers)
Flags -O2 -mcpu=r4300 everywhere. Status at the time of writing: see results.txt (EXACT/PARTIAL/NONE counts at its end).

TOOLING (affects every worker)
- tools/gas_compat.py: `op reg, sym(idx)` (symidx macro: lui $at / addu $at,$at,idx / op reg,%lo(sym)($at)) must NEVER be pulled into a
  return delay slot: the old assembler left `jr ra ; nop` after such a macro (func_00264790/002647A8: `D[i] = 1;` as the last statement).
  Patch (my workspace tools/gas_compat.py): in the symidx branch set `prev = 'macro'` and add `or prev == 'macro'` to the
  "keep a nop slot" condition.  Plain `sw $2,sym` (2 insn macro) was already right.  Please merge.
- tools/chk.py: with a file wip/_full present it prints every word of a non-EXACT function (I use it for side-by-side listings with
  rabbitizer); stock behaviour (first 4 differing words) otherwise.
- tools/asmfn.py is stale for this layout (asm is split into asm/<ROM>.s files: this batch lives in asm/2BD34.s).
- memcpy: gcc 2.8.1 turns `memcpy(d, s, n);` (result unused) into `bcopy(s, d, n)`.  The ROM calls memcpy: add a conflicting K&R
  redeclaration `void memcpy();` (gcc only warns "conflicting types for built-in function") or use the result.

GENERAL SOURCE-SHAPE LESSONS
- Struct by value: `f(T *p, s32 a, B14W3 w)` gives `sw a2/a3 home slots, lw back` and a struct copy `p->w = w` is lw,lw,lw / sw,sw,sw
  (grouped).  A struct param keeps its home slot and fields are read straight from it (func_002609CC reads `lwc1 0xC($sp)`).
  A struct copy through a block move is opaque to CSE: `B14Tmp l; l.r = r;` (local with an unused first word so the copy lands at sp+4)
  reproduces `copy home -> sp+4, then lw a2,4(sp)` exactly (func_00260BA0, func_00260F18).  Two such temporaries (sp+0 and sp+0x10) appear
  when a by-value struct is passed through an inlined helper.
- Compound assignment keeps ONE pseudo, which decides register reuse: `v >>= sh; hi <<= 32 - sh; v |= hi;` gives `srlv v1,v1,a0 / sllv a1,a1,v0 / or v1,v1,a1`
  (all six bit-reader helpers func_002606E4/002607D8/0026102C/00260DC8/00260E6C/00260BA0 need exactly this); `v = (v >> sh) | (hi << (32 - sh))`
  allocates a fresh register.  Statement ORDER inside (`v >>=` before `hi <<=`) fixes the instruction order too.
- Loop strength reduction decides base registers: indexing (`D->blk[i]`, `p[i + 1]`) lets gcc derive per-array pointer IVs whose initial
  value is created AFTER hoisted constants (func_00261110: `li 0xDEADBEEF` before `move v1,t1`); an explicit pointer variable creates its
  init BEFORE them.  For several arrays at fixed offsets from one global use `B14Mgr *m = D_xxxx;` once (or the array form
  `extern S D_x[]; D_x->field`) so one base register is kept (func_00264D00 / 00264BAC / 00264A40).
- `for (i = 0, s = D_x; i < 4; i++, s++)` (both inits in the for header) vs separate statements changes register numbering (func_00264874);
  a second loop in the same function wants its own variable (func_002648E8: first loop temp regs, second loop callee-saved).
- Two uses of the same `&global` after calls are normally CSE'd into a callee-saved register; the ROM often re-materialises lui/addiu
  (func_00264268).  Inlined helpers (static inline in the original) keep separate expansions: func_00263B14 only matches with
  `static inline s32 b14_lock(s32 flag) {...}` called as `if (b14_lock(0) != 0) {...}` (result tested twice, not merged).
- A `switch` on a u8 gives `lbu; beqz; bltz; slti 4; beqz` (range checks NOT merged into sltiu) -- written as 4 sequential cases
  `case 0: ... case 1: case 2: case 3: ...` (func_00263B14).  Nested ifs `if (x >= 0) { if (x < 4)` were merged by gcc.
- `if (...) return 1;` chains over several [lo, lo+size) address ranges match the `&&` form (`a >= lo && a < lo + size`), with the symbols
  declared as `extern u8 D_xxx[]`; size symbols like D_C5E98 (absolute small numbers) are plain `extern u8 D_C5E98[]` and used as (u32)D.
- const float globals (`extern const f32 D`) are loaded once for several uses (func_00260570); non-const is reloaded after each store.
- u32 -> f32 needs the unsigned correction (ldc1 2^32 literal, cvt.d.w): use u32 operands; signed ints give plain mtc1/cvt.s.w (func_0025FD94).
- Early-return vs else form matters for the beql/delay-slot choice: `if (f(i) == 0) return 0; r = ...; return r;` matched (func_002645A0)
  while the else form did not; for `return r` of a nested call chain the other way round (func_002612C8).
- `(1 << n) - 1` after a float branch is reloaded from the struct copy (n not cached in a local): do not add `u32 n = l.r.n`.
- Global used twice as `D_8014687C[?]` with the ROM base register at the *later* symbol and a negative offset: declare the later symbol as
  `extern s32 D_80146894[];` and use `g[0]` / `g[-6]`, and assign the base inside the condition: `*(g = D_80146894) != 0` (func_00264124).
- Struct by value in the o32 ABI: first FP-looking arg after an int arg is in an INT register; `s32 a0, s32 a1, ...` with `*(f32*)&a1`
  reproduces sw/lwc1 puns; an unused incoming stack arg shifts the real one (func_002615D4 needs a dummy `s32 unused` before `f32 t`).
- Hidden struct return pointer: `B14Span f(f32 a, f32 b, f32 c)` takes a1.. for the floats (a0 = result pointer).
- Callee argument inference: if a2/a3 are not set before a jal the callee takes fewer params than m2c shows (func_00274B00 has two f32 params).
- Bit-address helpers: func_00260684 (x<<3 | keep top nibble), 002606A4 (>>3), 002606C4 (word aligned >>3) and func_0026162C (x & 0x1F) are the
  building blocks (static inline) of all bit readers/writers; inlining them reproduces register order (func_00260744: `sh = b14_ofs(addr)`
  makes gcc keep the `andi` result and a copy of it).
- gcc folds `x >= 0 && x < 4` but not the u8-switch form; `bc1fl`-style likely branches come out of if/else value selects.

STRUCTS / SUBSYSTEMS RECOVERED (include/b14_types.h, provisional names)
- B14Ctl: 0x224-byte controller/rumble slot, array of 4 at D_8010F328 (+0 active, +4 port, +0x2C ent[32] {a,b,c=0x30,d}, +0xB4 flag word,
  +0xC8 rumble present, +0xCC enable, +0xD0 f32 strength, +0xD4 f32 accumulator, +0xD8 OSPfs, +0x140/+0x16C decoder blocks).
  D_8010FBE0[4] = 4-byte port flags (byte 3 bit 3 = no pak), D_8010F308[4] u8 flags, D_8010FBF0[4] s32 values, D_8010FBC0 OSMesgQueue
  used as a lock (osRecvMesg/osSendMesg around controller polling), D_8010FC00 OSMesgQueue (siMesgQ), D_8010FC40 B14Mgr (demo record/playback).
- B14Trk: bit-packed keyframe channel (bits, base, range, data bit address, cached cubic c0..c3, span/start/cur).  func_0025FD94 builds the
  cubic from 5 bit fields, func_00261438/00260158 seek (bit-address records of 4*bits+6 bits), 0025FFD0 / 0025EF54 read the header
  (two 32-bit floats or two 6-bit scaled floats), 00260BA0/00260F18/002609CC read an n-bit scaled float, 00260744/00260848/0026035C write them.

ADDITIONS (late batch lessons)
- Bit writers: the `hi` half must be its own variable: `lo = val << sh; hi = val >> (32 - sh);` and `hi = mhi = 0;` in the sh==0 arm
  (reusing `val` for the high half costs a register swap).  With that func_00260744 / 00260848 are EXACT; func_0026035C / 00260A7C
  (same tail after a float quantiser) are 3 words off: ROM schedules `andi sh ; move ; (slot) and q,q,mask`, we get `and ; andi ; (slot) move`.
- In-place parameter updates with s8 params: `void f(S *c, s8 x, s8 y, s32 b) { ... x = x - 3; if (x < 0) x = 0; ...}` reproduces
  `addiu v0,t0,-3 ; addu t0,v0,zero ; sll/sra` (the u8/s8 narrowing of the pseudo happens at each use, params are NOT sign-extended on entry).
- `u8 old = e->c; e->c = old - 1; if (e->c < 0) e->c = old + 4;` (not `e->c--; if (<0) e->c += 4`) gives `addiu v0,v1,4` from the OLD value (func_00263E1C).
- Branch-likely + delay slot: `if (e->b < 0x1E) {b = 0; a++; ...} else {a = 1; b = 0;}` (condition written as `<`, arms duplicated) produced the ROM's
  `beql ... ; sb t5` layout, `if (>= ) ... ; b = 0;` did not.
- Box-overlap tests `D[3] > e->minx` (constant on the left) give the ROM load order (const first), `e->minx < D[3]` loads the entity first.
- Declaring the whole 6-float box as `extern f32 D_801031F0[]` and indexing [0..5] reproduces `lui/addiu (D+0xC)` + negative offsets (func_00262EA8).
- Struct return `B14Span f(f32,f32,f32)`: hidden pointer in a0, floats in a1..a3; the compiler copies the local result through the stack (lw t1,t2,t3 / sw).
- Remaining unsolved register-allocation effect (seen in func_00264E10, func_00263E1C, func_002609CC, func_0025F454): the ROM gives the
  low FP registers (f0,f1) to the long-lived float variables and f2/f3 to the loaded constants; our source gives constants the low registers
  (constants are allocated first).  Variable declaration order, temporaries and `const` did not flip it.
- Inlined seek loop (func_00261438 / 00260158 / 0025F0F4 contain the same inlined code, 8 callee-saved registers: frame, t, rec, idx, p, addr, start, end):
  I could not make gcc keep idx and the tbl pointer as two separate IVs; every variant ended up with 7 saved registers (frame 0x130 instead of 0x138).

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b15
FINDINGS - batch 15 (main image 0x2661FC-0x26CE18: 67 functions; sound/effect wrappers, targeting predicates, model/gfx draw code)
Compiler/flags: gcc 2.8.1 -O2 -mcpu=r4300 everywhere (no -fno-builtin needed, see sqrtf note).

STRUCT-BY-VALUE ARGUMENTS (the key to the 0x267xxx..0x268xxx wrapper family)
- `sw $a3,0xC($sp)` (a3 spilled to its home slot) in a function that does not otherwise need a3 = a 12-byte struct passed by value
  that starts in a3: `Vec3f pos` as 4th parameter (x in a3, y/z on the stack).  Empty stubs `jr $ra; sw $a3,0xC($sp)` are
  `void f(s32 a, s32 b, s32 c, Vec3f pos) {}`.  A Vec3f beginning in a1 or a2 spills a1..a3 (or a2,a3) the same way.
  The callee call then reloads a3 from the home slot (`lw a3,home($sp)`) and copies the stack part (lw/sw pairs, LAST arg first).
- Further struct params after the Vec3f are 8-byte structs (2 words) read with plain lw/lh/lb from the stack:
  {s16 a,b; s32 c} (lh 0x42/lw 0x44 style), {s32 idx; s32 y}, {s32 a; f32 b}, {s32 a; s16 b; u8 c}, 4 x s16 (2-byte aligned: gcc then
  copies it halfword-wise, so a struct with only s16 members cannot be forwarded by value like the word-aligned ones), 7 x u8 (lbu 0x40..0x46).
  A scalar stack parameter is loaded once into a pseudo (cached across calls); a member of a struct parameter is a memory read: it is
  RELOADED after calls/joins (`lw v0,0x30($sp)` before every use in func_00267EE8).  To get the reload use a >= 8-byte struct param.
- Narrow members read as `lh 0x42($sp)`/`lbu 0x1B($sp)` (halfword/byte of the arg slot, big endian) are struct members, not s16/u8 params
  (a plain `s16 id` param gives lhu+sll/sra or lw at entry).
- A 16-byte struct (quaternion) passed by value is copied with 4 lw/sw (func_00267968).

CODEGEN IDIOMS
- `x = y = 1;` (chain) vs two statements: `s6 = s3 = 1;` made the second variable a COPY (`addu s6,s3,zero`) instead of a second `li` (func_002676FC).
- `out->x = out->y = out->z = 0.0f;` stores via one FP reg (`mtc1 zero,f1; swc1 f1,8/4/0`) in z,y,x order; separate `= 0.0f` stores use `sw $zero`.
- Dead local aggregates are real: locals assigned but never read (Vec3f scale = {s,s,s}) keep their stores and their stack slots; unused local
  arrays/vectors only enlarge the frame (func_002675E0 needed `Vec3f v; u8 unused[0x64];`, locals are allocated FIRST DECLARED = LOWEST address,
  struct locals >= 8 bytes are 8-aligned).
- A shared `return vol;` tail with a hoisted `mov.s f0,f21` into the `j` delay slot = if/else with ONE final return (not early `return 0.0f`).
  `return vol = 0.0f;` or `vol = 0; return vol;` is folded to a constant return by gcc: use the if/else + common return shape.
- Post-increment RMW of a global (`g = D++;`) gives `la`+lw/sw 0(reg) (explicit address) but `g = D; D++;` gives lui/lw + lui $at/sw.  Plain
  scalar RMW like `D |= 0x20` stays lui/lw/sw unless the address is also needed (a pointer variable `fp = &D; *fp |= ...; f(fp)` gives the ROM's `la a1`).
- sqrtf: gcc inlines `sqrtf` as sqrt.s + c.eq.s + fallback jal; the ROM calls it plainly (jal func_002BC380).  Either declare the callee as
  func_002BC380 (that is how the matches here are written) or compile with -fno-builtin and the name sqrtf (also exact).
- `jr $ra ; sw` stubs with a K&R-declared callee: if the ROM calls a function without setting a0..a3 (func_00296FF8(), func_0026925C(0x1D)) declare
  it `void f();` and call it with exactly the args set up.
- Calls with stale registers (func_00280094 style 12-arg calls etc.) are just long prototypes; Vec3f/Vec4f by value in the middle of them are
  copied from locals `lw/sw` (Vec3f z = {0,0,0}; func(..., z, ...)).

DISPLAY LIST (F3D) EMITTING CODE  (draw-model functions 0x26925C..0x26CE18) - what finally matched
- Each command is `P4Gfx *gN = D_80110634++; gN->words.w0 = ..; gN->words.w1 = ..;` with `extern P4Gfx *D_80110634;` (scalar).
- EVERY command site (including the two arms of `if (flag) {..} else {..}`, every case of a 42-way switch, the lights block, the loop body)
  needs its OWN pointer variable (g0,g1,g2,...).  Re-using one `g` gives lui/lw+lui $at/sw macro forms and different register choice;
  distinct variables give the ROM's cross-jumped arms (`la a0,&D; lw v0,0(a0)` in each arm, shared tail `addu v1,v0,zero; addiu v0,v0,8; sw v0,0(a0)`),
  the copy idiom and the loop copy `addu s6,s0,zero`.
- if/else arms must be written out twice (two `g` blocks) so gcc cross-jumps the common tail.
- The 42-case set-combine switch (func_00268CE0) / 31-case set-othermode switch (func_0026925C) match when written as one block per case with
  distinct variables; case 30 of the combine switch has no `break` (falls into 31, emitted twice - real in the ROM).
- A u16 value tested after merging several arms (`v = pad->buttons & mask; if (v == 0)`) must be declared u16 (u32/s32 add an extra andi 0xFFFF).
- `lui a1,..` 12-word `F3D` constants are loaded at the top of a run of consecutive commands; the number/order of the hoisted constants follows
  the order of the statements, but loads of globals used in a command (`D_800D15D4 | 0x400`) are scheduled where the source puts them relative to the
  stores through the command pointers (stores through pointers are not reordered with global loads).

TEST/TOOLING NOTES (tools added in this workspace, all under tools/)
- tools/asmfn.py now scans every asm/*.s (it only knew entry.s/16E000.s).
- tools/vv.py <variants.txt> <fn> <rom> [flags] [-v]: several full-file variants separated by a line `////`, one fchk call, prints one line per variant.
  Note: vv writes wip/c/vv_<fn>_<k>.c (the prefix must not start with `_v_`, the fchk rsync excludes those).
- tools/side.sh <file.c> <fn> <rom> : side by side ours | rom;  tools/al.sh <file.c> <fn> <rom> [ctx] : aligned diff (- ours, + rom);
  tools/gccs.sh <file.c> : raw gcc -S output (shows `la`/`lw sym` forms before the assembler expands them);  tools/mk.py / tools/res.py : check/record results.

INLINED STATIC WRAPPERS (struct params)
- Double copies of by-value struct arguments (param -> local temp at sp+0x3C/0x48 -> outgoing arg area) with locals that are only 4-byte aligned
  (p at 0x3C below an 8-byte struct at 0x48) are the signature of an INLINED `static inline void w(.., Vec3f pos, Blk blk)` whose parameters become
  local copies.  func_002683F0 = `if (dy < 0 ? -dy <= lim1 : dy <= lim2) w(a,b,c,pos,blk);` with `static inline w()` calling func_00266830(.., 1, 0, 0x200).
  A plain call with the params passed on (func_00267BD8) has no copies; writing explicit local struct copies gives 8-aligned locals and a different frame.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b16
FINDINGS batch 16 (main image 0x6DE7C-0x75144: display-list helpers, vec/matrix math, small tree/list code)
Flags -O2 -mcpu=r4300 everywhere. Tooling I used: wip/ck.py (check list), wip/vv.py (variants file separated by ==== lines,
one fchk call), wip/side.py (side-by-side), wip/fin.py (check + copy to wip/out + results.txt). rabbitizer must be pip-installed for lu_side.py.

1. Matrix / vector idioms
 - Matrix initialisers (identity/translation/scale/rotation) are written with CHAINED assignments: the 0.0f zero lives in an FP register
   ("mtc1 $zero,$f0; swc1 $f0,..." x N) only if the zeros come from `a = b = c = 0.0f` chains; plain `m[0][1] = 0.0f; ...` stores $zero (sw $zero).
   Store order in the ROM is descending (right-most target first): write the chain in ASCENDING address order:
   `m->m[0][1] = m->m[0][2] = ... = m->m[3][2] = 0.0f;` stores 0x38,0x34,... ; the diagonal `m00 = m11 = m22 = m33 = 1.0f` stores 0x3C,0x28,0x14,0x0.
   Each function gets its own 4-byte literal (1.0f) in .rodata (ro=...:4): literal pools are per function, not shared.
 - Parameters declared as `typedef f32 Mtx[4][4]` ("Mtx m" decays to f32 (*)[4]) give the ROM's row-pointer strength reduction for loops over m[i][j];
   a `Mtx4f *`/struct pointer with ->m[i][j] gave different loop code (func_00272898 clamp loop, func_002730B0 copy+transpose).
   For straight-line code the struct form `struct {f32 m[4][4];} *` is fine.
 - Order of the three last items (m[3][2] copy / zero chain / m[3][3]=1.0f) changes the scheduling; permuting the 3 statements found the ROM order (func_00273E10).
 - sqrtf: with -O2 -mcpu=r4300 gcc inlines `sqrtf(x)` as sqrt.s + NaN check + call. The ROM has a plain `jal sqrtf`, so the game never saw a builtin
   sqrtf: declare it under another name (`f32 func_802BC380(f32)`; sqrtf is at 0x802BC380) and it matches. sinf/cosf (no builtin) are fine by name.
 - Vec3f returned by value (`Vec3f f(Vec3f*, Vec3f*)`): hidden a0 pointer, local Vec3f built on the stack, then lw/sw copy to *a0 and `move v0,a0` (func_00272284).
 - A 12-byte local Vec3f filled field-wise and then only read through f32 fields keeps its stores on the stack but loads get forwarded:
   `r.x = m[0][0]; ... sqrt(r.x*r.x...)` reproduces the "swc1 x,0x10($sp)" noise of func_0027335C (no inline helper needed).
 - Comparison written `a > b` instead of `b < a` changes which operand is loaded first (func_00274004), and `if (t < -1.0f || 1.0f < t) return 0.0f;`
   (single || condition) gave the ROM's bc1t/non-likely delay slot where two separate ifs gave bc1tl (func_0027266C).
 - angle wrap helper: `while (*p < -PI) *p += 2PI; while (*p >= PI) *p -= 2PI;` with literal float constants.

2. Control flow / misc
 - Loop `for (i = n - 1; i != -1; i--)` gives `beqz n; addiu v0,n,-1; ... bne v0,-1` (func_002729B4); `while (n--)` and `for (i=0;i<n;i++)` do not.
 - `do { } while (--i >= 0);` with `i = 3` = the `v0=3; addiu v0,-1; bgez` busy loop of func_00273014.
 - Display list pushes: `DlCmd *p = D_80110634++;` declared INSIDE each if/else arm gave the ROM's copy (move) of the loaded pointer (func_0026D914);
   a declaration at function scope or array form did not. Sequences of 5 pushes with one function-scope `g = D_80110634++;` reproduced the register-held &D_80110634 (func_0026E390).
   ROM addresses used as display-list data (gSPLoadUcode) are plain `extern u8 D_E0470[]` cast to u32.
 - Insert into a tree + threaded list: the "empty tree" case must be the `else` of `if (parent != NULL)` (stores to the root/first pointers after the nested ifs).
 - Rotation of two matrix rows (func_00273C08/00273A34): the ROM re-loads the second row element (lwc1 twice) and loads the first only once. Source:
   `x = m[0][k]; m[0][k] = c*x + s*m[1][k]; m[1][k] = -s*x + c*m[1][k];` (statements in this order, x a local): the m[1][k] read after the store to
   m[0][k] is NOT merged by cse here, and the scheduler hoists all loads above the stores. Use separate temps for BOTH and gcc merges the loads (mismatch).
 - `2.0f * x * y` compiles to add.s x,x ; mul.s : the ROM's (x+x)*y in the quaternion->matrix function (func_002742B4) is exactly that source; hand-named temps (x2 = x + x; xy = x2 * y) gave
   the same instruction mix but different register numbers. Quaternion product (func_00274108) matched from the plain component formulas.
 - Inline helpers: the vector TU contains `inline` helpers (cross product writing through a Vec3f* parameter, normalize(Vec3f*), scale returning Vec3f by value).
   func_0027230C (plane-normal projection) is only reproduced with `static inline void vcross(Vec3f*,Vec3f*,Vec3f*)`, `static inline void vnorm(Vec3f*)`,
   `static inline Vec3f vscale(Vec3f*, f32)` called with &local: the inlined pointer parameter shows up as the `addiu s0,sp,0x10` base register used for
   y/z accesses of a local that is also accessed via sp. Tell-tale: an inlined body that mixes `0x10($sp)` and `0x4($s0)` accesses to the same local.
   (A non-static `inline` also emits an out-of-line copy with its own literal pool: ro= doubles; use `static inline`.)
 - Clamp macros (func_00271B18, atan2-like): `func(MAX(-1.0f, MIN(1.0f, x)))` with `#define MIN(a,b) ((a)<(b)?(a):(b))`, `#define MAX(a,b) ((a)>(b)?(a):(b))`
   expanded INSIDE the call argument reproduced the ROM's odd clamp (first test `1.0 < x`, redundant second `1.0 < fa0` test with bc1tl).
   Negated clamp argument: `t = MAX(..); func(-t)` (a `-MAX(...)` expansion pushes the negation into the ternary arms and adds a 6th literal).
   `ABS(x) < ABS(z)` with `#define ABS(a) ((a) < 0.0f ? -(a) : (a))` gives the threaded abs compares. `res = -r; if (z < 0.0f) res = r;` gives bc1f + neg in the delay slot.
   Register trick: reusing ONE variable for the sum of squares and then `len = sqrtf(len)` puts len in $f12 (like the ROM); a separate `len` variable lands in $f2.
 - Struct result via hidden pointer vs explicit out-pointer: both pass the destination in $a0 (shifting float args to $a1..). Tell: with a real struct return the ROM does
   `addu $v0,$s_dst,$zero` before the final word copy; with `void f(T *dst, ...)` it does not, and results are written straight into *dst (func_00270B1C slerp:
   `*dst = *q1; return;` and `dst->x = ...`). A by-value struct return copies a local (`r`) to the hidden slot (extra lw/sw), so mismatching frame sizes
   (0x70 vs 0x60) are the hint to switch to the out-pointer form.
 - Stack-allocated 16 byte `B16Vec4 neg = *q2; neg.x = -neg.x; ...` compiles to lw/sw copy + lwc1/neg.s/swc1 exactly like the ROM; redundant duplicated `if (d1 < d2)` tests
   in the ROM (bc1tl re-test after the sinf calls) are source-level duplicates: write the condition twice.
 - libultra guMtxL2F (func_00270770): fully unrolled in the ROM; `u32 a, b;` reused for all 8 pairs gives the ROM's a3/t0 register choice.
 - Reusing ONE variable for len and then `len = 1.0f / len; v.x *= len; ...` (func_0026F3D8, 3 normalizations) puts the reciprocal in len's register ("div.s f3,f0,f3");
   a separate `inv` variable lands in the next free register (mismatch). Same for the first fallback `else { f.y = 0; f.z = 0; f.x = 1.0f; }` (stores y, z, then x).
 - func_00271CC4 (|angle difference|): macros WRAP(x) {while (x < -PI) x += 2PI; while (x > PI) x -= 2PI;} expanded 3 times (each with its own 4 literals = 96 bytes of ro):
   first copy works on a temp (`t = b`), the two branch copies work in place on b: `if (d > 0.0f) {WRAP(b) d = a - b; WRAP(d) return d;} else {WRAP(b) d = a - b; WRAP(d) return -d;}`.
 - Literals vs externs: float constants that live in the rodata pool of the function (ro=...) must be written as literals (1.0f, 0.5f, 80.0f ...) not `extern f32 D_xxx`;
   with the literal chk reports the ro= piece at exactly the ROM address of the D_ symbol the asm uses (e.g. func_0026D844: 2.0f -> ro=0xCA39C:4, D_800C979C).
 - Variables shared between two branches become callee-saved when one branch has calls (inv in nlerp/slerp branch of func_00270D40 landed in $f20): declare per-branch
   block-local variables (`{ f32 len; f32 inv; ... } else { f32 th, s, inv, w1, w2; ... }`) to get the ROM's caller-saved temporaries.
 - Sparse flag switch (func_0026E5E0, 29 visible cases, binary compare tree, no jump table): gcc balances the tree over ALL case labels, including labels that jump to the
   default label (their compares are omitted). The ROM root pivot 0x8000 (13th visible case) only reproduced after adding `case 0x1: case 0x4: case 0x10: default: break;`
   (the 3 missing power-of-two flags) next to a separate `case 0x20: break;`. Look at the pivot constants of the compare tree: if they are not the median of the visible cases,
   hidden default-labelled cases exist (gcc 2.8 balance_case_nodes: root index = (count+ranges+1)/2).
 - A call `f(obj, id, Vec3f pos, flag)` where pos is a local Vec3f: pos is passed by value in $a2,$a3,0x10($sp) and `flag` goes to 0x14($sp); declare callee K&R `void f();` when
   some call sites omit trailing args. The `beql ... ; sw s0,0x14($sp)` delay-slot copies in the compare tree mean those case bodies also store the flag argument.
 - Struct with u8[4]/s16 members copied by value (8 bytes) uses lwl/lwr unless the type is 4-aligned: `__attribute__((aligned(4)))` gives the ROM's lw/sw.
 - Loop with two induction pointers (flags at ents+0xC and ents): keep a single walking pointer `ents` and increment it at the end of the loop body (`ents++` after the if), loop var
   `for (i = n - 1; i != -1; i--)`; the `s2 = 0` initialiser must be placed right before the `switch` (scheduler hoists it) to get the ROM's delay slot filling.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b17
FINDINGS batch 17 (main image 0x7518C-0x7C390; append-style)
[tools] tools/gas_compat.py patched in this workspace (copy: wip/out/gas_compat.py), 2 changes, both needed to match real FP code:
  1. An FP compare stays "pending" across a label: old gas puts the compare->bc1x `nop` AFTER the label ($L: nop; bc1tl). Seen in
     func_00274810/74878/748E0 (shared `if (x<0) x=0` tail after two c.lt arms: natural nested-if source matches once the tool is fixed).
  2. `mtc1 $int,$fp` counts as a writer of the FP reg for the return-slot rule: `mtc1; cvt.s.w; jr ra; nop` keeps the nop
     (func_00278900), the assembler does not fill the delay slot with the cvt.
[builtin] `f32 sqrtf(f32)` declared in game code is expanded inline by gcc -mcpu=r4300 (sqrt.s + NaN check + call). The ROM calls the libm
  function (jal 0x802BC380): declare it under another name (`f32 func_002BC380(f32);`) -> real jal. (func_00274C64, 00274CEC)
[idiom] normalise vector: reuse the length variable for the scale: `len2 = K / sqrt(len2); z = v.z*len2; x = ..; y = ..` (ROM scale lives in
  the reg of the call argument), `z = 0; if (len2 != 0) {...} else {x=0; y=0} dir.z = z;` matches the shared z store.
[idiom] adjacent byte fields: `r->b11 == 10 && r->b12 == 1` is merged by gcc fold into ONE word compare (lw + and + compare) when both fields sit in
  the same struct. The ROM has two lbu: write nested ifs (`if (a == 10) { if (b == 1) ... }`) (func_00279490).
[idiom] null-safe field read `r = f(); return r ? r->b : 0;` -> ROM wants `if (r == 0) return 0; return r->b;` (beql/move in slot) (func_002760C4).
[idiom] clamp macro MX(MN(x,hi),lo) with MN(a,b)=((a)<(b)?(a):(b)), MX(a,b)=((a)>(b)?(a):(b)), x a u8: gives the ROM's odd
  `sltiu v1,x,hi+? ; beqz ; andi ; slti ; bnez; beqz(second test of v1)` decision tree (func_00278A78) - plain if-chains do not.
[idiom] struct copy of a 0x14 byte record then patching two bytes of the copy (`tmp = *r; tmp.b12 = 2; tmp.b10 = 0; f(&tmp)`) -> lw x4 / sw x4 /lw/sw
  (struct must have a word member else gcc copies byte-wise) (func_00278F70).
[idiom] stores `a->x0 = a->x1 = v`: x1 is stored FIRST (assignment chain right-to-left) (func_00276544).
[idiom] float values accumulating `D[idx % 90]` etc: `n = frame + (...)*16; return tbl[n % 90]` keeps the sum in the register that later holds the remainder
  (func_00278900); the two-statement form `n = ...; n %= 90;` equals it.
[globals] `extern SessMgr D_8011FE88;` (struct) used as &D_8011FE88 several times plus field reads: struct-by-value access gives lui/lw each time,
  array form `extern SessMgr D_8011FE88[]; D_8011FE88->count` keeps the base in an s-reg across the loop/calls (func_00278B2C).
[idiom] pointer-walking loops over arrays of records (`pts++` in a for-header) make gcc keep two pointers; the ROM's single walking pointer came from
  `for (i = 0; i < n; i++) { p = pts + i; ... }` (func_0027647C bounding box min/max; matches p3's `p = pt + i*3` finding).
[idiom] param vs walking pointer: when the ROM copies an incoming pointer arg into an s-reg before the loop (prologue order `move s7,a1; move s6,a2; move s5,a3; move s2,a0`)
  declare the parameter as `base` and a local `r = base` (func_00279274); with the parameter itself as walker the prologue copies come in a0..a3 order.
[idiom] one counter reused for two loops: `total = 0; ...; r = rand() % total; total = 0; while (...) { total += w; if (total >= r) break; }`
  (weighted pick, func_00279808/00279918): a second `sum` variable changes the register allocation.
[idiom] unsigned loop count `u32 n; while (n--)` / `for (i<n)` with u32 n gives `beqz n` + count-down loop with `bne t0,-1` (func_00279620).
[idiom] list unlink/append inlined as macros; with a local `DLList *u = &p->usedl` the second list is addressed via a pointer reg (func_002796AC/00279764 EXACT only with the local pointer).
[call] unprototyped callee `s32 f();` (K&R) lets leftover a0/a1 flow through without being set (func_00279808 calls the rng this way); 
  mixed proto: `ActorX *func_002833A4();` called once with and once without an arg in func_00279A70.
[inline] func_00275D04 / 00275E44 contain the body of func_00275C38 (cached triangle normal) inlined: write a `static inline` helper with the same body in the
  same file (gcc 2.8 -O2 honours `inline`); the out-of-line copy still exists in the ROM as its own function (func_00275C38). The inline copy
  stored the cache pointer BEFORE the struct copy in 00275D04, after it in 00275E44/00275C38 (scheduler order only; try both).
[idiom] `if (c) { r = A; } else { r = B; } return r;` (single result variable) reproduced the ROM block order (A first, `bc1t` over it) where two `return`s
  put the else block first (func_00275E44).
[idiom] `for (i = 0; i < m; i++)` right after `m = n - 1; ...stores...`: loop.c only knows i==0 (-> `blez m` test) when `i = 0` is in the for-header and
  no branch/label is between; do not hoist `i = 0` above the enclosing `if` (func_002759C4).
[tools/ASSEMBLER] The ROM assembler NEVER fills a `jr $ra` delay slot itself (gcc's own reorg fills it when it can, otherwise the ROM has `jr ra; nop`).
  Seen in func_00276BB4 (`sb;sb;sb;jr ra;nop`; gcc leaves `j $31` unfilled there, modern-gas emulation of tools/gas_compat.py fills it with the last sb -> 2 words
  differ). The ROM has ~190 `store; jr ra; nop` sites. Fix: tools/gas_compat.py patched in my workspace (wip/out/gas_compat.py): env GAS_NOFILL=1 keeps the nop
  for returns in lu/lu3 mode; all my 89 other EXACT functions are identical under both settings, so it can be the default for game code (my fchk.sh exports
  GAS_NOFILL=1). tools/lu_match.py also got `GAS_MODE` env (not needed). Real build path (cc.sh game -> lu3) should apply the same no-fill rule.
[LOOP SHAPE - big lesson] Loops over vertex/record arrays: the ROM keeps ONE walking base `b+8` (offsets 6/0xC/0xD/0xE added to it) and a separate counter.
  Pointer-walk source (`for (p = b->v; ...; p++)`) makes gcc 2.8 combine the address givs into a shifted base `p+0xE` (offsets -8,-2,-1,0): never matches.
  What matches: hoist `vp = b->v;` (loop invariant pointer), keep counter `i` and use `p = vp + i;` (or `vp[i].field`) inside the loop
  (func_00278A78, func_002782E4 base, func_0027647C). The remaining detail for macros: apply them to the memory operand `p->col[0]`
  (re-evaluated in the macro) not to a cached local (ROM shows one load because gcc CSEs them; a local gives store-per-arm code).
[inline] func_00275480: a `static inline Vec3f *tri_unit(Vec3f *out, Tri3 *t)` helper did NOT match when called as tri_unit(&n, t) (gcc keeps `out`=&n in an s-reg across the calls).
  A `static inline void tri_unit_upd(Tri3 *t)` that only updates the cache globals (+ `D_800D263C = t` in the caller), then `n = D_80115E20;` in the caller,
  matched exactly. Same shape for the nested tri_normal_upd (stores D_800D2638 = t at its end, copy D_80115E20 = D_80115E10 in the unit helper).

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b18
FINDINGS batch 18 (append-style)
- tools/asmfn.py only read asm/entry.s + asm/16E000.s; now globs asm/*.s (this batch lives in asm/2BD34.s). tools/asma.py prints asm with VRAM addresses (needed to map jump-table targets). tools/jt.py <va> <n> dumps jump table words (ROM = VA-0x80000000+0xC00).
- wip/side.ps1 <file> <fn> <rom> (side-by-side with -O2 -mcpu=r4300), wip/vv.py (variant search without full sync; placeholders @@x@@), wip/gs.sh (raw gcc asm; run via wsl with MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1).
- Block-scope locals per loop: `B18Ent *e = tbl[i];` declared INSIDE each loop body (separate pseudos) lets e live in a call-clobbered reg ($a1); one function-wide `e` reused by 3 loops is live across calls and wastes a callee-saved reg (func_00281A70 needed this to match).
- `extern const f32 K;` makes gcc hoist the lwc1 of K out of a loop containing calls into a callee-saved fp reg (ROM idiom: lwc1 $fs0 in loop preheader). Non-const extern is reloaded each iteration.
- Local copy of a struct count (`cnt = D->count; for (i<cnt)`) reproduces the once-loaded count in a callee-saved reg when the loop contains calls.
- Jump-table switch after `lbu; addiu 8; sll 24; sra 24; sltiu 0xB` = `switch ((s8)type)` with negative case labels; a shared block (`case -5: return D;`) placed right after the preceding case (fallthrough from its else branch) gives the ROM block order (func_0027D950).
- Local array placement: `u8 buf10[0x40]; u8 buf50[0x10];` (bigger first) gave buf50 at sp+0x50, buf10 at sp+0x10. A local address needed across calls (`p = buf50` assigned in each arm, used after a call) is kept in $s0 only if you route it through a pointer local.
- Pointer + offset expression: parenthesise the whole offset (`(u8*)o + ((idx<<6)+0x60)`) to get the ROM's addiu-then-addu order; `(idx*0x18+0xE0)` likewise. A local `zero = 0` used in `zero % *p` stays in a callee-saved register (mips div has no immediate), matching the ROM's early 'move s5,zero'.
- u16 table entries compared against 0xFFFF: keep the values in s32 locals (lhu zero-extends, compare directly); u16 locals add andi 0xFFFF. Struct-by-value args (Vec3f/8-byte/16-byte structs, passed from globals) reproduce the lw t0-t3 / sw to outgoing-arg area idioms with plain prototypes taking the struct types. Fields of tables via c->tab->a70[idx] (c cached in a local) reload tab each time like the ROM.
- Sparse switch (10 case values, no jump table) compiles to the same balanced compare tree as the ROM when written as a plain switch with all labels stacked on one body. Branch-likely delay-slot layout: for 'if (p) {x=p+K} else {call...}' the ROM has the nonzero test first (beql/bnel + j join): write the positive condition first (func_0027DAA4: 'if (ow->unk5DC != 0) mm = ow->unk5DC+0x160; else {call}'), likewise 'if (flags & M) {...} else mm=p+0x74'.
- Block-scoped duplicates again (func_0027D47C): when the same table-fetch code (idx/s2/s3/v1 locals) appears in two separate blocks, declaring the locals INSIDE each block (`{ s32 idx, s2, s3, v1; ... }`) instead of function-wide changed the callee-saved assignment (c=s0 vs s2) and turned 39 differing words into 0. Always try per-block locals when two blocks reuse the same temp names and the ROM shows different regs for them.
- f32 literal passed as an argument in an int register: declare the callee parameter as f32 and pass `1.024f` (gcc emits lui/ori itself, scheduled with the jal delay slot as in the ROM); passing the same bits as an s32 (0x3F83126F) gives a different slot order (a1 move before the lui/ori).
- Separate sibling globals that the ROM addresses via different lui/addiu pairs (D_801041F8 pos vector, D_801042C8 vector) must be declared as SEPARATE externs, whereas a symbol and its +0xE8 member (D_801041F8 / D_801042E0 reached as -0xE8($s4)) must be ONE struct (`B18GPos { Vec3f pos; u8 pad[0xDC]; Vec3f vF0; }`): decide by whether the ROM shares the base register between the two uses (cse shares bases only within one symbol).
- Literal pools: float constants used in a function appear in .rodata in order of use (D_800C9E00..9E24 = 10.24f, 102400.0f, 0.785398f, 0.30517578f, 200.0f, 255.0f, 7.68f, 8.0f, 0.1f, 2147483648.0f(u32 cast)); write them as float literals in C (not externs): chk then reports ro=. `(u32)alpha & 0xFF` produces the 2^31 compare + sub + or sequence from the pool.
- Clamp macros: MAXC(MINB(255.0f, a), 0.0f) with MINB(x,y)=((x)<(y)?(x):(y)), MAXC(x,y)=((x)<(y)?(y):(x)) reproduced a double-evaluated clamp exactly (func_0027E758 alpha); macro-style double evaluation is the original idiom (ROM recomputes `s-t` in the else arm).
- Vec3f struct pairs: `struct { Vec3f min, max; } bb;` is one 24-byte local; two separate Vec3f locals are padded to 16 bytes each (frame 8 bytes bigger).
- Per-iteration reset in a loop (`vis = 0;` as first statement of the body rather than in the for-increment) reproduces the `addu s0,zero,zero` before the loop test and in the loop-back delay slot.
