- Block-scoped `DlCmd *dl = D_80110634++;` inside a macro matched the ROM's interleaving of D_80110634 stores with the data stores (function-wide `dl` did not).
- LOOP INVARIANT HOISTING (loop.c) of constants: gcc -dL dumps (`gcc ... -dL`, wip/dl.sh) show per movable `regno N (life L), savings S moved|not desirable`; a constant is hoisted into a callee-saved reg only if savings*life >= threshold, i.e. when its uses (all merged into ONE pseudo by cse) span a long distance. If the ROM hoists a constant (lui/ori or addiu into an s-reg before the loop, used in several far-apart branches in the loop) but your build re-materialises it at each use, give it ONE local: `s32 one = 1;` (placed immediately before the loop, e.g. inside `if (n > 0) {`) and use `one` at every site (func_00281C74: 1 -> fp, matched the loop; func_00282304: `u32 m10000 = 0x10000;`). Put the assignment AFTER the n>0 test (else the lui lands before the branch).
- Same idea explains 'n2'/consts reg order: ROM hoists in order of first appearance in the loop body.
- A pointer/handle cached in a local (`B18Hnd *h = o->handle; h->a=..; h->b=..`) avoids the reload gcc does after each short store through the pointer (aliasing); the ROM shows no reload -> original code used a local.
- Membership tests like `type == 0x57 || type == 0xC || type == 0x22` followed by a `switch` with the same case values: write BOTH as switch statements (`switch(type){case 0x57: case 0xC: case 0x22: list[n++]=o; break;}`); the ROM's `beq 0x57; slti 0x58; beql; 0xC; 0x22` compare tree comes from the switch, not from an || chain.
- `for (i=0;..)` loops over a list built in an earlier loop: reuse the SAME function-level counter `i` for consecutive loops but give each loop its own block-scoped element pointer `B18Ob *o` (ROM: counter in s3 for both loops, element in call-clobbered reg in the simple first loop, callee-saved in the later loop with calls).
- Struct-by-value Vec3f args from a global are loaded through a t3 base via lui/addiu each time (separate statement per argument); passing `o->pos` (field) gives lw t0..t2 from the object base.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b19
FINDINGS batch 19 (game code ROM 0x83618-0x8B...; written incrementally)
1. gas_compat fixes needed (patched in this workspace's tools/gas_compat.py, please merge):
   a) return slot after a block-move / struct copy (lw,lw,lw,sw,sw,sw): the original assembler did NOT move the last sw into the jr slot
      (ROM: `sw ; jr ra ; nop`); chk flag -DNOFILL (tools/lu_match.py sets gas_compat.NOFILL) reproduces it; used for func_00283BA0.
      Also seen in ROM at func_002390C0, func_0023E8A4, func_00260EEC (not mine).
   b) a macro-expanded big-offset store/load (`sw $t0, 0x1B660($a0)` -> lui/addu/sw via $at) as the last insn before `jr ra` is never
      moved into the slot by the original assembler: ROM `... sw ; jr ; nop`.  Fixed by recording the expansion in `ih` (dep rule) for
      bigoff_re and symidx_re (func_00286920).
   c) FP compare followed by a LABEL and then bc1t/bc1f: the old assembler still inserted the nop between the c.xx and the branch
      (ROM: `c.lt.s ; L: nop ; bc1f`).  gas_compat reset `prev` at labels; now kept for 'c.*' (func_00283A54).
2. sqrtf calls: functions calling sqrtf need -fno-builtin (else gcc inlines sqrt.s with a NaN check). Used in func_00283A54, 002849C4, 002859A0.
   d) gas_compat bigoff_re also has to match gcc's `l.s/s.s/l.d/s.d` mnemonics (float fields at offsets > 0x7FFF, func_00286050).
3. Inline functions: GCC 2.8 emits a non-static `inline` function out of line as well, AFTER the first caller (deferred); several ROM groups are
   "inline helper + callers that inlined it": func_00284544 (callers func_002830A0, func_0028324C inline it, while func_00282E6C/0028444C/00284640
   only call it => those probably use the inline function before its definition or it is a macro), func_002859A0 (inlined into func_002856B0),
   func_00285880 (inlined into func_00285B34). In those groups write `inline T fn(...) {...}` BEFORE the user in the same .c; check ALL functions with
   one chk list (same path, different function names).  Compare with a by-value struct param: when inlined the param is copied to a new stack local.
4. Float literals vs extern floats: a float referenced as a ROM literal pool entry (1.0f etc.) is hoisted out of loops (preheader) only when
   it is a real literal or `extern const f32`; plain `extern f32` is re-loaded inside the loop.  func_002856B0 needed LITERALS 1.0f
   (chk then prints ro=0xCABB0:20 for the 5 pool entries); func_00284D98/00284E70/00284AF4: `extern const f32 D_xxx` hoisted into $fs0.
5. Struct param by value in a1..a3 (Vec3 after an int arg) shows as sw a1..a3 to home slots + lwc1 from them (func_002859A0, func_002856B0):
   declare `B19Vec3 p` by value, NOT three f32 params (those get mtc1 a1,...).
6. Loops/CSE tips seen:
   - `owner->slot[i].tail` with big struct offset: write `(B19List *)((u8 *)owner + i * 0x14 + 0xFC28)` so strength reduction keeps `owner` as base and the
     constant 0xFC28 in a register (func_00284640, func_0028444C).
   - local pointer `row = D_800D052C[i]` made gcc strength-reduce differently; using D_800D052C[i]->a[j] directly matched (func_00282C60 family).
   - `(owner->slot + idx)->head` instead of `owner->slot[idx].head` flips the addu operand order (func_0028469C).
   - `if (!(p->flags&1))...` merged mask tests: nested ifs `if (f & 0x100) { if (!(f & 0x800)) {` keep two andi (func_00284544).
   - `u16 x = tbl[k]` kept in an `s32` local avoids the andi 0xFFFF after lhu (func_00283D24).
   - Return slot `jr ra; nop` after a block-move / bigoff store: see 1a/1b.
   - while(o){next=o->next;...;o=next;} gave the ROM's delay-slot choices where for(;;o=next) did not (func_002830A0).
   - an unused local u8 pad[16] gives `addiu sp,-0x10` frames on tiny leaf functions (func_0028403C, func_00284054).
7. decomp-permuter WORKS for game code (huge win): wip/perm_setup.sh <fn> <wip/c/x.c> sets up ~/perm/b19_<fn> (base.c = cpp -P of the file stripped to the
   function by tools/perm_strip.py, target.s = the glabel..endlabel of the function from asm/*.s with macro.inc header, compile.sh = tools/permcc.sh with
   PERM_FLAGS "-O2 -mcpu=r4300", gas_compat 'lu' mode), wip/perm_run.sh runs `permuter.py -j 14 --stop-on-zero` with a timeout.
   Register-allocation-only mismatches that I could not fix by hand in 8+ variants were solved in 1-4 minutes each, e.g.
   func_00285D80 (temp `tmp = (B19Obj*)(base+idx*0x2E8)` used for ->info), func_00286254 (copy `o2 = o` used for calls and flag tests).
   Typical fixes the permuter finds: extra temp copies of a pointer/value, reordered declarations, `x = (tmp = expr)->f` forms.
   Do this EARLY for any function with a pure reg-allocation/scheduling diff (score < ~100) instead of hand variants.
8. struct-by-value / local copy tell: ROM `mtc1 a1,f5 ; mov.s f4,f5` in a delay slot = inlined function parameter copy (or `t = x` copy that is modified later).
- gcc builtin memcpy changes argument register order; game code is -fno-builtin (func_00285150, 00283A54...).
9. Display-list pointer pushes: declare the DL pointer as a SCALAR `extern B19Dl *D_80110634;` and write `d = D_80110634++; d->w0=..; d->w1=..;`
   repeatedly: gcc then loads D once and forwards the stored value to the next pushes (like the ROM: one lw, then v1+8, v1+0x10 ...).
   Declaring it as an array (`D_80110634[0]`) makes every push reload it (in-struct/array MEM aliasing in cse).  (func_00286424 base)
10. Same-value scalar floats in the literal-pool region (e.g. D_800C9F5C == D_800C9F64 == 1/2032) are separate extern floats, not literals
    (gcc would merge equal literals); isolated 1.0f entries used once or twice per function may be real literals (func_002856B0).
11. Totals: 88 targets: 71 EXACT (incl. the inline-helper groups), 7 PARTIAL (drafts in wip/hard), 4 stray-padding fragments (not functions), 6 big
    functions not attempted (func_00286A78 885 insns, 0028795C, 00287EE8, 00288908, 00289054, 0028994C) -> NONE.
12. alndiff helper (wip/alndiff.py <file.c> <fn> [flags]) aligns our words against the ROM with difflib: shows which regions are structurally equal
    (use it to judge whether a permuter run is promising; bases with < ~70% equal words did not converge in 10-20 min).

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b19a
FINDINGS b19a
- Delay-slot-copy tell: `blezl vX, L ; <insn>` where <insn> is the first insn of the loop top (ROM jumps past it) = do { x += n; } while (cond1 && cmp(x) <= 0) loop (the increment sits at the loop top; the branch-likely copies it into the slot). Plain while loops give a different layout. Same for `bgezl ... subu` = do { j -= size; } while (...).
- Outer driver loop with a stack index (stk[sp] = ..., sp++): write it as goto (label top) not for(;;)/continue: otherwise loop strength reduction turns stk[sp] into walking pointers (extra regs, spills).
- `j - (lo + 1)` on u8* compiled to (j-1)-lo; a separate temp `lo1 = lo + 1;` assigned right after the preceding statement forced addiu+subu as in the ROM.
- Tooling: wip/s.ps1 <file> <fn> <rom> [flags] (side-by-side, ours | ROM, works from windows python+WSL) and wip/run.py <fn> <rom> "<flags>" files... (one chk call for many variant files).
- Display list push with ROM idiom (lui/addiu &D_80110634 in a reg, ONE lw for several consecutive pushes, ptr stored back after each, copy only on the first): declare `extern B19Dl *D_80110634;` (scalar, NOT array: array/struct accesses alias the DL stores and force reloads) and write each push as a block `{ B19Dl **pp = &D_80110634; B19Dl *q = (*pp)++; q->w0 = ..; q->w1 = ..; }` with a fresh block-local q per push (a shared `d` var adds `addu` copies).
- A compare tree `beq t,1,B; slti t,2; beqz ->B; bnez t ->B` with everything going to ONE shared block B cannot be had from switch (gcc merges the identical default/case arms); write it with gotos: `if (t==1) goto bb; if (t>1) goto bb; if (t!=0) goto bb; A; goto jn; bb: B; jn:` (t as s32 local).
- Switch code layout follows source case order of the bodies (cases 0,3,1 in source gave body order 0,3,1 while the compare tree is the same).
- Loop over an array of structs where ROM has ONE giv reg (cells+8, += 0x14), plus separate counter i with `slt i,n` at bottom and `blez n` guard: `cb = cells->c; if (n > 0) { do { c = &cb[i]; ...; i++; } while (i < n); }`. Walking `c++` or `c = &cells->c[i]` made extra givs (&c->n biv, spilled regs) because the base was recomputed.
- Initialisations that the ROM schedules AFTER the early-return branch must be assigned after the early return (not in declarations), and their exact position relative to the following calls (before/between/after two jals) changes the scheduled order: brute-force the 3^k placements (81 files in one chk call took seconds).
- `p->x >= c->x0` (rather than c->x0 <= p->x) loads p->x first; `c.le.s f0,f1` result identical.
- addu operand order (`addu s0,v0,fp` vs `fp,v0`) for `tri + idx` on pointers: use `(B19Tri *)(idx * 32 + (u32)tri)` (or tri as s32 and idx*32 + tri).
- REGISTER ALLOCATION RECIPE (worked for func_00288440, 63 -> 0 differing words): (1) dump allocation: wip/dump.sh <file> -dg -dl; in <dmp>/in.c.greg "N regs to allocate: ..." is the priority order (priority = floor_log2(refs)*refs/live_length), "Register dispositions" gives pseudo->hard reg (16..23=s0..s7, 30=fp). Callee-saved regs are handed out in ascending order by priority; pseudos that do not conflict share a reg, so the ROM's register PAIRS (e.g. s0 = {B, cell}, s1 = {D, i}) tell you the priority order of the long-lived pseudos. (2) Spill slots (stack, highest address first) follow DECLARATION order of the locals reversed: ROM order high->low cam,E,count,i0,cols = declare cols first ... cam last (locals declared at the top without initialisers; assignments later). (3) A variable the original reused for two purposes (camera cell i,j and loop i,j) must be split into separate locals: merged live ranges gave i/j the wrong priority.
- `x = 0` placed textually AFTER the call statement but emitted BEFORE the jal as `addu s2,a1,zero` (copy of the arg zero): put j0 = 0 after `r = f(a, 0);`. Likewise `fp = rows-1` after the next-but-one call statement fixed the `addiu a1,4` vs `addiu fp` scheduling order. When the ROM shows a reg-copy FROM an argument register, move the initialiser textually after the call that sets that arg.
- `while (i < n && B[i] < K) i++;` (&& with the count test first) yields blez-guard + test-at-top loop with the other test at the bottom (loop inversion only duplicates the first condition).
- Count-down clear loops in the ROM (`li v1,15 ... addiu v0,v0,-4 ; bgez`) come from ASCENDING source loops `for (i = 0; i < 16; i++) a[i] = 0;` (gcc reverses them and strength-reduces the index to a decrementing giv); writing the loop descending gave `sll` indexing and no giv.
- Each loop of a long function should have its OWN counter/pointer locals (declared separately): in func_00286A78 sharing `i` between loops changed which loops get strength-reduced and moved the whole register assignment (w got s4 instead of s3). Reg priority = floor_log2(refs)*refs/live_len; params that are copied (w, lvl, flag) compete with 15-ref temporaries (tmp) and a +11 weighted-ref nudge flips them. wip/prio.py + wip/dump.sh print the table.
- 24-byte struct copy (`dst->box = *(Box6 *)p`) reproduces lw x4/sw x4 + lw x2/sw x2 sequences that a field-by-field copy does not.
- A global read once in a loop but with `lui fp,%hi(sym); addiu fp,..; lw 0x98(fp)` hoisted: declare `extern Struct sym[]` and use `sym->field` (array-of-struct, deref), not an s32 array with a constant index.
- Pointers to arrays of 12-byte entries where the ROM shows ONE walking reg initialised by `addu s4,v0,zero` (v0 = base computed before the blez guard) = `pe = ow->ent;` as a pseudo and `pe[i].field` (or `e = pe + i;` at the top of the body); never `e = &ow->ent[i]` (const-folded base) and never e++ plus field accesses (that creates an extra +0xA giv).
- When the body of an inner loop uses `pe[i].x` as an expression, loop.c hoists invariant copies (extra pseudos 223/307 in func_00289054); assigning `e = pe + i;` once at the top of the outer body avoids those copies.
- Switch on u16 id with 4 consecutive cases + preceding hoisted constants (0x653..0x656 in s4..s7): gcc hoists them into regs when 5+ constants appear in one compare tree inside a loop.
- Unresolved (func_00289054): ROM allocates s1=ow, s2=k, s3=v although v has more weighted refs (65 vs ~60: floor_log2 jumps at 64); both our weights and ROM-asm weights agree, so something else (live length? a hidden second pseudo?) differs. Same family of problem in func_00286A78 (w vs tmp).
- Tools added in wip/: s.ps1 (side by side), sdiff.py (opcode-only structural diff), rdiff.py/regmap.py (register renaming votes ours->ROM), prio.py/dump.sh/pseudo.sh/pdef.py (gcc -dg/-dl allocation tables), depth2.py (loop-depth weighted register mention counts for ours vs ROM), run.py/best.py (many variants in one chk call), cc1.sh (compile snippet and show asm; copy the .c to /tmp first, cpp fails on /mnt/d).
- LOOP NOTES MATTER FOR REGISTER PRIORITY: REG_N_REFS is weighted by loop depth taken from NOTE_INSN_LOOP_BEG/END. A loop written with goto/labels has no notes (no strength reduction, no invariant hoisting, depth stays 1), a for/while/do loop has. Switching one loop between the two styles changes the weighted ref counts of every long-lived pseudo (e.g. ow/v in func_00289054 went 60/65 -> 43/43 with a goto entity loop) and therefore the s-register order. Use wip/depth2.py (loop-depth weighted register mention counts, ours vs ROM) to compare against the ROM before guessing. Try all 2^k note/no-note combinations of the nested loops as a first experiment (wip/gen9054.py does this for the three nested loops of func_00289054).
- Struct copy of the camera box, per-loop distinct counters, ascending clear loops, array-of-struct globals: see above; with them func_00286A78 reaches the ROM instruction count (885) and a matching skeleton, leaving only the s-register permutation (w/tmp/a/k/const1 ordering).

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b19b
FINDINGS batch 19b (hard leftovers) -- append-style
1. m2c ADDS bogus third arguments: when a callee leaves a2 untouched, m2c shows `f(a,b,x)` because a2 happened to be live
   (func_0022A1D0 and func_00262EA8 take 2 args only).  Check the callee's asm for the arg regs it reads.  Those bogus args
   also changed reload register choice: gcc picks the reload (spill) register AFTER registers "explicitly used in the rtl"
   (a0..a3 arg moves), so a stray arg shifts every `lw $7`/`lw $6` reload temp.
2. Spilled pseudos reloaded as `lw $6,0x14($sp)` everywhere: reload picks the first reg not mentioned in the function's RTL.
3. Hard-reg allocation: pseudos are allocated in order of priority = floor_log2(n_refs)*n_refs/live_length (dump with -dg: the
   ";; N regs to allocate: ..." list is the allocation order, .lreg has "Register N used X times across Y insns").  A hoisted
   constant (e.g. 0xE compared 4 times in a loop) is moved out by loop.c and then competes for a callee-saved reg.
   Use wip/lr.sh / wip/prio.py style tooling: compute priorities, find which pseudo loses, and change the source so the
   number of live pseudos matches (e.g. one section counter var instead of constants; separate temp var PER SITE: a single
   `t` reused at 8 sites became one global-alloc pseudo in $4, separate t0..t7 are local-alloc'd to $3/$4 like the ROM).
4. Struct field vs scalar matters for sched1: `D_801462D0.mask` (struct member => MEM_IN_STRUCT_P) keeps its order against
   stores through `w->n2`, a `*(s32*)u8array` access does not (the scheduler moved the load below the stores).
   For globals the ROM reaches with negative offsets from a symbol (`lw -0x1228($s0)`) declare a small struct for the fields
   at/after the symbol and use `(u8*)&sym - 0x1228` for the reach-back accesses.
5. Store->load not forwarded in the ROM (`sw v0,0x100(s0)` then `lw v0,0x100(s0)`): the field is accessed through a
   volatile lvalue at those sites: `(*(volatile s32 *)&o->flags) |= 0x80000000;` and `s32 u = *(volatile s32*)&o->flags;`.
   A volatile access disables cse forwarding AND the unfolded `(x & 0x7FFFFFFF) & ~0x200` (two ands) appears.  A volatile
   store may not be put in a branch delay slot, the plain store may: keep the volatile only on the reads.
6. Loop with pointer `o++` plus index `i++`: gcc picked o+0x100 as the giv base (negative offsets -232(reg)).  Writing the
   body as `o = &objs[i];` (array indexing, i is the only biv) gives the ROM's `lw 0x100(s0)` and `addiu s0,s0,0x2E8`.
   Same trick for all array-walk loops with strided structs: `&items[k]`, `&src[k]` (struct typed by stride), not byte math.
7. Two identical tails `if (cmp) s2 = 1;` reached from different float compares are cross-jumped between the c.lt.s and the
   `nop; bc1f` (FP cc is a hard reg): write plain `if (a < b) s2 = 1;` in both places, not `cond = a<b; goto join`.
8. Float box tests: `lwc1` order follows the C operand order: the ROM's `lwc1 f1,0(s7); lwc1 f0,0xE8(s0); c.lt.s f0,f1`
   is `box[0] > o->fE8`.
9. Hill-climbing declaration order of locals (wip/hc2.py) improves register assignment a bit but cannot fix a pseudo that
   loses the priority race; use priorities (3.) to reason instead.
10. Tooling used: wip/fv.py (variant runner via fchk), wip/ev.py+evw.sh (parallel WSL evaluator, normalized mismatch count),
   wip/sd.sh (side-by-side, --norm hides register-only differences), wip/dump_in.sh/lr.sh/prio.py (rtl/alloc dumps).
11. When the only difference is which of two hoisted/long-lived pseudos gets s5 vs s6 (a priority tie such as 0.0864 vs 0.0862
   from floor_log2(n)*n/live_length) and no source shape changes the RTL lengths, `register T *a asm("$22") = arg;` pins the
   register and gives an EXACT match (func_00288E78).  Flagged as HACK in results.txt.
12. m2c passes hard-reg leftovers as arguments (func_0026D980(&D, ...) is really func_0026D980(void)); check the callee.
13. Switch case ORDER changes the loop RTL length and therefore priorities (cases swapped: arg/const pseudo order flips) but
   also changes the branch layout; use pv.py (lengths+priorities per variant, 13 s for 3888 variants) to filter variants before chk.
14. Local register pins `register T v asm("$N")` (gcc 2.8.1, GPR $16-$23,$30 and FP $f4.. both work) are a usable last resort for
   allocation-only mismatches.  Cautions: (a) a pinned loop counter/pointer is no longer strength-reduced (the ROM's `addiu s1,s1,0xE8`
   walk turned into a multiply chain) -> pin only non-induction variables; (b) pinning a variable that is a copy of a call result
   changes cse (`lw s0,0(s3)` became `lw s0,0(v0)`); (c) two pins to the same reg with overlapping lifetimes silently break code;
   (d) FP: to get ROM's `mtc1 a1,f5 ; mov.s f4,f5` (param pseudo and a separate copy used afterwards) use
   `register f32 xx asm("$f5"); register f32 t asm("$f4"); xx = x; __asm__("" : "+f"(xx)); t = xx;` (the empty asm stops cse
   from propagating x into later uses).
15. wip/hc3.py = hill climber over pins (one var at a time) with the difflib mismatch objective; wip/hc2.py = declaration-order
   climber; wip/ev.py/evw.sh evaluate 250 variants in ~10 s (16 cores) via WSL; EVFLAGS env selects flags.  Raw difflib mismatch
   (ours vs ROM, relocs masked) and the register-normalized count are better guides than chk's "words differ" for 300+ insn functions.
16. A hoisted-compare quirk: `for (j = 0; lastA >= j; j++)` nested in an outer for gives in the ROM an UNFOLDED `slt v0,lastA,j`
   (j = 0 in a reg) while ours folds to `bltz lastA`; micro tests show gcc hoists `(set tmp (lt lastA 0))` out of the outer loop
   when lastA is loop-invariant (then the slt+bnez stay split).  The ROM keeps it inside the loop, so lastA is NOT invariant there:
   probably re-read from a struct/stack each time.  Unresolved (func_00288908).
17. Array-of-struct indexing vs pointer walking decides which loop variables become givs: `ent = &ents[k]` with k the only counter
   gives the ROM's single pointer increment; `ent++` plus k++ gave an extra `ent+48` giv and a different register set.
18. Level-builder pattern: two spilled counters/sizes allocated as stack slots in pseudo order; declaring locals in the order of
   their ROM stack slots (ascending address) reproduced the spill slot layout (func_00287EE8 lim/mask/sflag at 0x10/0x14/0x18,
   func_00288908 tmp,cntA,ci,lastA,lastC,e4,cnt,pbp,start,row at 0x3768..0x378C).

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b20
FINDINGS - batch b20 (game code 0x8AE58-0x91DE8, -O2 -mcpu=r4300, main image)

TOOLING / CHK
- tools/gas_compat.py (lu mode) lacked two old-gas behaviours; patched in my workspace (lead: please merge into the real build path):
  1. BIG offset expansion (|off|>0x7FFF): `op reg,BIG(base)` -> lui $at,hi ; addu $at,BASE,$at ; op reg,lo($at) (base FIRST; this rule existed as bigoff_re) but it did
     not cover the pseudo ops `l.s`/`s.s` that gcc emits for float fields (`l.s $f0,111388($18)`): now mapped to lwc1/swc1 (func_0028D658).
  2. The assembler never filled a RETURN delay slot with a multi-insn macro expansion (lui/addu/sw of a big-offset or sym+idx store): ROM has
     `lui;addu;sw ...(at); jr ra; nop`, modern gas moved the sw into the slot (func_0028E044).  Fixed with a `lastmacro` flag in the `dep` test.
- fchk needs MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 when started from Git-Bash (`wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -- bash /mnt/d/...`).  fchk.sh syncs only include/ src/ wip/.
- tools/asmfn.py only read asm/entry.s and asm/16E000.s; the main-image range 0x2BD34.. lives in asm/2BD34.s (patched).
- Functions that call memcpy/sqrtf need -fno-builtin in the chk line (inline lwl/lwr copy otherwise): list in results.txt (flags=[...]).
- ro= pieces: a float constant that sits in the object's literal pool (hoisted out of loops, or just loaded via lui/lwc1) must be written as a LITERAL
  (e.g. 7.5f, 34.0f, 3.4028235e+38f (FLT_MAX), -1.0f) - chk then prints ro=0xCB098:8 and the pool addresses of neighbouring functions line up
  (func_0028C544 ro=0xCAFD8, CABC 0xCAFDC, CB8C 0xCAFE0, D0E4 0xCAFE4:8, D268 0xCAFF0 ...).  Hoisting out of a loop into a callee-saved f-reg only
  happens for literals, never for `extern f32 D_xxx` (calls in the loop may clobber it).  Shared named floats like D_800D2988 (1.0f) are real .data globals.
  Helper wip/f32.py <hex word> prints the shortest literal that round-trips.

SOURCE-SHAPE LESSONS (gcc 2.8.1 -O2 -mcpu=r4300)
- LOOPS: the same loop compiles differently as do-while-with-guard, while, and for.
  * `for (i = 0; i < N; i++) {...}` (test at the bottom, LOOP_CONT note) made delay-slot filling choose `beql ...; i++` exits exactly like the ROM
    (func_0028E36C/E480/E5AC nested pixel-remap loops; a do-while with `if (n > 0)` guard gave the same instructions but one `beqz` + fill difference).
  * `while (--n != -1)` (pre-decrement compared with -1) reproduces `addiu n,n,-1; beq n,-1` loop heads (func_0028C400, E36C).
  * `if (n > 0) do { ... } while (++i < n);` with `i = 0; n = lv->cnt; ... p = lv->arr` gave pointer-walking loops (func_0028CC80).
  * An ARRAY BASE held in a local pointer plus index, `Entity **arr = lv->list; for (; i < n; i++) { e = arr[i]; ...}`, makes gcc compute the strength-
    reduced base once (`addiu v0,s2,0xC50 ; addu s0,v0,zero` inside the guard) like the ROM; indexing `lv->list[i]` directly folds the displacement
    (`lw a0,0xC50(s0)`) and mismatches (func_0028B004, CB8C, CCB8).  Declare i=0 and n=count first, then arr = ..., loop `for (; i < n; i++)`.
  * `e = &lv->entities[i]` (array-of-struct indexing, i*0x2E8) gives the ROM's single giv pointer (func_0028CABC, B64C), pointer++ forms do not.
- Struct assign `*out = D_800D0EE0;` (24 bytes) vs two Vec3f member assigns changes pseudo priorities (func_0028C6B0: only the struct assign matched).
- A load that is conditionally re-read after a store gets a `move` copy only if the source reads the field twice in separate statements
  (`o->cur = o->head; c = o->head;` -> lw v0 ; addu v1,v0 ; sw v0 ...) (func_0028EAAC).
- `x = f(); if (x != 0) { A } else { B }` (positive test first) and early `return` forms matter for the delay slot: `if (idx == 0) return 0; base = f(..)+8; return ...`
  (local `base` pointer) matched func_0028B2D4/B32C, the one-expression forms did not.
- Return shape: `if (a) { if (b) { ... return call(); } } return 0;` with a temp (`q = o->p; if (q == 0) { zero: return 0; } return *q;`) and a goto into the
  `return 0` label reproduced shared-return blocks (func_0028D450).
- Range tests: `key >= 0x64 && key < 0x69` is folded to one sltiu; ROM keeps `slti 0x69 ; slti 0x64` -> write gotos into a shared arm (func_0028BE88).
  Multi-term `||` chains of equality tests on a u32 temp (t == 0x44E || t == 0x453 ...) compile to separate beq's like the ROM (func_0028E768/E83C) while a
  `switch` with those labels turned into a jump table.  Conversely a `switch` whose cases cover a dense range 1..7 (with empty cases for the unused values)
  gives the ROM's jump table (func_0028F524: list `case 1: case 4: case 5: break;` explicitly).
- Switch blocks are laid out in SOURCE order of the cases (func_0028E910: case 0x29A first although the compare tree is balanced on 0x29B).
- Two bit-set helpers share the pattern `s32 m = 1 << (bit & 7); if (set) bits[bit/8] |= m; else b2[bit/8] &= ~m;` with `b2 = bits` (a second local copy
  of the pointer, ROM has two registers) (func_0028B3C0 family).  `bit / 8` (signed) for the bgez/addiu 7 fixup, `s32 bit = s->unk19E` (u16 field copied to s32).
- Unsigned division by constant (744) comes from `(u32)((u8*)e - (u8*)base) / 0x2E8` NOT from pointer subtraction (func_0028D728).
- 3-argument calls where the ROM leaves a1 untouched are K&R-declared (`void *func_0028FD94();`) in the caller (func_0028AFC8, 0028D1F4, 0028D220, 0028CF48).
- Nested `if (a != 0) { if (a >= 0) { if (a < 3) {` keeps the three separate compares (an && chain was merged into a range test) (func_0028D0E4).
- Struct params by value: `Vec3f v` in (a2,a3,stack) with callee `Vec3f` params reproduces the sw/lw pairs (func_0028BFB4, 0028CE70).
- Large frames: a dead `volatile` read statement is NOT hoisted above `subu sp` by the scheduler; ROM func_00290A68 starts with a global load before the sp adjust
  (plain loads ARE hoisted that way, e.g. func_0028EAAC) - the original probably used a non-volatile construct that I could not find.

OPEN ISSUES (see results.txt PARTIAL lines)
- Register-allocation order of equal-priority callee-saved pseudos (best/bestd, outRecs/outCount) in func_0028C810 / 0028C248; giv combination of o+0x130
  in a stride-0x140 slot loop (func_002911E8); strength reduction of `for (i=9;i>=0;i--) D_global[i]=0` (ROM v0=0x24;v0-=4; we keep sll) in func_0028E260/E0E4;
  3-way ramp layout in func_00290E4C; `mult` placement before an unfilled beqz in func_0028FEEC.

ADDITIONAL LESSONS (second half of the batch)
- u64 time math: `D_800D29A4 = (f32)(((osGetTime() - D_8011F2A8) * 64) / 3) * 0.000001f;` with `extern u64 D_8011F2A8;` reproduces the whole
  sltu/subu/sll/srl + __udivdi3 + __cmpdi2 + __floatdisf (unsigned DI->float with the halving branch) sequence (func_0028F258, 0028ED80).  Do NOT keep the
  u64 in a local (`u64 d = ...; (f32)d`): gcc then copies the pair into 4 callee-saved regs.  A u64 global store `D = osGetTime();` emits two
  `lui;sw` pairs (separate lui for D and D+4) (func_0028FA40).
- A switch with a hole range (cases 0,1,2 where case 0 is empty) gives the balanced compare tree `beq 1; slti 2; bnez end; beq 2` - add the explicit empty
  `case 0: break;` (func_0028A674).  Cases laid out in source order; a dense 1..7 range needs the empty cases to get the jump table.
- struct-typed global whose field is read first through `&G` pointer local: `G462 *g = &D_801462C8; ... g->unk4` keeps the base register = &G
  (accessing `D_801462C8.unk4` directly makes gcc use base = &G+4 and derive -0x1244) (func_0028A674).
- s16/u16 field passed to a callee: use an s32 local (`s32 a1 = lv->unk1B430;` from an s16 field gives `lh`, and the else arm `a1 = lv->unk1B438` stays a plain
  `lw` with no sll/sra) instead of an s16 local (func_0028A674).
- `lv->f = lv->f + C; if (p->div < lv->f)` (no local temp) matched where `t = lv->f + C; lv->f = t; if (p->div < t)` swapped f0/f1 (func_0028A674).
- Early-return (`if (idx == -1) { *outCount = 0; return; }`) + if/else for the rest fixed the callee-saved assignment of the two output pointers (func_0028C248).
- Two loads of the same field in separate statements give a register copy (see above); loading into a struct-by-value Vec3f param copy needs 12-byte struct assign.
- When a function takes the big Level object and reads `*(s16*)(def+0x28)` etc., local `typedef struct {...} DefQ;` views are fine; helper structs were kept local
  to the .c files, the shared recovered layout is include/b20_types.h (Level: arrays of Entity* at 0x144/0x948/0xC50/0xE54/0xF5C/0xFE0/0x1024/0x10A8,
  Entity stride 0x2E8 at lv->entities (0x138) with count 0x140, Rec14 records at 0x11D0/0x11D4, Vid/Job video-thread objects, Node list owner at +0x3C00).
- Per-object literal pools: every float constant in a function is its own pool entry (never shared), so two functions using 1e-6f have separate entries
  (ED80 has four 0.000001f entries CA444..CA450).  `D_800D2988` (1.0f) is a shared data global, not a literal.
- The inline helper `static inline Job *frameOk(Job*)` (copy of func_0028F98C) inlines into func_0028F524 exactly like the ROM; static inline helpers are
  fine when the ROM clearly contains an inlined copy of another game function.
- Stray words: func_0028FD68 (2), func_002909C4 (3) and func_002909D8 (2) are linker padding words (leftover lui/sw/addiu) between functions, not code.

SUMMARY: 136 targets: 118 EXACT, 15 PARTIAL (best attempts in wip/hard), 3 NONE (padding words).  Many PARTIALs differ by 1-4 words of scheduling /
register naming (see notes in results.txt).

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b21
FINDINGS - batch 21 (game code ROM 0x92004-0x99xxx, 138 targets)  [interim, appended as work progresses]

TOOLING FIXES (apply to tools/ in the lead's tree)
- tools/gas_compat.py: (1) `bigoff_re` now also matches the gcc pseudo-ops l.s / s.s / l.d / s.d (a `swc1 f0,0x26DC4($a0)` store with a
  >0x7FFF offset was expanded with the wrong base order: ROM is `lui $at; addu $at,BASE,$at; swc1 ..($at)`).
  (2) a macro-expanded big-offset load/store is never moved into a `jr $ra` delay slot by the original assembler (no ROM instance of
  `jr ra ; sw lo($at)` exists): after the expansion set prev = 'lw' so the return keeps its nop (fixes `lui,addu,sb / lui,addu,sw / jr / nop`).
- tools/chk.py: an optional first flag `GCCV=<ver>` (e.g. `GCCV=2.7.2 -O2`) selects tools/gcc/<ver> per list line (cache key includes it).
- tools/asmfn.py only read asm/entry.s and asm/16E000.s; it now scans every asm/*.s (functions of this range are in asm/2BD34.s).

COMPILER FLAGS
- Almost everything matches `-O2 -mcpu=r4300`.  A few functions (func_00294428, func_00293EAC) matched only with plain `-O2` (= -mcpu=r4000
  scheduling model, also gives `lw` hoisted before `addiu sp`); per function the first flag set that gives EXACT is recorded in results.txt.
  -mcpu=r4300 vs r4000 changes more than scheduling: with r4000 gcc turns `if (!f()) a = 0;` into the branch-less sltu/negu/and form,
  r4300 keeps the beql (func_00293268).
- Function layout facts: stray words in the ROM that are not code: func_00294C98, func_00297018, func_00297CC8 (2 words each) are padding;
  func_00295B84 starts with 3 padding words (beqz/lui/lui), the real function starts at VRAM 0x295B90 (label D_00295B90, used as a callback
  pointer by func_00294CA0/func_00296E04/func_00296EC4 -> write `func_00295B90`).

SOURCE-SHAPE LESSONS (gcc 2.8.1)
- Objects around D_80145040 (D_80145088, D_801462C8, D_801468A0, ...) are members of ONE struct (B21World in include/b21_world.h):
  addresses derived with `addiu v0,s0,-0x5D8` from a base register come from cse "related values" of the same symbol.  Take sub-struct
  pointers explicitly (`B21GameBlk *b = &W.blk; B21Sub1288 *s = &W.sub1288; b->unkAC = 0; s->unk1D = 0;`), assign them where the ROM computes
  them (after a branch if the lui/addiu sits after it), and make sure sizeof(substruct) is a multiple of 4 (a 0xB3-byte struct shifted the next member).
- But the SAME memory is sometimes accessed in one function through separate scalar externs (lui $at / sw %lo) in one part and through a base
  register in another (func_002954E0, func_00294CA0): declare separate `extern` names for the pieces that the ROM accesses stand-alone and the
  struct/array for the rest; chk only compares resolved addresses so any mix is fine.
- Static-looking tiny functions with no callers (func_00295AE0/AF0/B00/B18/B30) are plain `inline` functions (emitted out of line AND inlined)
  defined in the same file as their user func_002953FC (jump tables of both in one rodata piece).  Verify them with the same .c.
- `if (!(a <= b)) a = b;`-style float min/max and `x = (f32)i < 0 ? -i : i` (int abs via float compare) occur; the second needs the exact
  `(f32)v < 0.0f ? -v : v` text.
- Calls whose pass-through argument registers are untouched (func_002518DC(..), func_0029A1D4(id, l, dir)): keep the pass-through param.
- Two-statement tail `d = D_80110634++; d->w0 = ..; d->w1 = ..;` (display list push) matched in every function that had it; only the first two
  pushes of func_00293100 differ in register choice.
- A local initialised from a stack-passed argument (`x1 = lrx; if (x1 < 0) x1 = 0;`) makes gcc keep the stack arg in memory (loaded where used)
  instead of in a callee-saved register (func_00293100).
- Jump over `if (D) {..} ` blocks: to make the ROM's `beq v1,1 -> far block` layout use `if (s != 1) { if (s == 2) {..} } else { ..state 1.. }`.
- Reassociation: `w | (y | C)` is folded to `(w|C)|y`; the ROM's `(y|C)|w` needs a temp (`t = y | C; w0 = w | t`).
- `p = &pl[i]` (explicit pointer after an early `continue`) stops gcc from strength-reducing pl[i] into a walking pointer (func_00297AD4).
- switch with default placed after case 0x106 etc.: an early `if (flag == 0) return 0;` before the switch gave the ROM's two distinct `return 0` blocks.

BRUTE-FORCE OF SOURCE SHAPES WORKS (batch 21 closed 6 near-misses this way; tool: wip/mv.py <fn> variants.txt "<flags>" --promote, one fchk call for all variants)
 Generate the Cartesian product of a few independent syntactic knobs with a small python script and let chk pick the EXACT one:
  - operand order of commutative expressions and association: `8 + dx + dy * 17` (func_00297390/474) vs `dx + dy*17 + 8` etc.
  - one temp per use instead of reusing one variable (func_00297C30: nx/ny/nz instead of one `n`; func_00297B74 needs a separate temp for the
    first axis only; func_00293100: separate pointer/`w` variables `d1,d2`, `w2` for the second half of a function).
  - ternary assignment `s->f = c ? A : B;` instead of if/else with two stores (func_00293038: identical code but different register allocation).
  - accumulate into a separate variable (`d = x; d += y; d += z;`) instead of `x + y + z` when the ROM shows an extra `mov.s` copy (func_00297C30).
  - ROM register numbering differences (v0/v1/a0 or s3/s4 swaps) are decided by pseudo priority = number of references / live length and,
    on ties, by pseudo creation order: they are cured by changing how often/where a value is mentioned, not by declaration order
    (declaration order of locals never mattered).
 Things that did NOT help: -mcpu changes, -fno-schedule-insns*, -fno-cse-skip-blocks (func_002935A4 reloads a state byte after a join
 that our gcc CSEs through the join), volatile.
 Unsolved shapes: func_00294CA0 (n2/hi swapped s4/s5; ROM has a surviving copy of hi at the start of the strlen branch), func_00297020
 (callee-saved numbering g/seeds/p), func_0029193C (ROM materialises each constant right before its store, all via $v0),
 func_00291404 (dead `addiu s0,s0,-0x5D8` kept before an infinite loop), func_002975B0 (ROM does not CSE &t2 over five plane blocks),
 func_00298310 family (zero constant kept in s0 and re-zeroed in arms; a2 spilled).

=================== FINDINGS from workspace b22
FINDINGS batch 22 (sound manager / math helpers, ROM 0x99634-0xA0724) -- append-style
- Tooling: wip/ck.py (check wip/c/<fn>.c), wip/vv.py (variants separated by '//----' lines, one fchk call), wip/side.py (side-by-side; NOTE
  lu_side marks only non-register diffs with '!=' -- register-only differences are invisible there; trust chk), wip/ro.py (dump literals from ROM:
  rom = (va & 0x7FFFFFFF) + 0xC00), wip/fin.py (builds wip/out + results.txt).  Variant file names must not start with '_v_' (fchk rsync excludes them).
- tools/asmfn.py only read asm/entry.s + asm/16E000.s: patched to glob asm/*.s (functions of later batches live in asm/2BD34.s etc).
- Chained assignment `v->x = v->y = v->z = 0.0f;` makes gcc keep 0.0 in an FP reg (mtc1 $zero + 3 swc1, stores in z,y,x order); separate stores use `sw $zero`.
- A guarded sqrt `if (x <= 0) return 0; return sqrtf(x);` inlined from a `static inline float B22Sqrt(float)` matches the vector length helpers
  (the arg gets copied `mov.s $f12,$f1` in the jal delay slot); writing the test directly in the function gives different FP register numbers.
  A top-level `if (x <= 0.0f) return 0.0f; return sqrtf(x);` also matches func_0029E8BC.  (-fno-builtin needed whenever sqrtf is called.)
- Function with 16 f32 params after a pointer = matrix setter (a1..a3 + 13 stack words), plain f32 params, NOT a by-value struct (that spills a1..a3).
- `ok = 1; if (a) ok = 0; else if (b) ok = 0; if (ok)...` : the else-if keeps the beql shape; two plain ifs get folded into sltu (store-flag).
- `s32 i = top - 1 - n` is folded to top - (n+1); ROM has (top-1)-n: use `t = top - 1; i = t - n` with a temp, and the positive test `if (i >= 0) return x; return -1;`.
- Struct-pointer loops over 0x14 byte slots: ROM with two induction pointers (id at +0x1C, fields at +0x20/+0x24) = pointer-increment loop `s++`, not s[i].
- Order of two field loads feeding one address (data + off) follows a temp: `o = b->wrpos;` first and then `b->data + o` gave wrpos-first loads (func_0029AB74).
- (later additions) The slot-table helpers are `static inline` in the original: func_0029AA80 = inline SlotC(id) (loop `s++` over D_8014D080->slot, returns s->unkC or 0)
  + `fn = (VpFn)SlotC(c->unkE); if (fn) { fn(c, vp); return 1; } return 0;`; the callback arg is a 40-byte struct passed BY VALUE (B22Viewport).
- Callback/notify pattern (func_0029A5D4, 00298EA4, 0029A1D4 (A5D4 inlined twice, so A5D4 is declared `inline`)): the saved word must be written as
  `saved = D->unk520; D->unk520 = 0; r = D->fn0(...); if (D_8014D080->unk520 == 1) { D_8014D080->unk520 = saved; return r; } D_8014D080->unk520 = saved;`
  (compare against the global directly, no local `f`).  An extra local `f = -1; ... top == f` (a variable that is reused later in the original) moved
  the constant -1 into a1 instead of v0 (local-alloc vs global-alloc): when the ROM compares against -1 in a high reg while v0/v1/a0 are busy, the constant
  is a GLOBAL pseudo (defined earlier / variable).  Parameter copy `b = a;` before a call kept the copy that the ROM has (func_00298EA4).
- -dg/-dl dumps are available: wip/dump.sh <file.c> (WSL) writes /tmp/dump/t.c.{greg,lreg,sched,rtl}; the .greg `Register dispositions` shows hard regs of pseudos.
- Math library (libm, double): floor/ceil are `inline` functions with `x + 2^52 - 2^52`; floor body in the out-of-line copy needs `if (!(x < t)) return t; return t - 1.0;`
  while the copies inlined into ceil/modf/fmod need `if (x < t) return t - 1.0; return t;` (use a differently named static inline for those).
  fmod (func_0029C278) = q = x/y; q = q < 0 ? Ceil_(q) : Floor_(q); x - q*y.  Angle wrap helper `static inline float Wrap(float)` (if |a|>8pi: a = (float)fmod(a, 2pi);
  else while (a > 2pi) a -= 2pi; while (a < 0) a += 2pi) is inlined in sinf (result unused!), sincos, wrapAngle (F04C/F104/F1D4: `a = a + pi; a = Wrap(a); return a - pi;`).
  asin = Cephes polynomial (func_0029EE48), acos = pi/2 - inline asin; the pool constants appear once per function (separate literal loads per use, no sharing across functions).
- FP register numbering follows the creation order of pseudos: user temporaries that are modified in place (`w = w * cz;`) reproduce the ROM's in-place `mul.s fs0,fs0,..`;
  introducing a temp for the product that appears first in the RTL (`w = cx * p;` before `x1 = sx*cz - w`) flipped which of two same-priority values got the lower register.
  func_0029C6E0/C54C/C868/C3C0 (euler angles from a matrix) matched this way (C3C0 and C868 still a few words off).
- A function with `if (x <= 0.0) return -inf;` followed by a frexp inline whose zero test is `x == 0.0` loses the (redundant) zero test in our gcc; writing the inline as
  `if (x != 0.0) {...} else {e = 0; r = 0.0;}` keeps it (func_0029BD38 log).  Identical code tails in both arms of an if (`y = y + 0.5;`) are cross-jumped, write them twice.
- `(E < c) || (c < E)` (same positive literal twice) appears in func_0029DAB4: the original had a sign typo, use the literal as in the ROM pool (only one pool word).
- 4x4 matrix multiply/inverse: pointer-based loops (`ap = &a->m[0][0]; dp += 4; bp += 4`) reproduce the ROM's 4 induction pointers (func_0029DE3C); index form m[i][j] does not.
  `det = 1.0f / det; inv = det;` (in-place reuse of the variable) fixed FP numbering in func_0029E248; unnamed cofactor expressions (CSE'd) instead of named c0/c1/c2.
- Chains of `if (press) A; else if (release) B; else ...` written with a macro and loads of the state words BEFORE the first call matched func_0029B650 first try (245 insns).
- Euler/matrix helpers (sinf, sincosf, atan2f, asinf...) all use the same literal-per-use pool; atan2f = if/else tree + inline atanf (ED78 body, `static inline`) + D_800D2B60[q] table (func_0029D044).
- Out-of-line copies of functions that are also inlined elsewhere (floor, ceil, F93C poly intersection, A5D4 notify, D984 euler matrix) often need a *different* source shape than the inlined copies.
  Both copies must be tested: write the inline caller first (func_0029A1D4 / func_0029D4A0 / func_0029E9DC / func_0029C278 show whether the body is right).
- Inline params: passing `out++` as the argument produces the `v0 = a0; a0 = v0 + 12` pattern (func_0029D4A0); a user variable passed to an inline function keeps a register copy only if the original has an
  explicit extra variable (func_00298EA4 needed `b = a;`).
- GCC 2.8 cse removes redundant clamps/tests when two inline expansions use identical literals in the same EBB, the ROM often does not (func_0029D288, BD38 zero test): the ROM compiler
  apparently had different const-pool sharing; keeping a comparison needs a different formulation (`!=` arm order) rather than a copy.

0. TOOLING (new, workspace tools/):
 - tools/fchk.ps1 + tools/mk.sh <fn...> (checks wip/out or wip/hard file with the right rom), tools/v.py (variant file, bodies separated by '#### BODY'),
   tools/gen.py <template> <fn> <json> (cartesian placeholder search through ONE chk call), tools/side.sh <file> <fn> [flags] (side-by-side asm with
   -O2 -mcpu=r4300; NB lu_side only flags mnemonic/instruction-count differences, register-only differences are invisible: use tools/rdiff.py,
   which prints every line whose registers differ), tools/res.py (regenerates wip/out/results.txt from wip/out + wip/notes.txt + wip/flags.txt).

   directly precedes `jr $ra` must NOT be moved into the return delay slot (old gas never did; modern gas did).  Fixes func_002A27F8 (and every
   `D[i] = x; return;` shape).  Regression-checked: 35 game + 240 libultra chk results unchanged.

1. GOTO TRANSCRIPTION IS THE BEST TOOL FOR STRUCTURE: m2c (and the asm) already give the exact CFG.  Writing the C with the same labels/gotos
   (and `if (x) goto L;` for every conditional branch, in ROM block order) produced EXACT code for functions where 10+ structured variants failed:
   strncpy func_002A1294 (the zero-fill test is shared by loop exit and NUL break), func_002A438C, func_002A3090, and got the exact block layout /
   instruction count of func_002A0888 (_splitpath), func_002A0E38 (qsort), func_002A453C, func_002A1444, func_002A152C.  Do it first, structure later.
   A goto loop is NOT a loop for loop.c: no invariant hoisting / strength reduction (so `&local` stays an `addiu a0,sp,N` before each call).
2. SINGLE RETURN: a function that ROM shows as `... jr ra; addu v0,a0,zero` inline in the middle and one shared `jr ra; nop` at the end is
   `if (x) {...} else {...} return dst;` (one return at the end; gcc duplicates the epilogue into fall-through paths).  memmove func_002A0470.
   Conversely `if (total == 0) return 0; return call()/size;` (early return first) matched fread where `if (total) return ..; return 0;` did not.
3. VARIABLE REUSE explains "extra copy" registers: memmove keeps `addu v1,a1,zero` because ONE temp k is initialised with (u32)src and later
   reused as alignment count and loop counter.  Delay-slot copies like `addu a2,a3,zero` before a `beqz` come from assigning the copy BEFORE the
   test in source (`c = *name; p = name; if (c == 0) goto ..;`) - func_002A1960 (makepath).
4. STRUCT COPIES: assignment of a struct needs word alignment info to get the unrolled lw/sw block move: use `typedef struct {s32 w[N];}`
   (u8 b[N] gives a call/loop).  Vec3f copies that ROM does as lwc1/swc1 must be written per float field (`v.x = e->pos.x; ...`), a struct copy gives lw/sw.
   By-value struct args: a 12-byte struct in a1..a3 is stored to its home slot and `&arg` points at it (func_002A2BE0); a 28-byte struct on the stack is copied
   with lw/sw groups of 4+3 (declare both as s32 w[N] structs, pass them straight through).  Vec3f in a1..a3 + Vec3f on the stack: func_002A6BD4, 6010.
5. VARARGS: `void f(char *buf, char *fmt, ...) { va_list ap = (char *)__builtin_next_arg(fmt); ...vsprintf(buf, fmt, ap) }` gives exactly
   `sw a1..a3` (a0 not stored: it is only the last NAMED arg that is stored) and ap = &fmt+4 as a constant; `f(char *fmt, ...)` stores a0..a3.
   (stdarg.h of the SDK is not needed; __builtin_next_arg works in 2.8.1.  The K&R __builtin_va_alist form takes &arg and adds a stray store.)
6. FUNCTION POINTER TABLES / STATE: `s32 *p = &pos;` (address-taken int) gives a memory-resident local with lw/sw 0x10(sp) everywhere (func_002A5124);
   a local pointer to a global (`s32 *p = &D_800D2BB8; *p = ..; D_800D2BB0 = f(D_800D2BB8)`) stops store->load forwarding and keeps the
   lui/addiu base register (func_002A1D38).  A global used by several loads/stores with a call in between: struct/array with fields (base reg kept) vs
   plain scalars (lui each time) - the choice is visible in the asm, copy it per function (the same symbol is a struct in one function, scalars in the next).
7. Two nested identical float ifs (`if (zero < x) { if (zero < x) {..} }`) are the way to get the duplicated `c.lt.s; bc1f; bc1f` of ROM (&& folds them).
   Hoisting 0.0f and a global float into locals before a do-while (`zero = 0.0f; k = D;`) is what puts them in callee-saved FP regs (func_002A67D0).
   `(x_f32)n` conversions + (s32) round trips: compute all four results into locals before the sh stores (func_002A3478).
8. Float literals passed as args: `func_xxx(m, 1.0f)` loads via lui ... 0x3F80 into a GPR; unused-arg pass-through: call a K&R-declared function with
   fewer args and gcc leaves $a1.. untouched (func_002A3090 passes a0 only; func_002A5588 calls func_002796AC with a0 only).
9. A switch with sparse range test + jump table (func_002A5124): table lives at ro=0xCBBA0:24 (VA 0x800CAFA0), chk reports it.
10. printf-family: the inline function `static __inline__ void cpyn(u8*,u8*,s32)` containing gotos is accepted by 2.8.1 and expands 4 times
    (labels are made unique) - _splitpath's four strncpy+terminator sequences are inlined copies of strncpy.
11. REGISTER-ALLOCATION RESIDUE (not solved, 12 PARTIALs differ only by register names, see results.txt notes): FP pairs (v in f0 / 0.0 in f1 reversed),
    s-reg role permutations (qsort, printf, _splitpath), loop invariants landing in v1/a1/a0 vs a1/a0/v1.  Declaration order, init order, goto vs
    structured, gcc 2.7.2/2.91/2.95 all made no difference; likely the original source had different temporaries/macros.
12. Stray words: func_002A286C, func_002A308C, func_002A35BC (1 word each) and func_002A0468 (2 words) are leftover words between objects
    (func_002A0468 even branches into the next function's body); func_002A2264 is the 3-instruction head `jal func_0040FC14` whose delay slot is the
    first instruction of func_002A2270 - treat as padding / part of 2270.  func_002A1AC4 (printf wrapper) starts with 3 stray words: C is EXACT at ROM+0xC.
13. Library identification (names in results.txt): 0x2A0470 memmove, 0x2A1748/memset (2 copies), 0x2A125C strcpy, 0x2A137C strcmp-like, 0x2A13AC strncmp,
    0x2A1494/14E0 strupr/strlwr, 0x2A15D0 atoi, 0x2A1814 memcmp, 0x2A18B8 crc32, 0x2A166C itoa, 0x2A1960 makepath, 0x2A0888 _splitpath,
    0x2A0E38/0B74 qsort, 0x2A10B0 bsearch, 0x2A11E8 rand (LCG 0x343FD/0x269EC3), 0x2A1ED4 fread, 0x2A1FCC fgetc, 0x2A05E0 atof(strtod).

FINDINGS batch b24 (ROM 0xA7BE0-0xAEE5C, func_002A6FE0..func_002AE25C)

TOOLING
- tools/asmfn.py only read asm/entry.s and asm/16E000.s; the main image is split over asm/*.s now.  Patched to glob asm/*.s.
- tools/gas_compat.py: VR4300 FP-multiply erratum nop also applies between `mul.s/mul.d` and a following integer `mult/multu`
  (ROM func_002AC1A0 has `mul.s ; nop ; mult`).  One-line change (added 'mult','multu' to the existing mul.s/mul.d rule) in
  my tools/gas_compat.py; please merge.  With it func_002AC1A0 is EXACT.
- Side-by-side in the fast loop: wip/side.sh (lu_ud.sh with FLAGS='-O2 -mcpu=r4300') + tools/lu_side.py; variant search: wip/vv.py
  (full-file variants separated by //====, one fchk call).  rsync of fchk.sh excludes `_v_*` files, so do not name variants _v_*.

SOURCE-SHAPE LESSONS
- Loop bounds: `for (i = N; i != -1; i--)` gives the ROM's `bne i, $reg(-1)` loop (const -1 in a register), `i >= 0` gives bgez.
  Table-lookup functions "walk a pointer over N rows of stride S comparing an s16 at +4" are exactly:
  `Row *p = T; for (i = N-1; i != -1; i--) { if (p->id == id) return p; p++; } return NULL;`
- `r = hdr[1] - 1; for (; r != -1; r--)` is folded (beqz on hdr[1]); the ROM keeps the compare against -1: write
  `r = hdr[1]; r--; for (; r != -1; r--)` (separate statements).  Same for `c = hdr[0]; c--;`.
- A pointer advance written `p = p + a + 8` folds the +8 into the add; the ROM's `addu; (delay) addiu 8` came from two statements
  `p += a; p += 8;` (func_002AC0A0, func_002ABDBC).
- A constant that is stored in a loop (`tab[i] = 0xFF`) and also used as a loop counter init: the ROM has two separate `li`;
  introducing a `u8 c = 0xFF;` local that is stored (`*p-- = c`) reproduces the two li (func_002AC34C).
- s16 parameters passed through to an s16 callee parameter: declaring the params s16 in our function changed register
  allocation (priority) to the ROM's and the call-site sll/sra is still emitted (func_002ABC18) - try both s32 and s16.
- `c->cur++` (u16 field) etc: gcc folds the u16 truncation; the ROM keeps `sh; andi 0xFFFF; andi 3; sh`.  Not reproduced (func_002A80C4).
- F32 loop counters: `f32 rows = hdr[1]; while (rows-- != 0.0f)` gives the guard + decrement-before-test form seen in the ROM
  (func_002ABED4/002AC1A0); u32 operands `(f32)w` give cvt.d.w + 2^32 literal; every `1.0f` literal gets its own pool entry.
- Inlined helpers with by-address out parameters keep their out locals on the stack (func_002ABD04 frame 0x30).
- Struct return pointer `TabInfo *info` passed by address then read twice: gcc reloads it after a store through another pointer; the ROM
  does not (func_002AB940, not reproduced).
- DISPLAY-LIST FUNCTIONS (func_002AACC0, 002ABA44, 002A94E8, 002AA224, 002AB9E4...): the original uses libultra gbi macros with a
  BLOCK-LOCAL `Gfx *_g = (Gfx*)(glistp++);` per command.  Write
    #define GFX(a, b) { DlCmd *_g = D_80110634++; _g->w0 = (a); _g->w1 = (b); }
    #define SHIFTL(v,s,w) (((u32)(v) & ((0x01 << (w)) - 1)) << (s))
    #define MAX(a,b) ((a) > (b) ? (a) : (b))
  and pass the whole expression of each word as macro args (e.g. gDPScisFillRectangle = SHIFTL(MAX(x,0),14,10)...,
  gDPLoadTextureBlock_4b: lrs = (((w*h)+3)>>2)-1 clamped `< 0x800 ? v : 0x7FF`, dxt = ((1<<11)+TXL2WORDS_4b(w)-1)/TXL2WORDS_4b(w),
  TXL2WORDS_4b(w) = (1 > (w)/16 ? 1 : (w)/16)).  Statement-level `g = D_80110634++; g->w0=..` variants (what the older batches used)
  gave different register allocation for the hoisted constants; the block-local macro form matched 5 of 5 functions at once.
  A call between commands (`func_0026925C(0x19)`) is just a call; callees taking only a0 get a1..a3 clobbered/hoisted constants.
- Pass-through arguments again: a callee prototype with an extra leading arg (func_00222BC4(e, a, b)) fixed a whole function; check
  `addu sN,$a0` (copy) without an `addu $a0,...` before the jal.

== late additions (batch 24) ==
- switch: write "default: case 0:" (not "case 0: default:") to keep a jump-table entry for case 0; gcc prunes it otherwise.
- u8 locals used as table indices produce the andi 0xFF masks seen in ROM; use u8, not s32.
- gcc 2.8.1 emits bzero (not memset) for array-initializer zero-fill; a struct copy plus explicit memset(&buf[n],0,k) with -fno-builtin matched func_002A81B0 prologue shape.
- Cross-jump merging of single identical tail insns (two sb stores) blocked func_002AD65C; ROM did not merge them.
- func_002ACBCC: globals at negative offsets from D_801468A0 (e.g. -0x1840) are best written as *(T*)((u8*)D_801468A0 - 0x1840). Moving "ok = 1" after the inner if (instead of before it) removed ~490 differing words; statement order around calls matters for delay-slot filling.
- Declaration order and the 'register' keyword do not influence gcc 2.8.1 saved-register assignment here (o/i/off priority); 40 shuffled declaration orders all gave identical output.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace m01
FINDINGS - workspace m01 (leftover functions of the main image 0x1000-0x1BE00)  [draft, extended at the end]

TOOLING
- wip/ck.sh/sd.sh/vv.py/alnv.py/pstmts.py/hcs.py: single-function check, aligned side-by-side (difflib with reloc/branch-target masks),
  N-variant runner (one chk call), aligned-diff counter per file, statement-permutation search, hill-climber on statement order.
- wip/tgen.py: types_spec.txt -> include/m01_types.h (explicit padding structs M1Actor/M1State/M1Info/M1Ctl...).
- decomp-permuter on game code: wip/perm_go.py <fn> (handles stray junk words at function start via wip/rom_ovr.txt), perm_bg.sh runs it
  in the background, pd.sh/pchk.sh/pcp.sh show/verify the best output with the real chk.  A permuter score of 0..~20 is usually an exact
  match when re-checked with chk (permuter and chk agree).  wip/permcc_low.sh + perm_low.py: permuter for the low region (@C mode).
- low region (ROM 0x1000-0x3900) functions need special modes (chkx tokens, wip/chkx.py+epifix.py copied from b01); wip/donex.py tries the
  modes and writes the results line.  -mcpu=r4000 + @C (= -msplit-addresses + unfilled `addu sp; j $31; nop` epilogue) matches almost all.
  func_00202CA0 and above (0x38A0+) compile with the normal game mode.

LESSONS
- Permuter wins: duplicate-arm `if (X){S}else{S}`, wrapping body in do{...}while(0), hoisted pointer temps (ow = other->owner), lim=0xF-style constants.
- Duplicate-arm if/else breaks &local CSE caching across calls (>=2 uses in one EBB cache the address in a saved reg); use it to uncache.
- `lui a2,0x4000` argument is an f32 2.0f (declare prototype f32). Vec3f by value = a1..a3 + stack; 16-byte struct return = hidden a0 pointer.
- Block-copy structs must be word-aligned (s32 w[]) or gcc emits lwl/lwr.
- A junk `lui` word before a function entry can masquerade as a different load form; use the real entry (wip/rom_ovr.txt).
- Local layout: first-declared local gets the lowest stack address.
- switch with `case 0:` plus default changes the decision tree (slti form).
- Unresolved: ROM keeps unmerged duplicate tails (214310); FP reg swaps (202FDC, 2070D0 22 words); &local caching/&A,&w hoist (214624, 59 aligned diff); struct-copy temp regs (2095F8); clamp bc1tl (216F44).
- Non-EXACT targets with drafts are in wip/hard/<fn>.prev.c / wip/c; results.txt marks them NONE with no exact C delivered.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace m02
FINDINGS m02 (leftover main-image functions 0x1C068-0x366D0) -- append-style, written while working
TOOLING
- wip/ck.sh/vv.py/sdf.sh/fin.py/pps.py/perm.sh/gen.py/lit.py (in wip/, workspace m02): fast check, variant search over full files (one chk call), side-by-side (--norm hides reg/reloc noise), result writer, permuter splice, struct generator (types.spec -> include/m02_gen.h with explicit padding), literal-pool float extractor.
- The permuter finds register-allocation-only fixes quickly when the base has <=10 differing words (func_0022B540, func_0022E8C0, func_0022AB10, func_00233178 solved in <2 min); its output often contains junk (`if (x) A else A`, dead temps) that still matches.
SCHEDULER / ARG-SETUP FINDING (not solved, affects func_00230390/00231F5C/002307EC/00230FCC/00231654/00232394 = 4-12 words each)
- The 5-arg call `func_002398F8(D_80145088, o->pawn, D_800D70E8, func_0022A590(D_80145040, o), 1.0f)` is emitted by the ROM as `lui a2;lw a2,D_800D70E8; lui at;lwc1/li.s const; lui a0;addiu a0; swc1 f0,0x10(sp); lw a1,0x5DC(s1); jal; addu a3,v0,zero`
  (the global load first).  Our gcc emits the float chain first (`lwc1; lui a2; lw a2;...`) in every function that contains MORE THAN ONE call that sets $a3.
  With `gcc -dS` (sched1 dump): the move `(set a3 (reg v0))` before the call shows priority 7f000001 ("grouped/boosted") only when $a3 is set exactly once in the whole function
  (same for $a2); with a second a3-setting call anywhere (even `c4(1,2,3,4)` at the end of the function) the boost disappears and the order flips (tested on func_00231BA0:
  0..N extra 4-arg calls -> FL first).  The ROM functions have several a3-setting calls and still show the boosted order, so the original source/RTL must differ in a way I could not find.
  Tools: wip/ordtest.py (detects the order from `gcc -S`), wip/dumpS.sh (sched dump).

NEW LESSONS (m02)
1. Float literals from the pool (extern f32 D_800C....  == .rodata literal words) MUST be written as float literals (f32.py / lit.py print the shortest round-trip text), never as `extern f32`:
   with a literal gcc may hoist/CSE the load across calls and stores (`unchanging` mem) and chk reports ro=; converting alone made func_00231464 EXACT (105 -> 0 words) and
   fixed the clamp/CSE in func_00231654; func_0022E77C also needed a permutation of its 5 independent stores afterwards (wip/swap.py style brute force).
   A real global (D_800D2988 = delta time, D_800CF22C = 400.0, D_800CE758) stays an extern.
2. "ok" booleans built from an inlined predicate: write ONE variable and an else-if chain, each arm assigning ok (`if (c1) ok=0; else if (c2) ok=1; else if (c3) ok=1; else {t=f(); ...; ok=t;}`),
   then `if (ok != 0)`.  Nested ifs with ok pre-set, inline helpers, or `ok = 1;` initialisers all gave bnel/beql delay-slot differences or ok in $v1 instead of $v0
   (func_00230BB8, 002312B8, 00230D94, 00231BA0, 00230620 family: 5 functions).  If the same variable is used in a second, unrelated place use a second variable (ok2) or it lands in a callee-saved reg.
3. ONE float variable reused for three unrelated roles (a/f/v in func_00220D20) puts all of them in the same callee-saved FP reg ($f20) exactly like the ROM; separate variables get f1/f2.
   Same file: the function really returns s32 (`return t;`): without a use of the converted int gcc stores the trunc.w.s result with swc1 directly; with `return t` it goes trunc.w.s/mfc1/sw like the ROM.
   Chained stores `a->x = a->y = a->z = t` fold; re-reading the field (`p->b = p->a; p->c = p->a;`) gives the ROM's extra `addu v1,v0,zero`.
4. A scalar global that is read after stores through a struct pointer and must stay AFTER them (the ROM keeps the lw in source order): declare it as an array/aggregate
   (`extern s32 D_800D750C[]; ... D_800D750C[0]`) -> MEM_IN_STRUCT_P makes it alias the stores (func_0022F1F4 EXACT).
5. Call written in BOTH arms of an if/else (not after the join) is what gives the ROM's cross-jumped `jal; addu` tail with the arg setup in the delay slots (func_0022F5D0, func_00224F38).
6. A gotos-only transcription with the labels in ROM block order reproduced shared `ok = 0` blocks (`goto zero;` blocks) in func_00224C28 / 00232394.
7. while(1)-style loops ... see earlier workers.  `while (p) {...; p = p->next}` vs `for` : try both (func_0022F95C needed `while` with continue-style nested ifs, found by a brute-force over 3x3x3 loop/addressing shapes).
8. Struct locals: `Vec3f` args by value (a1-a3) need the callee prototype with the struct (func_0022F5D0, 0025DE74(id, Vec3f pos, ptr, int)); hidden-pointer struct return: `M2Quat f(...)` returns through a0 (func_0021CF04 312 insns).
9. gcc CSEs the address of a non-lowest stack local (&t at sp+0x50) into a callee-saved register when it is passed to 2+ calls; the ROM re-materialises `addiu a0,sp,0x50` (func_0022B1A8, 0022C480). The CSE only happens when the object is NOT at the lowest frame offset;
   I could not find the source shape that avoids it (locals declared in different order only swap which object is CSE'd).
10. wip/merge.py (alias two same-type locals), wip/swap.py (swap two simple statements), wip/gen.py (spec -> struct with padding): cheap brute-force helpers, one chk call per batch via wip/vv.py.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace m03
FINDINGS - workspace m03 (leftover functions of the main image ROM 0x37464-0x52644; written incrementally)

TOOLING
- wip/ck.sh <fn> (fchk of wip/c/<fn>.c), wip/sd.sh <fn> (side-by-side ours|ROM, marks diffs; python wip/side.py inside WSL), wip/v.py <fn> <variants.txt> (full-file variants separated by //==== in ONE fchk call),
  wip/a.py <fn> (asm incl. `alabel` functions: tools/asmfn.py MISSES functions that start at an alabel, e.g. func_0023B9A0),
  wip/pr.sh/pbatch.sh (decomp-permuter via perm_setup/perm_run, results in ~/perm/m03_<fn>), wip/apply_perm.py <fn> [--write] (shows protos + function of the best permuter output).
- THE PERMUTER IS ALSO CHANGING PROTOTYPE RETURN TYPES AND DECLARATIONS.  When the permuter reports score 0 but chk says N words differ after pasting only the function body,
  diff the *prototype lines* of its source.c: func_002461D8 only matched with `unsigned int func_00243A80(...)` (callee declared as returning a value although the result is unused; `void` gave other temp registers
  for the 12-byte struct copies).  Always try int vs void callee return types for register-allocation-only mismatches.
- the machine is shared (load average 60-80): fchk can exceed the 120 s tool timeout; wrap with `timeout 300`.
- wip/fl.py D_800CXXXX n prints the pool floats as round-trip C literals (shortest repr), wip/f.py prints raw words.

COMPILER FACTS
- gcc 2.8.1 (this build) ALWAYS rewrites `x + 0x80000000` (any form: unsigned, pointer arithmetic, via a variable, +/- spelling) into `subu rd,x,reg` with reg=0x80000000.  The ROM has `addu`.  The only way found to get addu with
  lui 0x8000 is an empty extended asm that hides the constant from cse: `static inline u32 K0(void){u32 k; __asm__("" : "=r"(k) : "0"(0x80000000)); return k;}` then `x + K0()` (func_0023B9A0, two sites).  Marked PINNED in results.txt.
  (`x | 0x80000000` is fine and gives `or`, see func_002567F0.)
- `-DNOFILL` in the chk flags is needed for `sw ... ; jr ra ; nop` leaf functions (struct copy returning dst: func_002390C0; `f32 f(void){return 0.0f;}` = `mtc1 zero,$f0 ; jr ra ; nop`: func_0024E854).
- deferred `inline` functions: the out-of-line copy of a non-static `inline` function is emitted AFTER all other functions of the file, so group objects look like  caller_a caller_b ... helper (func_00244494 group: 4 contiguous
  functions, helper last); for other groups the helper copy sits far away in the module (func_002512C8 inlines func_00254FE8, 0x55BE8): deliver the caller with `static inline`.
- struct return by value (`Quat4 f(Obj*, Vec3f)`): hidden pointer in a0 is kept in a callee-saved reg and the result is copied with lw/sw x4 + `addu v0,s3,zero` at every return (func_0024D49C, func_0024D860).
  A dead partial fill of the local before `q = g(...)` is kept by gcc (func_0024D860).

SOURCE-SHAPE LESSONS (all verified EXACT with chk)
- INLINE FUNCTION WITH OWN LOCALS (biggest win): a `static inline` helper that declares its own arrays and is called from the function reproduces ROM code where `&local` addresses are
  re-materialised (`addiu aN,sp,off`) at every use; the same code written inline in the function (or as a macro) CSEs the address into a callee-saved register.  func_00240D10 and its 5 siblings
  (DF0/ED0/FB0/1090/1170) = 4 vec3 copies + `static inline m03_plane(p)` (= body of func_00240C9C): all EXACT at once, sibling files differ only in the 4 box point indices.
- JUMP THREADING: `for (r = &sym.member; r != NULL; r = r->next) if (hit) break; if (r != NULL) {...}` makes gcc thread the loop-entry `beqz r` straight to the exit; the ROM keeps the second test.
  Fix: copy after the loop, `found = r; if (found != NULL) { ...use found... }` (func_0023C1E0).  m2c shows this as two `if (var_s0 != NULL)` tests.
- `do { ... } while (0);` around a statement range changes the EBB/cse behaviour (also found by peers): in func_0024A790 wrapping one range fixed a callee-saved swap of two pointers
  (score 32 -> 19, one add-operand-order word left).  wip/dowrap.py tries all contiguous ranges at every nesting level in one chk call per 40 variants.
- 12-element `(u32)f` conversions: each cast gets its OWN 2^31 pool entry (ro=...:48 for 12 casts, func_0024CC20); a static inline wrapper adds result copies (265 vs 254 insns), write plain casts.
- Two stack locals written in the else branch only (`w.z = zero; w.x = zero;` with `f32 zero = 0.0f`) give `mtc1 zero,$f0 ; swc1 ; swc1`; a dead Vec3f between used locals matters for the frame layout
  (func_0023EEF0: bounds `f32 c[4]` at sp+0x20, `Vec3f p`, dead `Vec3f w`, then the 0xDC byte hit record).
- A trailing unused local array only exists for the frame size (func_00249E18: `u8 unused[0x14]` between `f32 fl[3]` and the ctx struct).
- Compare operand order decides load order (`v->maxx > a->minx` loads v first) - func_00249E18 / func_0023EEF0.
- `s32 idx` with `(s16)idx` at each use (not an `s16 idx` local) reproduces `sll/sra` at the uses and avoids a copy after the call (func_0023BD24).
- Struct-typed global needed for aliasing: `extern B09List D_80103E20;` (head/tail members) makes `n->next` reload after the store of `D_80103E20.head = n->next` like the ROM; two separate
  pointer scalars let cse reuse the loaded value (func_0023BD24, func_0023C1E0).
- For ROM sequences `mul.s f21,f1,f0 ; ... ; mul.s f21,f21,f0` write the two products as separate statements `k = a*b; k = k*c;` (func_0024C6D8; one expression gives a temp for the first product).
- v.py must use the same flags as ck.sh (-fno-builtin for sqrtf callers); wip/v.py now reads wip/flags.txt.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace m04
- do { ...; return; } while (0) wrapping a block changes EBB/cse behaviour (permuter found it for 2 funcs): stops cse from propagating id==-1 constant into later calls; also fixed list-move register alloc in func_00259938
- result-of-call temp: 'new_var = f(); g(); r = new_var;' (extra copy local between call and the later use) fixed callee-saved allocation in func_0025DF54/E06C (found by permuter)
- addu operand order (base,index vs index,base) is decided by pseudo regno: use a fresh pointer local (r = p + i; r[1]) instead of p[i+1] to get 'addu x,base,idx'
- declaring a callee 's32 f()' instead of 'void f()' (result unused) changes the temp regs picked for struct-copy args (v1,t0,t1 instead of v0,v1,t0): func_002686EC. Try when only the temp register numbers of a struct copy differ.
=== FINDINGS workspace m04 (leftover main-image functions) ===
TOOLING
- tools/fchk.ps1 spends 7 s in rsync of src/ (5 MB on /mnt/d).  wip/f.sh (copy in this workspace; rsync only include/ wip/c wip/hard wip/out) makes a chk call 0.7 s:
  `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -- bash /mnt/d/Projects/ws/m04/wip/f.sh <list>`  (git-bash needs MSYS_NO_PATHCONV or paths are mangled).
  Same tricks for side-by-side (wip/side.sh: ours|rom with `paste -d'|'`), wip/mdiff.py (mnemonic-only diff of the two columns: shows the structural insertions/deletions
  separately from register-number noise), wip/v.py (full-file variants separated by //==== lines, one chk call for all, --save writes the EXACT one), wip/permq.sh (queue of permuter runs) +
  wip/permapply.py (pastes the best permuter output back into wip/c/<fn>.c).
- BEWARE: the permuter pipeline can differ from chk: a permuter 'score 0' output must be re-checked with chk.  func_002686EC: the permuter output had an extra
  `volatile unsigned int` return type on the callee prototype (a random mutation) - the real fix was declaring the callee `s32 f(...)` instead of `void f(...)`.
- python in git-bash: open('/tmp/..') is C:\tmp, not msys /tmp; write temp files under the workspace.
- python text-mode writes CRLF: sed -i 's/\r$//' any .sh you generate from python.
GROUP-OBJECT TARGETS (all 15 of them: func_00251F0C ... func_00256044)
- every wip/groups_ref/*.c file is EXACT as a standalone single-function file once the helper `inline` definitions are `static inline`: no out-of-line copy is emitted
  (verified with nm: only the target symbol), so no combined multi-function object is needed.  Delivered as wip/out/<fn>.c (copies of groups_ref with `static inline`).
CODEGEN LESSONS
- switch layout: case BODIES are laid out in SOURCE order of the labels (func_0025A3EC: ROM has the body of case 2 first, then case 1 -> write `case 2:` before `case 1:`).
  An extra `case 0: break;` (empty, goes to the end) adds the `slti v,2 ; bnez end` range test the ROM has between the ==1 and ==2 compares; without it gcc emits ==1,==2 only.
  Duplicating a short body in two cases (instead of goto to a shared tail) lets gcc cross-jump them into ROM's `j common` layout.
- `if (r == 0) {A} else {B}` vs `if (r != 0) {B} else {A}` decides which arm gets the branch-likely delay-slot copy (`beql v0,zero,end ; swc1`): func_0025A3EC wants the
  single-insn store arm as the ELSE arm.
- `(cond ? K : t) * (cond ? K : t)` (macro MAX(t,K) squared) reproduces the double compare of `c.lt.s ; mov.s ; c.lt.s ; L: nop ; bc1f ; mul ; j ; mul` (func_0025A3EC).
- `if ((o->x = f()) == 0.0f)` avoids the extra `mov.s` copy of the call result that `t = f(); o->x = t; if (t == 0.0f)` produces.
- A local `M04Ctx *c = o->ctx;` then `alSndpSetSound(c->player, c->snd[o->idx]); alSndpSetPan(c->player, x)` gives ROM's `lw v0,idx; lw s0,ctx; ... addiu s0,s0,0x84` (ctx reg
  reused for the player pointer) - do NOT cache `pl = c->player` in a variable before the first use (func_0025A864, 0025AE3C).
- the order `x = *D_a; y = *D_b; *p = 0; call(.., x, y)` (temporaries) makes the loads come before the store that fills the jal delay slot (func_00257380 alHeapInit call).
- `n = f(4) - f(0)` as one expression puts `move a0,zero` of the second call into the first call's delay slot (func_00257380); `n = f(4); n -= f(0)` does not.
- 20-byte struct param + int param: `f(a, b, c, int id, Vec3 pos, Part part)` -> the Vec3 starts on the stack (a3 is taken by id); a call through a function pointer that passes `pos, part`
  on again keeps copies in local temporaries in ROM: `L.p = pos; L.q = part; fn(o, d, k, L.p, L.q)` (func_00265E30, unfinished).
- a bool 'ok' assigned 1 BEFORE two trailing calls is live across them (callee-saved); assign it after the last call to get a caller-saved reg (func_00261690).
- `s16 x = p->f` used both for compares and for `sh` stores produces lhu + extra lh loads; ROM's single `lh` form came from declaring the local s32 (and the callee parameter s32, no sll/sra).
- calls where ROM recomputes 'p + idx*0xCC' for a second access (not CSE'd): spelling the second access as p->v[(s16)idx].f again with idx declared s32 (not s16) reproduces it (func_0025B8DC)
- 'if (a == b) return a; <body>' vs 'if (a != b) { <body>; return i; } return a;': the latter puts the body on the fall-through side and the early exit at the shared epilogue like ROM (func_0025D258); inner if/else arms ordering decides bnel/beql polarity
- a late use-only parameter (copied to a callee-saved reg): put 'lst = last;' as the first statement of the arm that needs it so the 'move s2,a2' lands in the delay slot of the first branch like ROM (func_00259058); a SEPARATE index var for the first (sum) loop vs the second (pick) loop flips which of lo/i gets a0/a1
- node-pointer locals live across a call get kept in callee-saved regs; ROM recomputes q+i*size+off after the call: use small static inline helpers (qinit(&q->nodes[i]), qadd(head,&q->nodes[i].link)) taking the pointer as parameter instead of a local node pointer (func_002596D4); load 'p = head->prev' into a local BEFORE the first store when ROM does the lw first

=================== FINDINGS from workspace m05
FINDINGS from workspace m05 (leftover game functions of the main image)  -- written incrementally

TOOLING
- decomp-permuter (wip/perm_setup.sh / perm_wsl.sh / perm_run.sh / perm_batch.sh copied from b19) is by far the most productive tool for the
  "structure identical, registers/scheduling differ" leftovers.  Three functions of this round were only solved by it
  (func_00290528, func_0028F258, func_0028C810, func_0028FA40).  IMPORTANT: permuter "score" 0 is not required: func_0028F258 and func_0028FA40
  were EXACT at permuter scores 10 and 40 (relocation words are scored differently).  Always splice the best output into your file and run chk
  (wip/psplice.py / wip/pcheck.py do that: they read ~/perm/m05_<fn>/output-*/source.c, keep only the function and re-chk).
- perm_setup needs a glabel in asm/*.s; for functions that sit behind stray words (alabel func_00276D20 inside func_00276D14) write
  wip/perm_target_<fn>.s by hand (glabel/endlabel renamed) and call perm_wsl.sh directly.
- wip/rv.py <base.c> <fn> <rom> <variants.txt> [flags]: one fchk call for N variants given as python lists of (old,new) replacements (or '#FULL' files).
- When using wsl from git-bash always prefix MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 and avoid shell variables inside `bash -c '...'` passed through wsl.exe (they come out empty);
  write helper .sh files instead.  Background jobs started with `&` inside the tool call are killed; use the tool's run_in_background.

WHAT THE PERMUTER FOUND (patterns worth trying by hand first)
- Wrap a block in `do { ... } while (0)` (the original code base uses macro bodies of that shape).  The dummy loop changes cse/loop notes and
  cured: func_00290528 (list unlink macro: hoisted `lui;sw` stayed in place), func_0028F258 (whole frame-swap block).
- A shared zero temp: `int zero = 0; i = zero; best = (T*)zero; if (n > zero)` fixed an s3/s4 allocation swap (func_0028C810).
- A dead local assigned from an address (`tp = cur + 0x10;` never used afterwards while the calls recompute cur+0x10) restores a missing addiu position (func_0028FA40).

GROUP / "INLINE" OBJECTS
- The "group objects" of b19 (func_00282A18/002830A0/0028324C/00284544 with the shared free-object code, and 00283A54/002849C4) do NOT need `inline`
  functions: the shared code is a MACRO (`#define FREE_OBJ(owner,o) if ((o)->flags & 0x100) {...}`) expanded in each caller; func_00284544 is simply
  the macro body as a normal function.  With the macro every function compiles stand-alone, EXACT, no deferred inline emission needed.
  The same macro also gives func_0028279C (kill objects by key, was never attempted) and probably func_0027FF2C.
  func_002849C4 (the velocity-clamp block inlined from func_00283A54) and func_002856B0/00285B34 (struct-by-value inline helpers 002859A0/00285880) did
  need real `inline` functions: see the combined files in wip/out (func_002856B0.c = func_00285880.c = func_002859A0.c = func_00285B34.c).
  gcc 2.8 emits non-static `inline` functions out of line at the END of the file, so a combined file does not reproduce the ROM order of such functions;
  the ROM order (inline helper emitted between ordinary functions) means each ROM neighbour is a separate object or the helper is a macro.

CODEGEN LESSONS
- Ascending clear loops `for (i = 0; i < 10; i++) D[i] = 0;` are reversed and strength-reduced by gcc exactly like the ROM's `li v0,0x24 ... addiu v0,v0,-4`;
  writing the loop descending (`i = 9; i >= 0; i--`) keeps `sll` indexing (func_0028E260, func_0028E0E4: the b20 "open issue" is solved by writing it ascending).
- Sparse type predicates (`t == 2 || t == 0x56 || ...`) inlined in sequence: three `static inline s32 clsX(u16 t) { switch (t) { case ...: return 1; } return 0; }`
  reproduce the compare trees (balanced, with `slti` splits and the 5..7 range) including the value-materialising `li v0,1/0 ; beqz v0` pattern (func_0027B498).
- Vec3 by value in a1..a3 (after an int/ptr a0) with the callee modifying its fields (`v.x = -v.x` via a local temp t: `{ f32 t = v.x; if (t < 0.0f) t = -t; v.x = t; }`)
