  gives the home-slot stores of func_00277198; `x >= C` (not `C <= x`) selects the operand load order (field first, constant second).
- `D_scalar1 < o->f` vs `o->f > D_scalar1`: the C operand order decides which operand is loaded first (lui/lwc1 of the global before or after the field load).
- A single temp reused for a repeated pattern (`y = m[1][k]; m[1][k] = c*y + s*m[2][k]; m[2][k] = -s*y + c*m[2][k];` with the SAME y for all k) matches the ROM's
  register reuse where three distinct temps y0,y1,y2 do not (func_00273860).
- Multiple `if (cmp) goto Z; ... Z: ok = 0;` shapes (m2c prints them as `goto block_9`) are real: the shared block is reached by gotos from two range tests
  and avoids gcc cross-jumping the two identical `bc1tl ; addu v0,zero` tails (func_00276D20).
- A callee returning a struct by value (hidden pointer in a0) places `addiu a0,sp,N` AFTER the compare chain in the ROM; our scheduler hoists it into a bc1t slot (func_002743F0, unresolved).

MORE LESSONS (second half)
- Read the ROM LAYOUT, not only the instruction mix: func_00290E4C matched after (a) writing the 3-way ramp as `if (t<0) v=0; else if (!(16<t)) {..} else v=256` (the
  branch-taken block of `16<t` sits AFTER the multiply block) and (b) spotting that `.L00290FFC: addiu s2,s2,1` is a join label reached from the `bc1f` of an inner `if`,
  i.e. `idx++` belongs after the inner if, not inside it.  A label with an increment right after a call = statement after the inner if.
- A shared pointer variable instead of two (`sp = f(); cnt = *(u16*)sp; sp = sp + 1;` instead of `lst = f(); cnt = *lst; sp = lst + 1;`) cut func_00278090 from 79 to 12
  differing words (ROM: `addu s1,v0,zero ; ... addiu s1,s1,2` in a jal slot).  When the ROM increments a register in place, reuse the variable.
- Swapping the tail statements of a loop (`rec++; i--;` instead of `i--; rec++;`) changes which insn gcc copies into the bc1fl slots (func_00278090).
- Clamp idiom: `if (v >= 256) v = 255; if (v < 0) v = 0;` is NOT what the ROM has in func_002782E4; the ROM's `slti v1,v,256 ; beql v1,zero,Lst ; li v0,255 ; bltzl v,Lst ; move v0,zero ;
  bnel v1,zero,Lnext ; sb ; li v0,255 ; Lst: sb` comes from the 3-test ternary `r = v >= 256 ? 255 : v < 0 ? 0 : v < 256 ? v : 255; col = r;` (redundant third test, reuses the slt result).
  Check small snippets with wip/cs.sh (gcc -S of a test file) to find which C shape yields a given branch pattern: 10 snippets take one call.
- Functions whose f32 stack/register params are used after calls and modified (`sx = sx * W * k; sy = ...`) load them in the prologue: write the arithmetic back into the
  parameters (func_00291FE8: `y = (y*H)/222` also modifies the parameter) instead of using new locals; this also fixes callee-saved FP register counts.
- Display-list push macro `{ M5Dl **pp = &D_80110634; M5Dl *q = (*pp)++; q->w0 = a; q->w1 = b; }` (b19a) reproduces the 7-push bursts of func_00291BF8/00291FE8 down to the
  schedule except for the load order of the first statements.
- The permuter's output can be a long do{...}while(0) around most of a function (func_00273568: everything after the first statement), i.e. the original is macro-heavy.
- gcc places a stack-arg store (`sw v0,16(sp)` for the 5th argument) into the jal delay slot only when it is the last argument-setup insn; ours put it early.  (func_0028C1B0 / 0028C248, unresolved.)
- ZERO-CONSTANT SHARING: in func_00279BB0 the ROM compares x == 0.0 with the SAME register that holds y's initial 0.0 (`mtc1 zero,f1 ; c.eq.s f0,f1`, then y is overwritten by a lwc1 into f1 and a fresh
  `mtc1 zero,f0` is used for the second compare).  Writing `y = 0.0f; if (x == y) { if (t == 0) y = f; if (y == 0.0f) ...}` (use the variable as the constant in the first compare) reproduced it exactly,
  while `if (x == 0.0f && (t != 0 || f == 0.0f))` or `y = 0.0f; if (x == 0.0f)` made cse keep two live zero regs.  Similarly the second half of that function needed fresh locals (px, py) instead of reusing x,y.
- A single float variable used for two successive quantities (s then lim, func_0027E758: `s = lim` after the ternary) pulls the first one into the callee-saved FP register like the ROM; a separate
  local keeps it in a temp.  A DL push as `{ DlCmd **pp = &D_80110634; DlCmd *q = (*pp)++; ...}` fixed the whole tail of that function (60 -> 28 words).

=================== FINDINGS from workspace m06
FINDINGS - workspace m06 (leftover game-code functions; interim, appended as work progresses)

TOOLING / ASSEMBLER
- tools/gas_compat.py: the old assembler NEVER fills a return delay slot with mul.s / mul.d (VR4300 erratum): ROM has
  `mul.s f0,f12,f12 ; jr ra ; nop`.  One-line fix in the BRANCHES condition (`prev in ('mul.s','mul.d')` like LOADS); fixes
  func_0029E8B0 (sqrf) and every `return x*y;` float leaf.  Please merge (patched copy in tools/gas_compat.py of this ws).
- Group files (inline helpers emitted at the end of the object): wip/comb.py + wip/grp.py build one combined .c from the
  per-function pieces and verify every member plus the object LAYOUT (wip/lay.py: symbol offsets vs ROM, delta must be 0).
  Globals that different pieces declare with different types are handled with `extern T D_x_<fn8> __asm__("D_x");` aliases.
- chk's "ro=" for a combined object prints the pool of the whole object (not per function).
- Permuter: a printed score of 10..20 on functions with literal pools / float constants is often relocation-name noise, not a
  real difference: always re-check the permuter's source with chk (func_002A6B3C: score 10 was EXACT; func_0029ABEC 0).
  The permuter's output is ugly; hand-minimise it (usually 1 extra copy / reused variable) before delivering.
- The machine is shared (load average >100): permuter runs of 4-10 minutes got ~5% of a CPU.  Do cheap Cartesian searches
  with wip/gen.py (template + json of alternatives -> ONE chk call) instead and use the permuter only for allocation-only diffs.

LIBRARY RECOGNITION BEATS GOTO TRANSCRIPTION (biggest win of this batch)
- The libc-like chunk at 0x2A0xxx is classic C library code.  Writing the ORIGINAL source (not a transcription of the asm) gave
  EXACT immediately: qsort = 4.3BSD qsort() + qst() (thresh=4*size, mthresh=6*size, median of three; needs `char c` and
  `(u32)(lo/qsz)>>1`), strrchr = MS CRT version (`while (*s++); while (--s != start && *s != (uchar)c);` with UNSIGNED char),
  atof = hand written (sign, int part, '.', fraction/scale, e/E exponent, pow() for |e|>=30; every double literal is its own
  pool entry).  Plain `char` is signed here: use `unsigned char` when the ROM uses lbu on a string.
- A `char c` local assigned from a `char*` deref is loaded with lbu (promoted unsigned), an `s32 c = *p` is loaded with lb.

REGISTER ALLOCATION / COPY TRICKS (found with the permuter, all verified)
- An extra COPY of a value that is used later (`w = v; ... if (w > lim)`) raises the reference count/priority of the original
  pseudo: func_002A6B3C (clamp: v must get f0, 0.0/limit f1): `f32 v = *p; f32 w; w = v; if (v < 0) ...; else if (w > lim)`.
  func_002A0254: `nv = c; t = 1 - nv; ... m11 = tyy + nv; m22 = tzz + nv` (c itself used for the other terms).
  func_0029C3C0: y1 computed into a temp before y2 (`ytmp = p*sy+q; y2 = cy*sz; y1 = ytmp`).
- REUSING an existing variable explains odd registers: exp(): `z = g*g` must be written `y = g*g` (y reused), which moves y to
  f3 (func_0029EB9C, pow func_0029BEA4 which inlines it, tanh); func_0029A778: the else arm `p = e; p->f8 = val;` reuses the loop pointer.
- Local copy after a store: `q = info; *out1 = q->a; *out2 = q->b` stops gcc from reloading `info` (a struct-local) after the first
  store (func_002AB940).
- A local `top = D->top` kept in a callee-saved reg while `D->stk` is re-read at every use (func_00298DBC).
- Declaration order of locals never changed anything (checked again on 300 permutations).

INLINE FUNCTIONS vs MACROS
- A macro that declares/takes the address of locals gets CSE'd (the address stays in one callee-saved register); a `static inline`
  function with its own locals re-materialises `addiu aN,sp,off` at every expansion like the ROM (func_002975B0: 5 plane builds).
- An `inline` (non-static) helper is emitted out of line at the END of the object even when its callers come first; if the ROM has
  the helper before its user, the user must contain a differently named `static inline` copy (asinf/acosf: Asin_ in acosf;
  ceil/fract: Floor_ static inline, floor itself is a normal function).
- func_0026925C-style `D_80110634++` display list pushes: block-local `DlCmd *_g = D_80110634++; _g->w0=..; _g->w1=..;` per command
  (GFX macro) matched func_002A6488 / 002A577C / 002A6170 at once.  A 0xAC byte dead local struct (zero/float init of 7 words at +0x90)
  only exists for the frame size: model it as a struct with a pad[0x90] prefix.

FLOAT CONSTANTS
- When the ROM loads constants through SEPARATE named globals (lui at; lwc1 %lo(D_800Cxxxx)) the constants are extern f32, not
  literals: with literals gcc folds the second of two identical clamps (func_0029D288: Asin's own clamp after the caller's clamp,
  both +-1.0) and hoists the loads above calls (func_002A8650: k = D_800CB0B0 / D_800CB0B4 must be read AFTER the call).
- If a function inlines a library function that has a literal pool (exp inlined in pow) the pool pieces of all copies are
  identical text, gcc cross-jumps the tails (poly copy shared by the even and positive path).

SWITCH / CONTROL FLOW
- A 2-case switch (0,1) with the ROM tree `beq x,1 ; slti x,2 ; beqz ; bnez x` needs an explicit empty `case 2: break;`
  (root of the balanced tree is the middle case).  func_002A8650: `switch (which) { case 1: ..; case 2: ..; default: return; }`
  gives the ROM's `beq which,1; beq which,2` chain with r/g/b constants in the delay slots.
- `if (!(in_box)) cull else draw` (condition negated) gives the ROM layout where the cull arm falls through (func_002A6170).
- Loop head `n = an->n; n--; for (; n != -1; n--)` (separate statements) reproduces the `bne n,-1(reg)` loop (func_002A373C).

OPEN / NOT SOLVED (see results.txt notes): copies that the ROM keeps but cse propagates away (func_002A2FE8 a1=a0 copy in one arm,
func_002A35C0 n=e copy, func_002A152C reload of *q on the loop exit path only), ROM not folding s1 = s2+0x50 inside
`s2-0x70+D-s1` (func_002A8650), texel loaded twice (func_00295B90), FP register numbering of scale pairs (func_002A3478).

LATE ADDITIONS
- Permuter hits delivered: func_002A1C38 (test `*p == '\n'` in the loop while c is still assigned at the bottom) and func_002A7058
  (store unk1C before computing unk20 = k/(unk0-k)).  Both found at score 5/20 and verified EXACT with chk.
- func_0029D984: 300 random statement orders of the 9 matrix stores never beat 21 words; the add/sub operand roles of two product
  terms are swapped vs ROM, suggesting a different source expression (t1/t2 grouping), not an ordering issue.
- Many PARTIAL drafts are within 2-5 words (0029BD38, 0029C278, 0029D6A8, 002A2BE0, 002A13F4, 002AD65C): allocation-only, good
  permuter candidates for whoever has CPU time.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace o01
OVERLAY A (VRAM 0x400000, ROM 0x16E000) FINDINGS  (workspace o01)

Compiler flags: -O2 -mcpu=r4300 (no -fforce-addr). Add -fno-builtin for functions that call memcpy (gcc otherwise emits bcopy) or sqrtf.
  -fno-builtin used by: 004002D8 004042F0 0040458C 00404F58 00405DC0 (and 00404958 attempt).
Overlay data/code keep names D_0040XXXX / func_004XXXXX; chk and ro= pieces (e.g. 0xE18C0:48, 0xE1850:4) work unchanged.

Overlay-specific
- Hidden functions: asm labels can span several real functions (no glabel). Use ONE group file in ROM order, verify each function from it:
  func_00404BE8.c (3 funcs: 404BE8, 404D84, 404DDC), func_00404018.c (404018 + 4041E8).
- Shared header include/o01_types.h (O01Ctx, O01PadTab/O01PadEnt = OSPfsState layout, O01Item, O01Text...). Pak layer uses per-pad arrays D_801534F0/D_80153500/D_80153510.
- ConvName helpers are static inline copies of func_00405648 / func_0040570C bodies; inlining copies reproduces the ROM case-arm "addu v1,v0,zero" patterns (e.g. pick in func_0040A064).
- Struct copies of 0x70 / 0x584 bytes give the 4-word loop only with typedef struct { s32 w[N]; }.

General GCC 2.8.1 lessons
- Jump tables: need enough case labels; write "default: case 0:" (default first) so case 0 aliases default.
- "if (a && b && c && d) return 1; return 0;" gives the branchy boolean form; "x == 0" as a value gives sltiu.
- Early-return "if (x != 3) return -2;" gives the ROM bne layout. Single return vs duplicated epilogue matters; goto join labels avoid epilogue duplication.
- do { ... } while (0) (also do { return x; } while (0);) acts as a scheduling/loop-note barrier; found by permuter for 0040A708, 0040B560, 0040B60C.
- Phi-copy pattern (value computed in both arms into temps, then stored): if (c) {pv=..; pp=0;} else {pv=..; pp=..;} pad.v = pv; plr = pp;  (func_00407290).
- new_var = 0; if (r != new_var) stops folding of redundant r=-1; if (r==0) (func_00404F58).
- Struct return temps: declare in order t then v to get ROM stack slots. One-field struct stays in a register; two-word struct forces a memory local.
- extern const f32 hoists float constants; a local copy forces an early load. Array vs scalar global declaration changes load/store scheduling (func_0040A928).
- Base pointer before count (ents = ...; i = 0; n = ...) then e = &ents[i] gives one giv (func_004030E0). Loops with goto exits lose loop notes (no inversion/hoist).
- Statement expressions ({ ... }) work inside macro args (float clamp in func_0040293C).

Tooling
- decomp-permuter: tools/perm_strip.py must keep static inline helper definitions (patched), else scores are meaningless.
- wip/ad.py: register-insensitive aligned diff; wip/side.py reads wip/flags.txt; wip/vk.py variant runner.

Not matched: see PARTIAL lines in results.txt, best attempts in wip/hard/. Big NONE: 004009F4 00401980 00401D74 00402BA0 004058B0 004069F4 00407748 004082FC 004085E0 00408E1C; fragments 00403E88 (2 insns, j to another label) and 00405DB4 (3 stray insns).

=================== FINDINGS from workspace o02
FINDINGS o02 (overlay A game code, ROM 0x179C2C..0x1856xx, VRAM 0x40BC2C..)
====================================================================
OVERLAY SPECIFIC
- Compiler flags for this part of overlay A are the same as the main game code: gcc 2.8.1 -O2 -mcpu=r4300 (no -fno-builtin needed so far, no -fforce-addr).
  chk/fchk need nothing special: list lines `<path> <fn> <rom hex> -O2 -mcpu=r4300`, ROM offset = VRAM - 0x400000 + 0x16E000 (targets.txt offsets are right).
  Globals are main-image addresses (D_800E28xx, D_8014xxxx, D_8015xxxx) and the chk reloc check resolves D_/func_ names by number, so nothing to do.
- Some targets start with stray junk words before the real function (linker padding with real-looking instructions, e.g. `sub.s`, `beqz`, `slti ; beqz ; jr ; addu`):
  func_0040C638 real start 0x17A648 (4 junk words), func_0040C77C 0x17A780 (1 word), func_0041432C 0x182330 (1 word), func_00415C9C 0x183CA0 (1 word),
  func_00412D5C 0x180D60 (1 word).  Detect: first insn uses an unset register / is not a sane prologue.  The results.txt rom column is the REAL start.
  Tiny fragments that are not functions (3 insns: func_0040CB24, func_00415D04; 1 insn func_0040BC2C) cannot be matched (NONE).
  func_004143D8 (42 insns) is several tiny functions glued together by the splitter (junk + 3 small functions), NONE.
- The overlay contains several objects.  Group file (ONE .c, all functions of the contiguous span in ROM order, each verified from that file):
  wip/out/func_0040ECB0.c = func_0040ECB0, func_0040ED28, func_0040EDB8, func_0040EE64, func_0040EF24, func_0040F004 (tree-node helpers).
  func_0040ED28 is an `inline` (non-static) function: gcc 2.8 emits it AND open-codes it once into func_0040EE64 / func_0040EF24 (that is why those two contain a copy of
  its loop + a real call to func_0040ED28 for the recursion).  This is the only way to get the inline copy: define it first, `inline` keyword, same file.
- The UI/widget code is a tree of nodes {parent +0, next +4, child +8, id s16 +0xC, type u16 +0xE, flags u16 +0x12 ...}; render functions take a 40-byte context
  struct BY VALUE starting in $a1 (O02Ctx): m2c shows it as a1..a3 saved to the stack plus 7 words copied to 0x10..0x28; declare the parameter as a struct of 10 words
  and callees with the same struct, then `E154(&r1,&r2,n,&c.w[0])`-style calls take its address.  Rect = 4 words {x0,x1,y0,y1} and func_002A2898(&x0,&y0,&x1,&y1) argument order.

NEW LESSONS (general)
1. goto-loops: when the ROM loop is NOT rotated (test at top, `j top`, `bnez` back, nops in slots), gcc's loop notes were absent: write the loop with labels and gotos
   (`L1: if (c->type == 2) goto L2; c = c->parent; goto L1;`), while/for always get rotated by this gcc (func_0040F0C4).
2. A search loop whose `found = 1` is hoisted AFTER the entry test (`blez` first, then `li t2,1`) is `i=0; if (n > 0) { found = 1; do { ...; goto done; nx: i++; } while (i < n); } found = 0; done:`
   (func_0040CDD0, func_0040D00C).  Plain for-loops put the li before the blez.
3. Early `return 1` in several places that ROM keeps as separate `j end; li v0,1` blocks (no cross-jumping): write if/else with a return in BOTH arms
   (`if (!p) { return 1; } else { ...; return 0; }`), a plain `if (!p) return 1;` gets cross-jumped (func_00413500).
4. Switch compare tree: ROM `beq 1; slti <2 -> default; beq 2; beq 3` = cases 1,2,3 with an explicit `case 0: default:` FIRST in the source (func_0040C43C).  Without the
   `case 0:` label gcc builds the balanced tree (2 first).
5. Stores through casted pointers vs struct members: struct-member stores are `MEM_IN_STRUCT` and never invalidate a cached global pointer (`D_80153C28`), so gcc hoists/CSEs
   the pointer reload.  The ROM reloaded the global pointer after the store and kept the store BEFORE the reload: write the store as raw pointer arithmetic
   `*(u16 *)((u8 *)e + k*0x2C + 8)` / `*(s32 *)(...)` (func_0040F600, 004104C8: EXACT, struct form was 4 words off).  Also `*(u16 *)&D[i] -= 1` followed by an s16 read of D[i]
   forces the reload (func_00411E98).
6. Struct globals spread over several symbols: when the asm addresses `lh -0x48(a0)` from an address-of reg, the two symbols are one struct (`D_80153C0C.n`, `.mode` at +0x48;
   `D_80153C20.n` / `.mode` at +0x34; D_80153C40 / D_80153C54 at +0x14; D_801539B0 is a 0x2A8-byte struct with many pointer fields).
7. Local copy of a parameter (`S *q = p;`) fixes register choice (func_00412D20, 004126B4 found by the permuter); `a = c = 0` chained stores change store order (func_0040E914);
   `i * 8 + (s32)ptr` (index first) flips the addu operand order (func_00411B70); separate `u32 v` for the OR chain moves the result register (func_00413D60).
8. Struct copy of a struct containing only u8 pad[] is emitted as lwl/lwr (unaligned): give the struct an s32 member (func_00412790).
9. Function pointers called through globals: `fn = D_x; ...; fn(arg)` ordering is sensitive; the permuter helped (do { } while (0) wrapper around the statement group, func_00413D60).
10. Colour/bit-field pack/unpack helpers (func_00413E88, 00413F60, 00413A60, 00413D60, 00413BD4, 00412E14): unsigned operands (u32) for srlv, `s < 0 ? (c >> -s) & m : (c << s) & m` with the
    mask applied inside each arm (mask load duplicated in both arms), `if (s < 0)` arm first.
11. decomp-permuter works for overlay functions with `wip/perm_setup.sh` copied from another worker (uses PERM_FLAGS -O2 -mcpu=r4300); solved func_0040F1E0 (`sum++; sum--` perturbation),
    func_004126B4 (param copy).  Bases above ~500 score did not converge.
12. Inlined by-value struct params: func_0040DB84 (type dispatcher, EXACT, 372 insns) contains the bodies of func_0040C638 / 0040F3D0 / 0040F2B0 INLINED: define them as `static inline`
    helpers taking the same by-value context struct before the dispatcher and call them from the switch; gcc then copies the struct into a local (`lw/sw` x 10 words) exactly like the ROM.
    Local order of the rects in the inline body had to be `r40, r30, r50, r60` (declaration order = ascending stack address for the first-declared being LOWEST... check by trial, 6 permutations).
    The jump table of the dispatcher is reported by chk as ro=0xE1CB0:32 (8 words, main-image .data).
13. Boolean value chains: `v = (a != x) ? 1 : (b != y) ? 1 : (c != z) ? 1 : (d != w);` reproduces `bne ..; li v0,1` slots + final `xor/sltu` (func_0040D350); a plain `||` chain or nested ifs do not.
14. Structure copy `S tmp = D_800E2A10[i];` of a 20-byte table row gives lw t0-t3 + lw (func_0040BC30); an extra local alias `s32 *p = e->w;` found by the permuter fixed the arg registers.
15. Text-batch builder func_0040D350 (525 insns) and func_0040E154 (362) are PARTIAL (see wip/hard): remaining diffs are FP callee-saved register choice (fx/fy f20/f21) and the argument-register copies.
16. Several data tables are shared across objects: functions that look at `D_80153C54`, `D_80153C0C`, `D_80153C20`, `D_80153C40`, `D_801539B0..D_80153C3C` use them either as scalars (lui/lw each time) or as
    fields of one struct (address-of base + offsets): decide by the asm: `lh -0x48(a0)` from a register holding &D_80153C54 means ONE struct (D_80153C0C.n at +0, .mode at +0x48).
17. `volatile` POINTER globals reproduce ROM reloads: when the ROM re-reads a global table pointer (`lui;lw D_80153C48`) between statements although nothing could have changed it,
    declare the pointer `T * volatile D_xxx` (func_00410E9C needed `S_e * volatile D_80153C18` + a `do { } while (0)` around one store and the index-first form `(S_e *)(i*8 + (s32)ptr)`;
    func_0041174C needed `* volatile D_80153C48` plus the store written as `*(s32 *)&D_80153C48[i].p = (s32)func_00252FFC(...)`).  Mini experiments with tools/gcc show the effect:
    (a) struct-member stores never invalidate a cached global pointer, (b) `*(u16 *)&P[i] -= 1` on an `s16 *P` does (func_00411964), (c) volatile pointer => reload at every use.
18. Two separate loop variables (`i`, `j`) instead of re-using `i` in a second loop changed the register roles exactly as in the ROM (func_0041023C: s0/s1 swap).
19. Index form vs pointer-walk: `for (; i < n; i++) sx += g->colw[i];` (gcc makes the strength-reduced pointer a NEW pseudo) matched func_0041234C and func_00412FD4-style code where
    a user pointer variable `p = g->colw; *p++` gave swapped count/pointer registers.  Also `sy = sx = 0;` (copy direction) and `i = 0; if (i < n) {...}` (instead of `n > 0`, gives `slt` with the
    zero register) mattered there; `c = col;` placed inside the `if` arm lets gcc move the copy into the branch delay slot.
20. If/else assignment to two globals from a ternary: `D_80153C70 = cond ? a : b;` vs `if (cond) D_x = a; else D_x = b;` changes which register holds the value (func_00412FD4 needed the if/else form).
21. Dead-looking `D_800E32E4 = 0; D_800E32E4 = 1;` (two consecutive stores) in func_004171B8 means that variable is volatile in the original; making it volatile moved func_004171B8
    from 139 to 141 of 145 insns but the first display-list push (absolute lui/lw/sw of D_80110634 instead of a base register) is still unresolved in func_004171B8, func_004173FC and func_00416D74.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace o03
FINDINGS from workspace o03 (overlay A, ROM 0x16E000.., VRAM 0x400000..; menu / controller-pak / UI classes)

== OVERLAY SPECIFICS (read first) ==
1. Compiler flags: gcc 2.8.1 `-O2 -mcpu=r4300` exactly like the main game code (no -fno-builtin needed: cosf via `jal` matched, memset/strcpy are
   ordinary calls).  chk/fchk lines just take the ROM offset (VRAM - 0x400000 + 0x16E000); no special overlay handling needed.  Calls into the main
   image keep func_00XXXXXX names, overlay-internal ones func_004XXXXX.  Several functions that looked "frame-less/unbalanced" were just misaligned
   (see 2) -- I found NO sign of a different compiler/mode for the overlay.
2. PADDING JUNK BEFORE FUNCTIONS: the bytes between overlay functions are NOT zeros but leftover code words (fragments like `jr ra ; addiu sp,sp,0x18`,
   `j .L...`, `addiu v0,zero,2`, `bne v1,at,..`, a stray `jal`).  The glabel of func_0041A0DC / 00419B8C / 0041DFB4 / 00421B18 / 00422258 / 0042250C
   sits 1-3 words BEFORE the real entry (confirmed because the function-pointer tables in the main-image .rodata, e.g. D_800E3300.. / D_800E39C4..,
   contain the 16-aligned real entry address: 0x41A0E0, 0x419B90, 0x41DFC0, 0x421B20, 0x422260, 0x422510).  chk those at the real entry (results.txt
   lists the real rom offset and says how many stray words precede it; treat the stray words like the existing `pad_xxxx` words in funcs_a.txt).
   Pure fragments (NOT functions): func_004194A8, func_00419758, func_0041C67C, func_0041F1AC, func_0042204C -> NONE.
   A function that "has an unbalanced frame / missing epilogue / a call before the prologue" is almost surely this; re-check at the aligned entry.
3. Float literals, strings and jump tables of the overlay live in the MAIN image .rodata (VRAM 0x800E1440..0x800E3A00, ROM = VRAM-0x80000000+0xC00).
   They are ordinary per-function literal pools: write real f32 literals (0.01f, 1.2f, 45.0f, 1.333333f, 0.25500003f: use tools to print the shortest
   round-tripping literal, wip/fl.py), real string literals ("%d", "%ld") and plain `switch` for jump tables (chk prints ro=).  Equal values are NOT
   merged (1.0f appears three times as three pool entries).  u32 <- f32 conversion `(u32)(x*0.255f)` produces the `c.le.s / sub.s 2^31 / or 0x80000000`
   idiom + a 2^31 literal in the pool (use it for `alpha = (u32)f`).
4. Code structure: the overlay is a UI/menu framework.  Objects are "widgets" with a header (+0x04 next, +0x08 child, +0x0C s16 id, +0x0E u16 class,
   +0x10 u8 alpha, +0x12 flags) and classes registered with func_002992C4(classId, dispatchFn, stubFn, 3).  Every class has the same 36-insn dispatcher
   `for (i = 0; T[i].fn != 0; i++) if (T[i].msg == msg && (T[i].type == obj->id || T[i].type == 0x7530)) return T[i].fn(obj,msg,a2,a3,a4);`
   over a table of 12-byte {msg,type,fn} entries (14 identical functions: only the table symbol differs -> template), a varargs stub
   `void f(s32 a, s32 b, ...) {}` (stores a1..a3), a free thunk `func_00254784(self); return 0;` and constructors that struct-copy a 0x2C-byte template
   node (`*(O3Blk11 *)o = *(O3Blk11 *)src; o->col = src->col;`).  Pages (menu screens) are globals D_800E3518 / 3590 / 39C0 / 42D0 / 4400 / 4450 / 44A0 / 4510 ..
5. Several display-list helpers inline the same render-state setters as func_00419074/144 with constants (func_00418E60): they are `static inline`/macro
   originals, I wrote them as macros.

== SOURCE-SHAPE LESSONS (new) ==
6. STORE-THEN-LOAD ORDER = ALIASING.  When the ROM has `sw v0,0x14(v1)` BEFORE `lw a1,sym` (and `addu a0,v0,zero` last in the jal delay slot) but gcc
   hoists the load of a plain scalar global above the store, the loaded object must be an AGGREGATE MEMBER in the original: declare the text pointers as
   `extern char *D_800D749C[];` and use `D_800D749C[0]` (one-element arrays, each used once keeps one lui per use), and read the stored page field back
   through memory (`D->w[3] = ctor(); f(D->w[3], str);` -- cse forwards the value; a temp `t` or an argument-assignment `f(D->w[3] = ctor(), ..)` does not
   match).  Also read config fields through the struct (`D_801462C8->f10`), not through `*(s32*)((u8*)p+0x10)`, otherwise the load is scheduled early.
   (func_0042362C, 0042250C, 00420E90.)
7. "Early return" shape: ROM `bne ...,Lend ; addu v0,zero,zero` (return value in the branch delay slot) = `if (cond != X) return 0; ...; return 0;`
   (func_0041B078, 0041BBBC family, 0041C48C, 0041EE78).  `if (x == 0) return 1; return y == 0;` for the `return 1` in the delay slot (func_00419FB8).
8. Tiny if/else-if on a state with sound ids -> `switch (v) {case 1: f(0xE77); break; case 2: f(0xE76); break;}` (func_0041A4B0): the plain if chain
   gives a different branch layout.  switch with `default: return 0;` inside + `return 0` after (func_00421D18).  Case ORDER decides the compare tree
   (func_00423488: ascending 0x50,0x57,0x58).  A classification like {-4,-3,-1,0} written as an explicit if-chain with `return` inside (not switch) matches
   the loop (func_0041C21C), whereas inside a bigger function a `switch` with the same labels matched (func_0041BF10).
9. K&R / pass-through args: callee with no arguments set up before `jal` -> declare `void f();` and (if the ROM sets four registers) call with the extra
   args (func_00419ED4: `func_00419D04(o, -1, g, 0xB62)`); pass-through of $a1/$a2 means an extra unused parameter (func_00419FD8(o, n), 0041ADB4(list, src)).
10. Local temporaries to reproduce register/ordering choices (permuter style tricks that worked by hand): hoist `t = pts[i]/10; ... ` before the first
    store (func_0041E88C), `p = &pts[rand() % n]` pointer local, `O3Cam *c = g->p;` loaded once (func_004220D8), `s32 k = n - 1; if (n <= 0) ... D[b][k]`
    (inline of func_0041EBF8), separate loop counters for separate loops (func_0041E790).  `-(RND(40)+30) + base` instead of `base - (RND(40)+30)` stops
    gcc re-associating and moving the base load (func_00419D04).  A `do { ... } while (0);` wrapper around the tail of a function changed the schedule to
    the ROM's (found by decomp-permuter, func_004220D8).
11. Per-class structs by value: 40-byte transform block `O3Xf {s32 w[10];}` passed by value (a1..a3 + 28 bytes on the stack) and forwarded with `&x` of the
    first home slot (func_0041A834 family, 5 identical paint functions).  12-byte Vec3 structs by value in a1..a3 / on the stack (func_0041DC3C).
12. Switch jump tables inside loops are strength-reduced into a walking table pointer (`s1 += 4`): do not write the pointer, write `switch (i)`.
13. Statement order matters for the scheduler only through memory dependences: when the ROM stores field A before loading field B (`lh v1,0x18(v0)` first),
    put the load statement first (func_0041A600: `o->width = g->w;` before `o->max = max;`).
14. Tools: wip/vv.py (variant files separated by //====, one chk call), wip/side.sh (side-by-side, honours wip/entries.txt for shifted entries),
    wip/cc.sh (raw gcc asm of a wip/*.c: use it for micro experiments like #6), wip/perm_setup.sh/perm_run.sh/perm_batch.sh (decomp-permuter; SKIP=n env
    drops n stray leading words of the target).  Permuter hits: func_004220D8 (zero in <10 min); near misses of 10-70 points often need > 15 min.

15. INLINE HELPERS: three display-list render-state functions (func_00419224 PipeSyncOnce, func_00419074 SetModeA, func_00419144 SetModeB) are `inline`
    functions of the original (their out-of-line copies are the ROM functions).  When such a helper is inlined into a caller, the inlined copy's
    D_80110634 accesses compile NON-base (`lui v0 ; lw v0,%lo(sym)(v0)` ... `lui at ; sw`, with the `addu v1,v0,zero ; addiu v0,v0,8` post-increment
    copy), while ordinary `D_80110634++` code in the same function gets the base-register form (`lui a0 ; addiu a0,%lo`).  This is how func_00419074/144
    (first block non-base, mode blocks base) and func_00418E60 (everything non-base) are written: `static inline void O3_Sync()/O3_SetModeA(m)/O3_SetModeB(m)`
    in include/o03_types.h, the ROM functions call O3_Sync() and keep their own `O3_PUSH` for the mode arms; `switch (mode) {case 6: ..; case 7: ..}`.
    Static inline helpers that get fully inlined are not emitted, so they do not violate the "no extra ROM functions" rule.  Tell: a ROM function that
    contains the body of another ROM function with constants folded in (func_00418E60 contains 00419074(7) and 00419144(0xE)).
16. chk flag `-DNOFILL` is needed for functions that end `sw ; jr ra ; nop` (func_0041CE20/4C: struct-by-value setters).
17. SPANS WITH HIDDEN FUNCTIONS: `func_0041CB48` (206 insns) is really a 182-insn function followed by three tiny methods at rom 0x18AE20 / 0x18AE4C /
    0x18AE78 (Vec3 setters taking the vector by value: `a->v480 = v;`); `func_0041CE88` (434) is an 8-insn function (`o->f274 = 0xFFFF; func_0041C680(o);`),
    two junk words (`jal func_0041D9D0 ; nop`) and the big page constructor at rom 0x18AEB0 (424 insns, listed as func_0041CEB0 in results.txt).
    Check `jr ra` followed by code that no earlier branch targets before attempting a "big" target.
18. Parameters spilled to their home slots (`sw a1,0x5C(sp) ... lw t0,0x5C(sp)`) in func_0041C680 = register pressure (9 callee-saved regs live): keep a local
    `lv = D_8011FE88` base and `found`/`i`/`n`/`e` locals, `i` as plain s32 (an s16 loop counter adds sll/sra).

=================== FINDINGS from workspace o04
FINDINGS o04 (overlay A game code, ROM 0x191000..0x19D000, VRAM 0x4237C0..0x42F12C)
=====================================================================================
OVERLAY-SPECIFIC
- Compiler/flags: the overlay A game code is gcc 2.8.1 -O2 -mcpu=r4300 (same as the main game code, "game" mode of
  chk/fchk).  A few functions that call memset/memcpy need -fno-builtin (otherwise gcc inlines the copy): see results.txt
  (flags=...).  chk handles overlay addresses fine: list lines take the ROM offset (rom = VRAM - 0x400000 + 0x16E000);
  `D_xxx` / `func_004xxxxx` relocation addresses are checked numerically.  chk's `ro=` address is computed with the main
  image mapping (+0xC00) and is only trustworthy for jump tables of the main-image data (e.g. ro=0xE2478:232 is the right
  jtbl_800E1878 ROM address); for overlay-internal literals it prints garbage.
- STRAY WORDS BEFORE A FUNCTION: several "functions" in targets.txt start with 1-3 words that do NOT belong to them
  (they look like trailing/unreachable code of the preceding function).  The real entry is where the data tables point
  (search the ROM for the BE word of the address): func_0042DBC8 (real 0x42DBD0, 2 stray words), func_004293AC (0x4293B0),
  func_004264E4 (0x4264F0, 3 stray words: jal/addu/beql), func_00429178 (0x429180, 2 stray words), func_00423C18
  (0x423C20), func_00429C04 (0x429C10), func_0042F12C (0x42F130).  Those functions are matched from the real entry via
  wip/rom_override.txt (results.txt prints the real ROM offset); the stray words need to stay as asm/.word data.
- Menu "mode" functions follow a fixed pattern: (state ptr D_800E4xxx) + event handlers `s32 f(a,b,c,d)` where
  `(c >> 16) == 3` tests the event class and `d` is the id; dispatchers over tables {ev,key,fn} stride 0xC with the
  0x7530 wildcard (9 identical copies, only the table symbol differs).
GENERAL (new lessons, confirmed on many functions)
- "return 0" shape: when ROM has `addu v0,zero,zero` in the DELAY SLOT of the first branch (or right before lw ra), the C
  is the early-return form `if (!cond) return 0; ...; return 0;` (the `if (c) { ... } return 0;` form puts it after).
- Cross-jump: two arms that end with the same call followed by shared code are written with the call in BOTH arms
  (`if (c) f(x); else f(y);` -> gcc cross-jumps to one jal, argument setup stays in the arms).  Fixed 5 register-allocation
  mismatches (func_0042B1C0, func_0042AB70, func_0042ECF0 family, 00427F50/004280F0 call per switch arm).
- A switch with a result that falls through to `jr; nop`: use `switch ... { case: return K; }` with the case ORDER = block
  order of the ROM (blocks follow source order); a `r = 0; ... return r` form changes the layout.
- Switch tables: case labels that share a block in the jump table are written `case a: case b:`; a table with MORE entries
  than the highest used case means an explicit empty `case N: break;` (func_00425854: case 18).  Read the table words from
  ROM (VRAM 0x800Exxxx -> ROM = VA - 0x80000000 + 0xC00) to map case values to blocks; scripts that generate the switch
  from the table + asm blocks matched 3 huge functions (func_004255A8, func_00424FC8, func_004251F4 partly).
- Strength reduction: `p = &D_x[i]` INSIDE the loop (or `c = (u8*)sym;` with explicit byte indexing `c[0x11C + row + i]`)
  reproduces ROM loops that keep the symbol base in a callee-saved register (func_0042DDE4, func_0042D2F8, func_004285EC).
  Operand order of `base + idx*0x96` follows pseudo numbers: declare/compute `row` first.
- `D_80154024`-style neighbours: when the asm addresses D_X and D_X-4 from one register use `s32 *p = &D_X; p[0], p[-1]`
  (assigned where the ROM computes it; not at the declaration if the lui comes late).
- `lui at; sb sym` stores: when a global byte is part of a big struct (e.g. D_801462D5 == D_801468A0 - 0x5CB or
  D_80146398 - 0xC3) the ROM addresses it relative to the OTHER base register: write `((u8 *)D_801468A0)[-0x5CB]`.
- Loop var whose address is passed to a callee stays on the stack (`lw v0,0x10(sp)` ... func(&i)): keep `s32 i;` + `&i`.
- Locals initialised with {..} become .rodata copies (chk prints ro=): ROM used individual stores -> assign elements one by one.
- f32 args after an int: prototype with f32; `(f32)u8` / `(f32)s8` conversions as expected; `(u32)f` store into a byte
  gives the c.le.s/sub.s/0x80000000 sequence.
- `D_x = D_x + 1` vs `D_x[0] = D_x[0] + 1`: the array form keeps the address in a register (func_0042AEB8).
- Permuter (tools/perm_setup.sh + decomp-permuter) worked well for pure register/scheduling residue: wip/perm.sh <fn> <file>
  <seconds> runs it; found func_00426454 (`r = 1` after the early return), func_0042F014 (`if (D) return A; else return A;`
  duplicate return), func_004283F4 (row = i*0x96 inside the inner loop), func_0042B824.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace o05
FINDINGS workspace o05 (overlay A game code, ROM 0x19D988..0x1A9..., VRAM 0x42F988..0x43B3xx; menu / lobby / "mode" code)
Totals: 177 targets: 146 EXACT, 16 PARTIAL (drafts in wip/hard), 15 NONE (stray padding 1, 4 too large, rest not matched); plus
func_00438BA4 (12 insns, no target of its own, sits at the end of the ROM span of func_00438A08) = EXACT.

== OVERLAY-SPECIFIC ==
1. Compiler flags of overlay A game code = the main game flags: gcc 2.8.1 `-O2 -mcpu=r4300`.  No -fno-builtin needed (memset() with a constant size is a real call,
   cosf is a plain jal).  chk takes the overlay ROM offset directly (rom = VRAM - 0x400000 + 0x16E000, the offsets in targets.txt); reloc symbols D_800xxxxx /
   func_004xxxxx / func_00xxxxxx resolve normally.  Jump tables are placed in the shared .rodata (chk prints ro=0x...:N for them), no extra work.
2. Overlay layout facts: every function starts 8-aligned (some 16); several targets start with 1-3 junk words (tail of the previous object's alignment padding):
   func_00435CE4 (+0xC), 004374E4 (+0xC), 004366C4 (+0xC), 00439F0C (+4), 004390EC (+4), 004396BC (+4).  Use the real entry (first `addiu sp`) in the chk line.
   func_004399C8 is two stray words (not a function).  func_00438A08 is followed directly by a second, 4-aligned function func_00438BA4 inside the same target span.
3. Return with a struct copy as the last statement (Vec3 setters/getters func_00439DC0, 00439DEC, 00439E10, 00439E3C, 00439EBC, 00439EE0): ROM has `sw ; jr ra ; nop`
   (delay slot NOT filled).  Our chk only reproduces this with the extra flag token `-DNOFILL` in the list line (tools/gas_compat.py NOFILL).  Report: those 6 need flags=-DNOFILL.
4. These modes share ONE big heap block per menu, reached through a global pointer (D_800E54A4 lobby: header 0x58 bytes, 4 player records of 0xB68 at +0x58, 4 slots of
   12 bytes at +0x2DF8; D_800E59E0 second menu: header 8, 4 rows of 0x4D0 at +8; D_800E5830, D_800E5690, ... widget-info blocks).  Record-relative field offsets are what the C
   code uses: declare the record array at its REAL base (`O05Plr plr[4]` after a 0x58-byte header) and write `D->plr[i].field` EVERYWHERE (macros / `(u8*)D + i*0xB68 + 0x58`
   style casts never gave the ROM's `addu a0,L,off` operand order).  With the real struct the operand order, the folded 0x58 in the load offsets and the register use come
   out exactly (func_00430120 went from 8 words off to EXACT just by changing `(u8*)D + idx*0xB68 + off` into `D->plr[idx].f`).  If an access offset overshoots the record size
   (0xBBC with stride 0xB68) the base is wrong (records start after a header).
5. Event handlers ("Mode_DispatchEvent": func_00435C54, 00435F70, 0043630C, 00436634, 00436FE4, 00437454, 00437868, 00437B98, 0043905C, 00439390, 0043962C, 00439938, 0043A014)
   are 36-insn copies of one function over different 12-byte {id,key,fn} tables:
       for (i = 0; T[i].fn != 0; i++) if (T[i].id == id && (T[i].key == o->key(s16 @+0xC) || T[i].key == 0x7530)) return T[i].fn(o, id, a2, a3, a4);  return 0;
   (5 args: the 5th goes through the stack: `lw t3,0x30(sp)` ... `sw t3,0x10(sp)`).  The two index registers (byte offset t0 for id/key, pointer t1 for fn) come out of `T[i].x` indexing.
6. Float literals in the overlay are in the same pool as the strings and are real LITERALS in the original (0.005f, 100.0f, 150.0f, 0.0033333334f, 0.3f, 0.009f, 3.0f, -5.0f, -100.0f,
   -30.0f, 255.0f), not externs: with extern f32 globals gcc reloads them each time, with literals the loop-invariant ones sit in callee-saved FP regs (func_00433574 saves f20-f23).
   The u32 conversion adds the 2^31 literal to the pool ((u32)f -> `c.le.s ... sub.s ... or 0x80000000`); chk shows ro=...:16 for 3 literals + that one.
   Format strings: literal strings `"%d"`, `"%d%%"`, `"%d.%s.%s"` (the ROM's .rodata order is strings first in the function's own piece).

== SOURCE-SHAPE <-> CODEGEN LESSONS (new) ==
7. Shared epilogue vs early return: if the ROM puts `addu v0,zero,zero` before `lw ra` for ALL exits but the early exits carry `addu v0,zero` in their own delay slots, write
   nested ifs / `break` out of a switch and ONE final `return 0` (func_00435560, func_00432334, func_00438F74, func_00437790).  If the early exits jump to a label AFTER the
   result copy, use `return 0;` inside the arms.  Mixing both in one function is normal: each switch arm either `break`s (shared tail) or `return 0`s (own delay-slot copy).
7b. (found late, applies to 12 functions) Strength-reduced pointer/offset induction registers: write the access as it is in the original data layout and let gcc make the givs.
   a) The 150-byte records D_80146410[] / D_801462C8 stuff / D_801462D5 are all members of ONE global state struct at D_801462C8 (mode byte +0xD, two flag bytes +0x24/+0x25, records
      +0x148 stride 0x96, D_80146398 = +0xD0, D_801462E0/E1 = +0x18/+0x19).  Declare it `extern O05GameState D_801462C8[];` and write `D_801462C8->rec[i].b0`: gcc then keeps ONE base
      register walking by 0x96 with `lbu 0x148(s1)`; `u8 *q = D_801462C8; q[0x148]` or two separate symbols give a different prologue/giv order (func_00434CE4, 00434E08, 004396BC).
   b) Order of local declarations WITH initialisers decides the order in which the loop's givs are initialised in the preheader: func_004357C4 only matched with
      `s32 a = D->slot[i].a; s32 b = D->slot[i].b; O05Rec *rec = &D_80102B00[i];` (rec declared last) -- the same statements with rec first gave the ROM's order of `lui s1` / `addu s2`
      reversed.  Try both orders whenever a prologue/preheader differs only in the order of two `addu/lui` initialisations.
   c) A constant used as an ARGUMENT inside a loop (copy size 0x190, "%d", 0xAF, 1) is kept in a callee-saved register by the ROM only when it is an explicit local
      (`s32 sz = 0x190;` func_004349A8, func_00434CE4; `u8 col = 0xAF; char *fmt = "%d"` func_0043847C; `s32 one = 1` func_00438A08).
   d) Assignment as call argument keeps two names for one value in separate registers: `func_00433DA8(idx = -1)` (func_00434B58 found by decomp-permuter) -- the second copy of a value
      that the ROM keeps in two registers (`addu s1,s0,zero` after the loop) is usually a dead/live separate variable assigned from the first.
   e) A pointer whose register number the ROM puts LOWER than a loop counter: declare the loop counter assignment first (`i = 7; p = g + 0x172A;`) (func_004396BC).
   f) Semantics first: when a branch delay slot contains `addiu s1,s1,-1` / `+1` for BOTH outcomes of the surrounding `bne`, the update is unconditional in the source
      (`s1--; if (flag == 1 && s1 >= 3) s1 = 2;`) -- func_0043AED0 stayed 38 words off until the statement was moved out of the if.
   g) Table accesses `lui at; addu at,at,s0; lhu a1,lo(tab)(at)` with s0 = 76*k + 4*i: declare the table as an array of 76-byte rows (`typedef struct { u16 w[38]; } Row;
      extern Row T[]; T[k].w[i*2]`) so the byte offset (k*76 + i*4) is formed with k*76 first (operand order of the final addu).
   h) A switch with only 2 explicit cases but ROM tree `beq 1 / slti 2 / bnel 0` has a hidden 3rd label: add `case 2:` merged with default (func_0043B2AC, func_00433574).
8. switch case-body ORDER = ROM text order of the bodies, independent of the value order (func_00437790: bodies 13C, 13B, 13A; func_00436224: case 0x3D5 before 0x3D3;
   func_0042FD28: bodies 6,4,0,3,1,2).  An `if (e==A) .. else if (e==B)` chain gave different layouts than `switch` (func_004379F0, func_00435EB8).  Cases that all go to the
   end label are DROPPED by gcc and shrink the jump-table range: ROM `sltiu v0,v1,8` with only cases 0..6 present needs `case 7: break;` AND `case 5: break;` written BEFORE
   the first real case label (func_0042FD28; putting them last does nothing).  `case 3: case 4: default: x` also keeps the table range 5 (func_00436224, 00439F38).
9. Inner 3-way compare trees `beq 1; slti <2; beql ...; bnel 0` need a 3rd (empty) case label (func_00433574 `case 2: break;`).
10. By-value Vec3 args/returns/members: `O05Vec3` struct param after an int gives the sw a1,a2,a3-to-home-slots + lw/sw copy idiom; a Vec3 returned by value takes a0 as hidden pointer.
    A struct with Vec3 locals built from float literals: `a.z = a.y = a.x = 0.3f;` stores x,y,z in that order from ONE literal (func_004387F4); `a.x = a.y = a.z` stores z first.
11. A callee that takes NO arguments but is called after `a0` happens to hold something: declare it `s32 f();` (K&R) / call without args; otherwise gcc puts the arg move
    into the jal delay slot twice (func_00435D14: ROM has `bnez v0,L; addu a0,s0` and `jal func_0042B108; nop`).
12. `x - 1` clamp written by the original as the odd form  `y = f; x = y - 1; f = x >= 0 ? y - (y >= x) : 0;`  produces the ROM's `bltz; slt; xori 1; subu`
    sequence (func_0042FAFC case 4): do NOT write the natural `f > 0 ? f-1 : 0` (gives blez/addiu).  It must be stored through a local pointer `p = &D->h; p->f50 = ...`
    (a store through D_800E54A4 again would reload the global in between).
13. `s32 t = f(); if (t != G[1]) { G[1] = t; ... }` vs `prev = G[1]; G[1] = t; if (t != prev)`: ROM `beq; nop; sb` = store inside the if (func_004394E8); ROM `beq; sb(slot)` = unconditional store
    (func_004391F8 first test only).
14. Loop `for (i=0;i<4;i++){ if (r != -1) break; if (x[i] == -1) r = i; }` (not `for (...; r == -1 && i < 4; ...)`) reproduces the first-iteration test `bne r,-1` without an entry
    `slti` (func_004353C0 family).  The element address `base + i*12 + 0x2DF8` must be written `*(s32*)((u8*)D + i*12 + 0x2DF8)` or `&D->slot[i]` -> gcc folds the constant into the load offset only
    in the first form (struct-array form adds 0x2DF8 into the pointer first, ROM keeps it in the offset): `(u8*)D + i*12` as base with an O05SlotAt{pad[0x2DF8]; Slot s;} cast.
15. Constants kept in callee-saved registers across calls (0xAF in s5, "%d" address in s4, scaled index sel*4 in s3, 1 in s3, &D_800D15F0 in s0): in the ROM they come from explicit
    LOCAL VARIABLES assigned once (`u8 col = 0xAF; char *fmt = "%d"; f32 *pf = &D_800D15F0; s32 one = 1;`), assigned right where the ROM materialises them (after the early-return
    checks!).  Using the literal at every use rematerialises it each time (func_0043847C, func_00438A08).  Widget temp kept in a second callee-saved copy (`w6 = w;`) is also source-level.
16. Calls that are identical except for their argument list in three switch arms are NOT merged by us when written once after the switch: write the call in each arm (ROM loads a0 per
    arm and cross-jumps only `jal + delay slot`, func_0043847C last widget).
17. A global used through two different element views needs ONE symbol: `D_80146398[-0xC3]` (the mode byte D_801462D5) alongside `D_80146398[idx*150+0x80]` is how the ROM got its shared base
    register (func_0043AD20 not solved, see PARTIAL); likewise `extern s32 D_80146894[]` with `D_80146894 - 0x603` / `[-7]` for `addiu a0,s0,-0x180C` style args (func_00436488, 004360F4):
    take a local `s32 *g = D_80146894;` and use g[...] to force the base to live in a callee-saved reg (func_004360F4, func_00438E64).
18. Tail duplication / delay slots: the ROM often has `bnel/beql x,y,L ; <store or copy>` (likely branch with the then-store in the slot).  That is `if (a == b) {..} else {store}` with the
    ELSE written last and `else if` chains `if (k == -1) {zero} else if (w == f()) {rec} else {zero}` (func_00433F14) -- the variant `k == -1 || w != f()` gave a different layout.
19. decomp-permuter works here too (wip/perm_*.sh, uses ~/perm/o05_<fn>): func_00434814 (pure s2/s3 swap) was solved in <1 minute with a dummy `if (D->f3468) {A} else {A}`
    (not natural source but EXACT).  Scheduling-only diffs (func_00436898/00436AD8) did not converge in 5 minutes.
20. Struct layouts recovered are in include/o05_types.h (lobby O05Lobby / O05Plr / O05Hdr / O05Rec / O05Slot, second menu O05L2/O05Row2).
21. Chained assignment `r = p->s.c = 1;` reproduces a shared register for a stored constant and a local (func_004354A4).
22. A constant argument that the ROM keeps in a callee-saved reg needs an explicit local (`sz = 0x190`); `func(idx = -1)` keeps two names for one value.
23. Declare the real global struct (O05GameState D_801462C8, `->rec[i].b0`); declaration order of initialised locals decides giv init order.
24. Flags: overlay A uses `-O2 -mcpu=r4300`; six functions need extra token `-DNOFILL` (struct-copy return keeps `jr ra; nop`); six targets have stray head words (real entry in wip/romfix.txt).
25. Final: 156 EXACT / 16 PARTIAL / 5 NONE of 177 (func_00438BA4 is an extra group row). Permuter false zeros happen (func_00438BD0): always re-verify with chk.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace o06
FINDINGS from worker o06 (overlay A game code, ROM 0x1B4000.. / VRAM 0x43B000-0x44A000; targets func_0043B67C .. func_0044xxxx)

== OVERLAY-SPECIFIC ==
- Compiler: the same as the main game code: GCC 2.8.1 -O2 -mcpu=r4300 (no -fforce-addr, no -fno-builtin needed; sprintf/memcpy-like calls are plain jals).  ~210 functions matched with it.
  chk handles overlay addresses with no special treatment: pass the ROM offset (VRAM - 0x400000 + 0x16E000) in the list line; `jal func_004xxxxx` / `D_0045xxxx` relocs resolve by address.
- 8-byte alignment + STRAY WORDS: functions start 8-aligned and the 1..3 words in front of the real entry are junk (leftover/garbage; seen: `lw ra,0x24(sp)`, `subu v0,v0,v1`, `j .Lxxxx` + delay, `bnez/lw a1,0x58(a1)`).  The splat target list starts at the stray word, so the "function" has 1-3 extra leading words
  (funcs func_0043DCBC, 0043EF1C, 0043DD2C, 0043EFCC, 00444024, 0044632C) or consists only of junk (func_0043F284, func_00444F9C = padding, nothing to match).  Match from the first 8-aligned address (ROM +4/+8/+0xC) and say so in results.txt.
- Jump-table CASE LABELS ARE SPLIT INTO FAKE FUNCTIONS by the symbol splitter: func_004442B8/004442EC/00444320 are the loop bodies of cases 1..3 of func_00444260's switch (ONE function of 61 insns, verified as such).  Check every `jtbl_` in a target and look for targets whose address is a table entry.
- INLINE HELPERS / GROUP FILES: many functions inline smaller ones that sit LATER in the ROM (func_0043F404 inlines the typed getter func_00443030 and setter func_00443120; func_0043F290 inlines the getter; func_0043EFCC inlines func_0043F1B8; func_00444024 inlines func_00444204; func_0044632C inlines func_004467D8).
  gcc 2.8 with `inline` (non-static) emits the out-of-line copy after the user, so the original TU defines the helper first.  Verify the helper from the same file (it comes out EXACT out of line).  A helper whose body contains `return` in every case must be written with direct returns (`case 0: return *(f32*)p;`) so the inlined copy assigns straight into the destination register (func_0043F404 only matched after that; with `r=...; break; return r;` the result got an extra mov.s).
  A self-recursive helper (func_004467D8: tail recursion) turns into a LOOP when inlined (func_0044632C shows the loop).
- Menu option handlers (the bulk of this overlay): (obj, ctx) -> returns 0/1; they edit `D_801462C8` option flags, build text with sprintf into a buffer found through obj->0x14 (`s - 6 + func_00442158(obj)` = text-width based offset), and call a generic spinner func_0044252C(ctx, cur, step, min, max, wrap).  Patterns:
  * `if (X) a->unk14 = D_A; else a->unk14 = D_B; return 0;` (text pointer select) is plain C with `extern u8 D_A[];`
  * value cursors: `p = (b->unk1C) ? b->unk1C->unk5D8 : &D_80146302` written as `p=&D; if (b->unk1C) { q = ...; if (q) p = q; }`
  * switch over a u8/s8 field with cases 0,1: `switch (p->f) { case 0: ...; break; case 1: ...; break; }` (not if/else)

== GENERAL LESSONS (new) ==
- SCALAR vs ARRAY GLOBALS CHANGE ALIAS BEHAVIOUR: with `extern s32 D_x;` gcc moves the load of D_x freely across stores through pointers (and fills branch delay slots with a store from before the load);  with `extern s32 D_x[]; D_x[0]` the load stays in source order.  If the ROM has a load AFTER a store through a pointer and ours hoists it, declare the global as an array (func_00443D84, func_00443F40, func_0043EF1C, func_0044438C).  The same holds for floats.
- Symbol base kept in a register across a call/loop (`lui+addiu` once, then lw/sw 0(base), 0x10(base) ...): declare `extern T D_x[]` (or an array of structs) and use D_x->field.  If the ROM does `lui+lw`/`lui at; sw` each time, use a scalar.
- Operand order of `addu base, off`: `D[i].f` gives `addu off,base`; a pointer local `p = &D[i]; p->f` gives `addu base,off`.  For `tab + (sel<<4)` with a tab pointer the order flips if tab is held as u32 (`(O06T *)((sel << 4) + tab)`, func_0043BA34).
- `x = base + (a*K + b*4)` as one expression (`*(s32*)((u8*)D + (i*0x4D0 + j*4) + 0x4B8)`) reproduces gcc strength-reducing a combined giv in loops (func_0043BD90, 0043BA34, 0043BC3C).
- A loop that the ROM does NOT rotate (test at the top, `j top` at the bottom) is written with a goto: `top: n = a->list; if (!n) return; ...; goto top;` (func_00442BD0).  for(;;)/while/do loops get rotated by gcc.
- Switch trees: `switch(x){case 1: case 2: case 3: ...}` builds a median tree; ROM trees that test ==1 first then `<2` come from cases {0,1,2,3} where case 0 shares the default body (`case 0: default:`) (func_0044632C).  A dispatch placed after some case bodies means source order of the case bodies = ROM order of the bodies (default first when it is first in the ROM).
- Case bodies that call the same function with different constants: write each case with its OWN call (gcc cross-jumps the common `jal; addiu a2,a2,0x84` tail itself and leaves the per-case operand setup, func_00443650); a shared call after the switch with variables gives a different layout.
- if(x) {...} else if/…: per-branch `alpha = 0x80;` assignments in EVERY branch (not a default first) reproduce delay-slot filling (func_0044044C).
- `s - 4 + f(a)` (not `s + (f(a) - 4)`, not `s + f(a) - 4`) is what gcc 2.8 turns into `addiu v0,v0,-4 ; addu a0,s0,v0` after the call (func_00443B08 family).
- s16 field narrowing: `a->s16field = a->s32field - 1` loads with lhu 2(a) (BE low half), `(u8)a->s16field` loads lbu +1 etc.  ROM `lw a0,0x10 ; lhu 0x12` pairs are the same word accessed twice.
- Pass-through of unused incoming args matters: func_004422BC only matched after the 3rd (unused) parameter `c` was passed on to func_0028CF7C(D, b, c): a live pass-through a2 changes which temp registers the struct-copy loop picks (a3,t0,t1,t2 instead of a2..).
- `x = 0xFF; if (cond) x = lowbyte` / `(s32)f` etc.: see per-file notes.  Stack args after int/f32 args: f32 as the 8th parameter lives at 0x5C(sp) and is declared f32 (func_00444FA0).
- float->u32 casts (`(u32)v`) generate the 2^31 compare/sub/or sequence; (s8)(s32)v stores use trunc.w.s + sb.
- Struct copy (24 bytes) via `*(O06Seg*)p = D` = lw/lw/lw sw/sw/sw twice; a struct of u8[4] assigned as a whole becomes lwl/lwr/swl/swr (alignment 1), NOT lbu/sb: write the four byte copies separately.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace p1
FINDINGS - pilot batch 1 (40 functions from the low main image)
Result: 37 targets EXACT, 3 PARTIAL (return slot / one mov), 0 NONE (see results.txt).
  35 EXACT with the default chk setup (gcc 2.8.1 -O2 -mcpu=r4300); 2 more (func_00200568, func_00200880) only with gcc 2.7.2.
  Most functions matched on the first or second draft written from the m2c skeleton (about 25 min of wall time in total
  once the fast loop below was in place; the 40 s per chk call of the stock scripts is the real time sink).

0. TOOLING (saves most wall time)
 - tools/chk.ps1 / try.ps1 run wsl_sync.sh first (rsync of the whole tree, ~40 s per call).  Copy only what changed and run
   chk.py directly: wip/k.sh <listfile> (0.3 s per call).  wip/vv.py <spec.json> = vq.py without the sync, ONE chk call
   for all variants, keeps wip/c/_best_<fn>.c; spec may use "bodies":[...] or full-file "files":[...] and "flags".
   wip/side.sh <file> <fn> <rom> = side-by-side asm diff (-O2 -mcpu=r4300).  Env GCCV=2.7.2 and CHK=wip/chkvg.py select the
   compiler and the "game" assembler mode (wip/gamefix.py); wip/side3.sh / wip/lj.py give side-by-side for those modes.
 - Do NOT write `extern ... PI_STATUS_REG`: common.h #defines it (phys addr).  Use IO_READ(PI_STATUS_REG) (gives lui 0xA460/ori).
 - Shell note: very long bash commands with several heredocs containing apostrophes break the tool; write files in small chunks.

1. COMPILER / ASSEMBLER BOUNDARY (important for the next batches)
 - Everything from func_002039F8 (ROM 0x45F8) upwards matched gcc 2.8.1 -O2 -mcpu=r4300 with the normal 'lu' assembler
   (gcc fills `jr ra` with the sp adjust itself).
 - The lowest part (ROM 0x1168..0x2D98: func_00200568, 00200880, 00201A74, 00202198) is different (probably its own
   compile unit(s)):
   * the return epilogue is NOT slot filled: ROM has `addiu sp,sp,N ; jr ra ; nop` (unfilled), gcc 2.8.1 emits `jr ra ; addiu`.
   * func_00202198 starts with `lui v0 ; lw v1,lo(v0)` BEFORE `addiu sp,sp,-0x48` (scheduled prologue, RTL-style) and
     func_00201A74 stores a global via `lui a0 ; sw v1,lo(a0)` (explicit temp register instead of $at).  That is what
     gcc 2.8.1 -msplit-addresses produces: with `-O2 -msplit-addresses` (no -mcpu=r4300; -mcpu=r4000 also works, -mips2/3
     too) the bodies of 201A74 / 202198 / 200880 are identical to the ROM, only the return slot differs (2 words).
     I could not find the option/assembler setting that leaves that slot empty (tried -mips1/2/3, -mcpu=*, -fno-delayed-branch,
     2.7.2.3, 2.91.66, 2.95.2, bare-j-nop 'game' assembler).
   * func_00200568 and func_00200880 are EXACT with gcc 2.7.2 -O2 (200880 needs the 'game' assembler: bare `j $31` keeps a nop).
     For func_00200568 gcc 2.7.2 does not fill the beq slot but fills `j $31 ; sw`; gcc 2.8.1 fills both -> mismatch there.
     func_00201A74 / 00202198 are 8 / 2 words off with 2.7.2 (store order / lui temp register; 2.7.2 has no -msplit-addresses).
   Suggest a per-function mode in chk list lines (compiler version, return-slot behaviour); wip/chkvg.py is a hack for that.
 - func_002016DC (strlen incl NUL) matches both.

2. SOURCE-STYLE LESSONS (gcc 2.8.1 -O2 -mcpu=r4300)
 - Structs with big padding arrays and offset-named fields (unk5D8 ...) are fine: all field accesses are plain lw/sw at the
   m2c offsets.  Object graph seen: Actor.def (0x18) -> ActorDef.info (embedded block at +0x14: flags, flags24, unk50 ...),
   Actor.stats (0x5D8), Actor.owner (0x1D8) -> Actor.brain (0x1454), singly linked lists through Actor.next (0x16E0) starting at
   ActorGroup.head (0x20).  A second argument "state / handler block" (0x108/0x10C/0x110 handler pointers, 0x124/0x128/0x12C
   values that are s32 for some callers and f32 for others -> union) is shared by the func_002xxxxx handlers; the common
   dispatcher is func_00214178(actor, state, eventId).  m2c DROPS pass-through arguments: look for an untouched a0/a1 before
   a jal (it hid actor/state in 5 of my functions, e.g. func_002065C0 calls func_0024B690(actor, arg2, 1)).
 - m2c's literal shape is often exactly right, even for odd code: `u16 v = x + n; field = v; if (v > 100) v = 100; field = v;`
   (func_0022B10C) matched.  Start from the m2c text, convert the gotos into if/while/for, keep the evaluation order.
 - Return types: m2c prints s16 for an lh-loaded value that is returned; the ROM returns it without sign-extension, so the C
   return type is s32 (func_0020459C).  For a boolean result `if (D == 0 || x != y) return 1; return 0;` the matching form was
   `if (D && x == y) return 0; return 1;` (gives beql ... addiu v0,1 in the slot) (func_00203EB0).
 - Globals: `extern GameFlags D_x;` accessed as D_x.f makes gcc fold sym+off into the address (lui + %lo(sym+off)); when the
   ROM keeps a register with &sym (lui;addiu sym ; lw 0x54(v1) ; lw 0x68(v1)) declare the global as an ARRAY
   (`extern GameFlags D_801468A0[];` and use D_801468A0->field).  This fixed func_0022A410 and func_0022A67C.
   Separate scalar float globals: `extern f32 D_800C7DE0;`; arrays `extern f32 D_800C7DD8[]` indexed [1] both give lui $at/lwc1.
   Opaque struct globals passed as &D_xxx: `extern u8 D_8011FE88[];` works (lui/addiu).
 - Row arrays with a 400 byte stride: `D_80102B10[idx].unk0` with a 0x190 byte struct gives the sll/addu chain (x*25*16) exactly.
 - Switch statements: `switch (t) { case 1: case 4: case 7: return 1; default: return 0; }` gives the compare tree of the ROM
   (func_0020C9CC).  Case ORDER in the source matters: func_002172D0 only matched with
   `case 1: case 2: s0 = src; break; case 0: s0 = src->unkD0; break;` (case 0 first gave another tree).
   func_00209B64: `case 1: break; default: reset; /*fallthrough*/ case 0: ...; break; case 2: ...` (default in the middle).
   Dead-looking `lbu + bltz + slti 3` range checks in the ROM = a switch on a u8.
 - `if (a == 11 || a == 12)` is merged by fold into a single range test (addiu/sltiu); the ROM (func_0022ABF0) has separate
   compares in the order 11,12,14,13 and matched only with gotos into shared arms
   (`if (t==11) goto A; if (t==12) { A: r=0x19000; } else if (t==14) goto B; else if (t==13) { B: r=0x12C00; } else goto C;`).
   Look for this whenever the ROM shows sequential beq/bne chains on adjacent constants (original was probably a macro).
 - Floating point: operand evaluation order follows the C text: `(f32)cfg->x < call() + k` keeps the left operand live in a
   callee-saved FP register across the call (sdc1 spill); the ROM computed the call first -> `call() + k > (f32)cfg->x`
   (func_0020A884).  Re-comparing against the same global inside a loop (`min == D_800C6E90`) is what produces the hoisted
   `mov.s fv1,fv0` copy of the constant in func_0020D28C.
 - A struct copy of 12 bytes (`Vec3f a = src->v;`) gives lw,lw,lw / sw,sw,sw (func_002174E4); `b.y = 0` stores `$zero`.
 - Display-list pushes: `dl = D_80110634++; dl->w0 = 0xE3001201; dl->w1 = 0x2000;` with `extern DlCmd *D_80110634` matched twice
   (func_002183E8 / func_00218F08: same body, different tail callee, probably the same macro).
 - Flag updates written as two statements `actor->flags &= ~0x2000; actor->flags &= ~0x100;` fold into one and/and pair like
   the ROM; in func_0020459C the plain `actor->flags |= / &= ` in an if/else matched while a local copy of the word did not.
 - A test used as a value, `ok = (state->flags & 0x20000) != 0;`, folds into srl/andi; the ROM has lui/and/sltu: write
   `u32 f = state->flags & 0x20000; ok = f != 0;` (func_00207C94).  `if (ok) {...} else { state->unk40 = 0; }` (instead of an
   early return) produced the ROM's beql with the store in the delay slot; `a >= b` float compares: write `x >= y` as in C text.
 - Open issue: func_0022AA34 (ROM has an extra `mov.s f1,f0` after `mul.s f0,f1,f0` before the compare; ~25 variants incl.
   inline helpers, temps, ternaries did not reproduce it).  Everything else of that function matches.

3. PROTOTYPE HINTS
 - Handlers: (Actor *actor, ActorState *state[, extra]).  func_00200500 / func_00200538 look like an interrupt mask
   disable/restore pair (return old mask / take mask).  func_00200800 = read u8 from a PI/ROM address, func_002005A0 = read
   u32; func_00200880 = ROM-to-RAM byte copy with alignment fixup (lead-in byte loop, word loop, tail loop).
 - func_00201A74 / func_00202198 build a request and either send it to an I/O thread (osSendMesg to D_800F6F40 / osRecvMesg
   on D_800F7110 when D_800CD394 == 2) or call func_002017E8 directly under the interrupt mask.
 - Names in results.txt are guesses.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace p2
FINDINGS from pilot batch 2 (40 functions, main image 0x2C050-0x57A8C): 36 EXACT, 4 PARTIAL (1-6 words off), 0 NONE.
Workflow speed: ~28 of 36 matched on the first or second compile of a straightforward C transcription.
Flags -O2 -mcpu=r4300 are right (same compiler behaviour as libultra, all codegen idioms below reproduced).

== Workflow tips ==
- Write C directly from the asm + m2c (m2c is good for field offsets and call signatures, bad for control flow).
- wip/ck.ps1 (fn:rom list -> chk with game flags), wip/V.ps1 (variant search wrapper around vq.py; quote the rom as '0x..'
  or PowerShell turns it into an int and vq crashes), wip/side.ps1 (side-by-side with -O2 -mcpu=r4300; try.ps1 uses lu flags!),
  wip/types.py + wip/types_spec.py (sparse struct spec -> include/p2_types.h with explicit padding, avoids offset typos).
- chk masks relocs, so symbol identity is invisible, but the *shape* of address code (lui/addiu base register reuse, `lui $at` +
  lwc1) tells you how globals are declared (see Globals).

== Globals / addressing ==
- Scalar float global used once or twice: `lui $at,%hi(sym); lwc1 $f,%lo(sym)($at)`.  If we declare `extern f32 D[]` and index
  D[1] twice GCC builds `addiu $v0,&D` and uses 0($v0)/4($v0): MISMATCH.  Where the asm says `%hi(D_800CE3E0 + 0x4)`
  the original had a separate scalar (declare `extern f32 D_800CE3E4;` -> matched; same for D_800E28D0/D_800E28D4 as two s32 scalars).
- If the asm builds ONE base register with lui/addiu and then uses it for accesses at 0, +0x44, -0xBCC (func_002549C8), the
  original is a single big extern struct: PoolMgr at D_80103F70 (cnt at +0xBCC, field at +0xC10; D_80104564, D_80104584,
  D_8010510C, D_8010513C, D_80105180 are all inside it).  Declaring `extern PoolMgr D_80103F70;` (struct with padding) and using
  fields reproduced the base-register CSE exactly.  Generalisation: negative/odd offsets from a reg that holds a symbol address
  == members of one big struct/array, not separate globals.
- `lui $v0,%hi(g); lw $v0,%lo(g)($v0)` is the normal scalar integer/pointer global access.
- Arrays of structs: `D[i].field` with stride 16 (func_0023C6AC) compiles to pointer-increment loops; `for (i=0;i<24;i++)` matched at once.
- `if (&sym == 0)` style loop `for (n = &D_80103F88; n != NULL; n = n->next)` compiles with the `beqz` on the constant address (kept by GCC!).

== Structs ==
- Player-like object: huge struct (size >= 0x16E4) linked through `next` at +0x16E0; Game(+0x20) holds the head; global list head
  D_80145060.  Many fields at 0x5xx-0x12xx (state s16 at +0x650, pawn ptr +0x5DC, weapon idx s16 +0x62E, f32 fields 0x658,0x720,...).
- Actor-like object: pos at +8/+0xC/+0x10, info ptr +0x18, f32 at 0x194/0x198, s32 0x174; 24-byte `seg[]` array at +0x140 and
  64-byte matrices at +0x68 indexed by the global side index D_800D297C (<<6 and *24 address arithmetic).
- PNode/List (func_00255xxx list package): node key at +0, flags +0xC, prev +0x20, next +0x24; List = {head, tail} 0x14 bytes.
  A second plain doubly linked list (next +0, prev +4) exists (func_0023CB80).
- float args in o32: when the first arg is an integer, f32 args come in $a1.. (mtc1 $a1,$fv).  Prototype them as f32, GCC then
  emits lw/mfc1 exactly as the ROM (e.g. `void f(Player*, f32 d, s32)`); f32 fields passed on as args are loaded with `lw`.
- 12-byte struct passed by value in $a1..$a3 gives the "sw a1/a2/a3 to 0x1C.. then &arg" idiom (func_0023939C, printf-like wrapper);
  real varargs (`...`) additionally spills $a0 and uses different offsets, so it is NOT that.

== Idioms: source shape -> codegen ==
- `if (p) return x != 0; return 0;` (positive test, early-return of the false case last) matched where `if (!p) return 0;` did not
  (func_0022BA90).  Similarly `u32 v = flags & 2; return v != 0;` gives `andi; sltu v0,zero,v0` whereas `(flags & 2) != 0` inline
  gives the srl/andi 1 form (func_002458F8).
- A static inline helper returning 1/0 (with `if (c1||c2||c3) return 1; return 0;`) inlined into `if (helper(p)) return 0;` gives the
  `v0 = 0/1; bnez v0` value-materialised pattern with the result in $v0 and forces a reload of p->state afterwards (func_0022C6D4).
  Plain `||` chain in the `if` or a local `s32 r` put the temp in another register.
- Float clamp `t = lim; if (!(x <= t)) t = x;` produced `c.le.s x,lim; bc1t` + `mov.s` (func_0022B68C).  `if (x > t)` produced
  `c.lt.s` (different).  Negating the comparison in source is the way to flip c.lt/c.le + bc1t/bc1f.
- Two branches that both assign 0.0f and share one block (c.lt.s / bc1f / j): write them with a goto into the shared `d = 0` block
  (func_0022DD84).  The natural nested-if form lets GCC CSE the 0.0 into a spare FP register (mismatch).
- switch with a sparse jump table: put the generic `default`-like cases first in source order, and cases that share a tail block
  can have their labels *inside* the earlier `if` body (func_0022EAFC: `case 12: if (x) { case 0: case 18..21: return 1; } return call();`).
  Out-of-range goes to the real `default: return 0`.  Jump table words are plain 0x0022EBxx addresses at ROM = VA - 0x80000000 + 0xC00.
- 3-way if/else-if/else on ints that call the same function with different (ptr,const) pairs: use `switch` (func_0022F504) - the
  if-chain emitted different branch order.
- Independent stores: GCC's scheduler emits them in a different order than source; to get ROM order 0x14,0xC,0x10,0x18,0x8 the
  source order was 0x8,0x14,0xC,0x10,0x18 (i.e. reversed-ish; just try permutations with vq).
- Callee list order does not matter in these small files (no static callee), but declaration style does: K&R `void f();`
  (no prototype) is needed when the ROM does not set up argument registers for a call (func_0024AA08()).
- `return a >= 3 && a < 7;` folds to `(u32)(a-3) < 4`; the ROM keeps two compares, so write nested ifs (func_00232770).
- Loops over `p = p->next` lists with count/sum: for-loop transcriptions matched immediately (6 functions).
- Struct copy `a->seg[i] = *src;` for a 24-byte struct emits the lw t0-t3 / sw / lw t0,t1 / sw sequence: matched.
- memset call args: `memset(o->f1C = blk->data, 0, n << 6)` (assignment inside the call) reproduced the store-in-delay-slot order.

== Unmatched residue (for the next batches) ==
- func_0024B8DC / func_0024B980 (1 word each): ROM has `sllv $a3,$v0,$a2` (shift amount = the register that holds the constant arg 1)
  inside the `idx*24` address computation, we emit `sll $a3,$v0,1`.  Something makes cse reuse the pseudo holding constant 1 as
  shift count; local `one` variables, explicit shifts, casts did not reproduce it.  Probably the same trick as any other `x*3` next to
  a literal-1 argument; worth testing in other functions to learn the trigger.
- func_0022C1D8: pure FP register numbering (two live f32 pseudos swapped); func_0023C77C: pure callee-saved swap (s0/s1).
  Both are allocation-priority/pseudo-order effects; permuting local declaration order and expression grouping did not flip them.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace p3
FINDINGS from pilot batch 3 (40 functions, 5-60 insns, main image 0x59360-0x83C38): 37 EXACT, 2 PARTIAL, 1 NONE.
Flags used everywhere: -O2 -mcpu=r4300.  Most functions matched on the 1st-3rd try.

TOOLING
- tools/try.ps1 / lu_ud.sh use the libultra default flags (-O3 -fforce-addr) unless env FLAGS is set: for game code its
  side-by-side output is MISLEADING.  I use wip/side.sh <fn> [file] which runs lu_ud.sh with FLAGS='-O2 -mcpu=r4300'
  (then tools/lu_side.py).  wip/mk.sh <fn...> builds the chk list (wip/out/<fn>.c or wip/hard) and runs chk.
- vtry.py ranks by "first differing word"; a result "1000000 words" is either EXACT or a COMPILE FAIL (C89: no declarations
  after statements!).  Check the final line ("best file ... EXACT") or run chk.
- vq/vtry write wip_list.txt in the project root and keep stale wip/_v_*.c files: rm wip/_v_*.c between searches.

ARGUMENT PASSING (biggest win of the batch: unlocks many "weird" prologues)
- A `sw $aN, home($sp)` of an incoming arg register in the prologue + a `lw $aN, home($sp)` right before a call means a
  STRUCT passed by value that begins in a register (usually a Vec3 {f32 x,y,z} starting at a1/a2/a3: remaining words are on
  the stack).  Declare the parameter as a struct (P3Vec3) in both function and callee prototype; gcc copies the stack part
  of the struct to the outgoing area with lw/sw (this explains "stack args reloaded and re-stored in pairs").
  Float values passed as plain f32 after an int arg are in INT regs (mtc1 a1,...) - declare f32 params, no casts.
- Plain stack int args are loaded at function ENTRY only when the pseudo is multiply set/used; if the ROM loads a stack arg
  early but the C loads it late, add a local copy and a dead later assignment (func_00268864: `k = e; ... k = 0;`).
- `sw v0,16($sp); lwc1 f0,16($sp)` after a call returning s32 = bit-pun of an int return into a float:
  `s32 tmp = f(); x = *(f32*)&tmp;` (a union gets optimised into a direct mtc1/no stack trip and does NOT match).
- (s16)param passed on to a callee taking s16: `sll 16; sra 16` appears at the call, the raw register is stored elsewhere:
  make the parameter s32 in your function and s16 in the callee prototype.

GLOBALS
- A global array/struct accessed several times (`extern s32 D_x[]; D_x[0]` twice) keeps its address (lui+addiu) in one reg,
  while a scalar `extern s32 D_x` is lui/lw, lui/sw each time.  func_0025E2F4: use the array form to get `addiu $v1,$v1,%lo`.
- A symbol referenced as `D_800C9490` and `D_800C9490 + 4` in two separate lui/lwc1 pairs (each with its own lui $at) =
  two separate scalar globals D_800C9490 / D_800C9494 (an array would CSE the base).  Declare them as two scalars.
- Float/double literal pools: u32->f32 conversion goes u32->f64 (cvt.d.w + add 2^32 literal if negative) -> cvt.s.d; the
  literal (ro=0x...:8) is reported by chk.  Casting a u32 to f32 reproduces this.
- Tables of 4 pads etc. (D_8010F310[4], D_8010FC28[4], D_8010FBF0[4]) are separate s32 arrays; loops indexed `D[i]` compile
  to walking pointers anyway (3 ptr + counter), so write array indexing, not pointer walking.

CODEGEN <-> SOURCE PATTERNS
- Local `k = i*2` reused later gave a different register assignment than writing `i*2` and `i*2+1` inline (CSE does the
  rest): prefer repeating the expression (func_0025D370).
- Float min/max: `box[0] = pt[0] <= box[0] ? pt[0] : box[0]` generated the INVERTED compare (c.le swapped, bc1tl);
  the ROM form came from `!(p[0] <= box[0]) ? box[0] : p[0]`.  For a loop over 12-byte points use `p = pt + i*3`
  inside a `for (i...)` (the `pt++` form let gcc strength-reduce the z access differently).
- A short if-chain returning 1 at the start and in several places with a trailing `x==1||x==4||x==7||x==0xB` test is a
  `switch(kind){case 1: case 4: case 7: case 0xB: return 1;} return 0;` (gcc emits a compare chain for sparse cases, so
  the S flag in targets.txt is not reliable either way).
- Delay-slot filling: if our version has `bnezl` + hoisted store where the ROM has `bnez; nop` and the real first insn of the
  else block is a `lw`, swap the order of the first two statements of that block (func_00279530: `n->prev = l->head;` first).
- if/else shape: when the ROM lays out the "zero everything" case LAST (after the nested ifs), write
  `if (cond) {nested...} else {zero...}` (func_00263C44); writing `if (!cond) {zero} else ...` gives different jumps.
- Struct-local copies: `P3QArr loc; loc.q = a->q;` (copy only the sub-struct, local keeps the unused first word slot so the
  copy lands at sp+4) matched the frame layout of func_00260CBC; `P3QInfo q = a->q` gives sp+0.
- Hoisting of `w[1]` above `if (sh)` needs a named temp `hi = w[1]` before the branch (func_00260CBC).
- Leaf mutex-like code: `u32 mask = __osDisableInt(); if (++l->count != 1) {__osRestoreInt(mask); osRecvMesg(...);} else __osRestoreInt(mask);`
  matched directly (the duplicate __osRestoreInt in both arms is real, not a join).

CODE-BASE STYLE
- Game objects are big structs accessed with raw offsets: pad arrays + named fields are enough; there is no sign of
  bitfields.  Helper wrappers on one global object (D_8010C080: a sound/audio lock object) are 9-insn thunks
  `f(&D_8010C080[, arg])`: declare the global as `extern u8 D_8010C080[]` (array decay gives the lui/addiu pair).
- Vector/matrix code: 4x4 f32 row-major, rows 0x10 apart; vec math is spelled out per component, left-to-right sums
  `a*x + b*y + c*z`; mult operand order is (matrix elem * vector elem).  Matches first try with plain expressions.
- libultra-style doubly linked lists with embedded sentinels at +4/+0xD8 of the owner (func_00259938) and counted singly
  linked lists with head/tail/count (func_00279530) are common; sound code uses alSndp* with an ALSndPlayer embedded at +0x84
  of a bank struct.
- Unresolved ordering problems (scheduling / register naming only, instruction mix identical):
  func_00273860 (2 sinf/cosf rotation of matrix rows: ROM loads row-2 elements first, y1/y2 later),
  func_00259938 (t0/t1 swap for hoisted &sentinel2 vs copy of &sentinel1), func_00260CBC (bits/hi temp regs a0/a2).
  These smell like source shape differences in how temps are declared; maybe the real code used macros or helper
  functions that got inlined.

=================== FINDINGS from workspace p4
FINDINGS (pilot batch 4: 40 functions, 5-60 insns, ROM 0x8490C-0xAA3F0; 37 EXACT, 3 PARTIAL (0028B250, 0029A778, 002A13F4); 0028B250 far, other two 2-7 words)
Total effort: roughly 90 compile variants for 40 functions; 24 functions matched on the 1st or 2nd source, 13 needed 3-6 variants.

TOOLING (important, affects every function with a struct field offset > 0x7FFF)
- The ROM assembler expands `op reg, BIG(base)` (|BIG| > 0x7FFF) as  lui $at,hi ; addu $at,BASE,$at ; op reg,lo($at)  (base FIRST, always $at,
  also for loads).  tools/gas_compat.py did not model this (modern gas uses the dest reg for loads and addu at,at,base for stores).
  Patched in my workspace: new `bigoff_re` rule in fix() (copy at wip/out/gas_compat.py; diff is ~15 lines).  With it 00294AC4 and 00292FA4 match.
  Please merge it into the lead's tools/gas_compat.py (and the real build path which uses the same script).
  Source code for such fields is just `*(s32*)((u8*)p + 0x26DC8)` or a struct with a pad[] array; no special trick needed.
- tools/try.ps1 -Side hides relocation differences (it shows `<reloc>` for ours and real insns for ROM, and marks only non-reloc lines).
