  chk / vq's "best file ... EXACT" are authoritative.  A function that ends in an infinite loop (`j self; nop`) shows 3 trailing
  "differing" words in chk (object padding to 16 bytes vs the next function's code): treat first N real insns as the match.
- vq.py bodies: use one string per variant, separated e.g. by a `====` line in a text file (wip/mk.py in my workspace builds spec.json from header/body files).
  vq reports the best file as wip/_v_<k>.c (index = body order); files get overwritten by the next vq run, copy winners immediately.
- Do NOT leave failed attempts under wip/out (my batch listing picked up a stale file first); keep them in wip/hard.

COMPILER / STYLE OBSERVATIONS
- Unused incoming a0: many small wrappers (func_00293E60, 0029414C, 002943B8, 00294A10) call a callee with the first arg register never set:
  the real function takes an (unused here) first parameter and passes it through, so declare `void f(s32 a)` and call `g(a, ...)`.
  General rule: if a callee gets $a0 without an `addu $a0,...` / lui before the jal, add a pass-through parameter.
- Unused local arrays are NOT optimized away by this gcc: func_002AC398 has a 0x120 frame (0x118 ra slot) = `u8 buf[0x100];` unused.
- `extern const f32 X; *a = X; *b = X;` loads once (const => no reload after the first store); non-const reloads (alias with the stores).
- f32 arg after an int arg travels in the int regs (mfc1 $a3 / mtc1 $a1): just give prototypes with f32 params, gcc does the rest.
- double constants: asm `lui $at; ldc1 $fN,%lo($at)` = literal pool use; `extern f64 D_XXXX; x - D_XXXX` compiles to the same code (relocs are masked).
- Self-recursive tail call (strcasecmp 002A13F4) becomes `beq ..., func_self` (jump to the top, delay slot nop): write it recursively, not as a loop.
- Two calls in the arms of an if/else followed by common code: gcc cross-jumps the identical tails, producing `j .Lcommon` with the delay slot
  filled by an arg setup (func_0028414C).  Write the call twice (once per arm); a single call after an if/else with temps does NOT give that layout.
- Taking `s32 *p = D_XXXX;` once and using p[0], p[-4] is needed when the asm computes the symbol address once (addiu) and uses negative offsets
  (func_0028414C); using the global name directly gives lui+lw separately.
- Large-literal display list code (func_002A9700/97F0) is plain F3D macros: `P4Gfx *g = D_80110634++; g->words.w0=..; g->words.w1=..;` repeated; the compiler
  keeps glistp in a register and stores it back after each increment (no strict aliasing), exactly like the ROM.  7 commands: SETTIMG(FD900000,tex),
  SETTILE(F5900100,0700C040), RDPLOADSYNC(E6000000), LOADBLOCK(F3000000,0701F800), RDPPIPESYNC(E7000000), SETTILE(F5800300,0000C040), SETTILESIZE(F2000000,0003C01C).
- Register-allocation sensitive spots (hard cases): which of two loop variables gets the lower register depends on pseudo priorities; things that changed it:
  * `for (i = 0, found = NULL; ...)` (init inside the for header) vs separate statements (func_0028CC10: `x = y = 0` copy direction follows source order of the inits);
  * making the pointer a separate initialised local vs a for-header init;
  * reordering the operands of a && condition (`c1 == c2 && c2` vs `c2 && c1 == c2`) flips the register of the two loop-carried temps (func_002A13F4).
- Duplicate local copies matter: func_0028B874 needs `u8 *p2 = bits;` used in the else arm (asm has two registers holding the same pointer: addu a1,v0; addu a2,a1);
  func_0028C5E8 needs `obj = n->obj` cached in a local and a `done = expired` flag copy (asm: v1 = s1 ... s1 = v1).
- Bit tests: `(bits[bit/8] & m) != 0` with `s32 m = 1 << (bit & 7);` as a separate local produces and/sltu; inline shift expression turns into srav/andi 1.
  Use `bit / 8` (signed, with the bgez/addiu 7 fixup) when the asm has `bgez ...; addiu $x,$x,7; sra 3`; `bit & 7` for the mask.
- Comparison direction decides load order: `o->f58 > o->f50` (not `o->f50 < o->f58`) gave the ROM's load order (func_002A2D14).
- Loop `for (i=0;i<n;...)` with i known 0 compiles to `blez n` entry test only if the `i = 0` is directly before the loop (loop.c init detection);
  a `goto found` out of the loop with i = -1 after it reproduces `v1=-1,v0=-1; beq v1,v0` (func_0028B250 structure) but not its register numbers.
- Tables of records: indexing (`T->ent[i].id`) vs pointer walking give different strength-reduction; func_002992C4 needed indexing and func_0029A778 pointer-walk init
  (the ROM address arithmetic `D + (idx*20 + 0x1C)` comes from `&D->ent[idx]` with ent at +0x1C).

DATA STRUCTURE / GAME CODE HINTS
- D_8014D080 is a pointer to a table struct (header 0x1C bytes, then 64 x 0x14-byte slots: {id (-1 = free), f4, f8, fC, f10}); funcs 002992C4 (alloc/free slot),
  0029A778 (set value by id).  Looks like a sound-effect/voice slot table (id, kind, value).
- Game objects embed a doubly linked list node at +0x1D0..+0x1DC (flags, counter ptr, prev, next) and the owner keeps head/cursors at +0x3C00/04/08 (00290930, 0028CC10).
- Several objects carry a "tree/bitmap" accessed through func_0028FD94(node, idx)/func_0028FDD8(node, flag) returning child nodes (a small property/bitset container):
  0028DD80 tests a bit, 0028B874 sets/clears one.  Index of the bitmap node is at owner+0x1B40C, owner->f80 is the root.
- func_002A1238/12E8/13F4 are strlen / strcat / strcasecmp; 0029FBE4/FCBC/FFE4/A0118 are 4x4 f32 matrix helpers (scale by vec3, uniform scale, get translation row 3,
  extract the three basis rows); 002A9700/97F0 build a 16-bit texture-image load block in the global display list pointer D_80110634.
- Libultra calls are ordinary: osCreateThread/osStartThread/osSetThreadPri/osSetIntMask prototypes from common.h work unchanged.

=== round-3 r01 findings ===
FINDINGS - round 3, workspace r01 (main-image leftovers, ROM 0x1000-0x18530 region)

TOOLING (wip/, copied/extended from m01)
- wip/k.sh <file> <fn> [tokens]  one-function chk in ~2 s (chkx tokens: @C/@D/@H low region, @M mult-slot fixup, -fno-builtin...); wip/sd.sh = side-by-side (now understands @ tokens, --norm);
  wip/vx.py <fn> <variants.txt> [tokens] = N full-file variants (separated by //====) in ONE chk call, prints best (kept as wip/c/vx_<fn>_best.c);
  wip/fin.py <fn> <file> "<tokens>" "<note>" "<name>" = chk + wip/out/<fn>.c + results line; wip/declperm.py = shuffle local declarations (no effect in this gcc: order of
  declarations does NOT change register allocation, only stack slot order); wip/gs.sh <file> flags = raw gcc -S (GCCV=2.7.2 etc.); wip/dp.sh <file> = global-allocator priority table
  (pseudo, refs, live length, priority = floor_log2(refs)*refs/len, hard reg); wip/prun.sh/perm_bg.sh/pst.sh/pcp.sh/pd.sh = decomp-permuter queue / status / copy / diff.
- The ~/trw_<ws> sync is the bottleneck when 10+ workers run (a full rsync took >15 min): fchkx.sh now copies only include/ and the listed files.
- perm_setup.sh now also finds `alabel` entries (functions that start inside a group span) and ends the target with `endlabel <fn>`.
- decomp-permuter output is cpp-expanded (types may have changed!): it found `volatile` for D_800CD390 and `new_var = 0/-1` shared locals.  ALWAYS re-chk the permuter output and
  port the change by hand into the real file (the .pp.c is not deliverable).  `--stop-on-zero` can fire on a permuter-only zero that chk does not confirm.

NEW COMPILER FACT (affects every function with `mult ; beq/bne ... ; nop`): the original toolchain NEVER puts mult/multu/div/divu into a branch delay slot (0 occurrences in the whole
ROM asm), our gcc 2.8.1 does (reorg fills the slot with the mult that precedes the branch).  ROM shape: `mult a,b ; beq x,y,L ; nop`.  Fix = chkx token @M (wip/multfix.py rewrites the
gcc -S text `.set noreorder; branch; mult; .set reorder` into `mult; branch; nop`).  func_0020BC50 is EXACT with `-O2 -mcpu=r4300 @M` (was 91 words off in 3 earlier workers' hands).
The same issue blocks func_0028FEEC (m/o rounds) and func_002AA724 (mult before bgez) - they should match with @M too.  The lead should add @M to the real build (cc.sh: post-process the .s).

LOW REGION (ROM 0x1000-0x38xx, tokens @C = -msplit-addresses -mcpu=r4000 + unfilled epilogue)
- D_800CD390 (the I/O thread init flag) is `volatile s32`: func_002016FC only matches with it (store emitted before the first jal).
- Byte-valued parameters must be declared u8 (func_00200C28/00200CA0: `u8 val` gives the ROM's s1/s2 order; func_00200F98: `u8 val` fixes the prologue copy order).
- `while (n--)` (not `if (n) do {...} while (--i != -1)`) is the original byte-copy loop form (func_00201064).
- Table search with a plain `while (src < T[i].lo || T[i].hi < src) i++;` (no preceding `if`) gives the ROM's un-peeled loop; `u32 i` gives sltiu; `len = hi - (src - 1)` must be
  written through a temp `u32 e = src - 1` (func_002010D8).
- `switch (cmd)` with a hidden case label at the lowest value (`case 0x10: break;`) gives the `addiu -16; sltiu 10` jump-table range (func_002014A4).

GROUPS / RODATA
- func_00203F04 + func_0020402C are ONE object (203F04's 0.5f at ROM 0xC773C, 20402C's 122.88f at 0xC7740, jump table of 20402C's inner switch at 0xC7748).  Combined file
  wip/out/func_00203F04.c: both members EXACT with -O2 -mcpu=r4300 -fno-builtin.  Float literals come from the ASSEMBLER (li.s) and are appended AFTER the explicit .rdata (jump table) in our
  object (table, 0.5, 122.88); the ROM has literals first, then the (8-aligned) table.  Not fixable in C.  The ROM also has one extra word (0x4C2C, junk `andi v0,v0,0x400`) between the two
  functions (20402C entry is 8-aligned in ROM: 0x4C30), our combined .text is 0x400 bytes vs 0x404 ROM span.
- func_00200AD8 (leaf byte-RMW helper, low region, @C) is EXACT alone and in the combined 200AD8+200B14 compile (wip/c/grp_200AD8.c); it only "clashes" in the project build because that build
  lacks the low-region token.

GOOD FINDS (source shape)
- Early returns with a shared `return 1` block: `if (call() == 0) return 1; if (*p == t) return 1; return 0;` / nested `if (call()) { if (*p != t) return 0; } return 1;` give the ROM's
  `beql v0,zero,L ; li v0,1` + `lw v1,0(v0) ; bne v1,t,L ; move v0,zero` (func_00203F04); the `||` form and value-materialising forms (xor/sltiu) do not.
- `vis = 1; if (call(..) != 0) vis = *D == p;` (plain ==) gives `sltu vis,t,vis` with the constant 1 CSE'd into the vis register; the explicit `(u32)(a ^ b) < 1U` form does not (func_00203278).
- A global base + struct member kept as two live registers (lui/addiu s4 = &D; addiu s5 = s4 + 0x1860): write `g = D_80145040; gf = &g->gf;` as locals initialised in the for-header
  (`for (i = 0, g = D_80145040, gf = &g->gf; ...)`), see func_00210228.
- `x = 0.0f; if (c) x = ...` must be written `if (c) x = ...; else x = 0.0f;` when the ROM materialises the zero in the else arm / delay slot (func_00202FDC, FP temp in f1).
- A shared constant in a local (`s32 m1 = -1;` used by an early store AND in a loop; `zero = 0` for two call args) was what the permuter found for func_00208000 / func_002016FC-like cases:
  when the ROM keeps ONE register for two uses of a constant, give the constant one named local.
- ROM zero-test ordering of `switch (zone)` after other code: `case 0: break;` hidden label gives `beq 1 / slti 2 / bnez` tree (func_0021035C).
- Register pins (`register T x asm("$17")`): func_0021035C hit -> s1 brought it from 35 to 3 words (PINNED, not delivered unless the rest matches).

TOOL BUNDLE: wip/out/tools_r01/ (multfix.py + chkx.py token @M; the rest is the fast single-function loop).  chkx tokens: @C/@D/@H (low region), @M (mult/div never in a delay slot).
TODO for the lead: add the @M post-processing (wip/multfix.py: rewrite `.set noreorder; .set nomacro; <branch>; <mult|div>; .set macro; .set reorder` into `<mult|div>; <that block with a nop slot>`) to tools/cc.sh/gas_compat for
game code; confirmed EXACT: func_0020BC50 (r01) and func_0028FEEC (m05's draft: 14 words -> EXACT); func_002AA724 improves 74 -> 60 words.  The only `mult/div ; branch ; nop` sites in the ROM are
func_0020BC50 (2x), func_0028FEEC, func_002AA724 (ROM scan: 0 mult/div in any delay slot, 78 mflo/mfhi in slots, so only the multiplier insns are excluded).

ALLOCATION / CSE FACTS (gcc 2.8.1 as built here)
- Struct-copy scratch registers (movstrsi, 12-byte `a = b`) are picked by RELOAD: the first register not mentioned anywhere in the function's RTL.  The ROM's scratch set (a2,a3,t0 / t0,t1,t2) tells you
  that v0/v1 (and a0..a3) are mentioned elsewhere: if ours uses v1,t0,t1 a pseudo that the ROM keeps in v1 lives in v0 in our build (func_00203F04: `*D` load must land in v1 -> nested-if return form;
  func_002095F8: load owner before target so owner takes v1).
- Address-of-local args: `&local` (X != 0 from the frame base, i.e. anything but the FIRST declared local) is copied into a pseudo by expand_call; two uses in one extended basic block are
  merged by cse into one callee-saved register (`addu s1,sp,X` + `move a0,s1`).  The FIRST declared local (X == 0, address == the frame base register) is never cached.
  The ROM does NOT cache &qB / &v60 in func_00202E4C / func_00203D4C / func_002034A4 (the Quat result of func_00202CA0, then func_002742B4(&q, m)) although it is not the first local: unsolved.
  The permuter finds that declaring that local first uncaches it (100 insns like the ROM) but the stack layout then differs.
- Tail merging: `if (c<0) {...compare; return} else {...same compare; return}` is cross-jumped by gcc into one tail; the ROM keeps both.  Written as `goto no; ... no: return 0; yes: return 1;` with labels
  the arms stay separate and the insn count matches (func_00214310: 190 -> 197).
- `lw`-class insns never sit in branch delay slots (neither ROM nor ours); mul.s/mtc1/cvt too.
- decomp-permuter finds *type* changes (volatile) and shared constant locals; `else if (&p->field || x) {A} else {A}` (always-true condition + duplicated arm) is a recurring permuter trick (func_0020F6B8).


COMPILER-BEHAVIOUR TOKENS ADDED THIS ROUND (all in wip/multfix.py, applied by wip/chkx.py; the lead must mirror them in the real build for the listed functions)
 @M  mult/div never in a branch delay slot (ROM scan: 0 of 20141 delay slots hold mult/div; 78 hold mflo/mfhi): func_0020BC50 EXACT (plus func_0028FEEC / func_002AA724 of other rounds).
 @Z  dead epilogue after an endless loop (gcc 2.7.2 keeps it, 2.8.1 mips function_epilogue skips it after a BARRIER): func_002018A0 EXACT with `-O2 -mcpu=r4000 @C @Z`.
 @L  gcc 2.7.2 delay-slot policy for `if (x == 0) return v;` (branch-likely, slot = copy of the 1-insn return block, the insn 2.8.1 puts in the slot stays after the branch; skipped when the slot insn is
     a `li`): func_00201E94 and func_00202018 EXACT with `-O2 -mcpu=r4000 @C @L`.
 Together with @C these show the low region (ROM 0x1000-0x38xx) was built by a gcc that behaves like 2.7.2 (reorg policy, epilogue after barrier, no cache-sched change) but has -msplit-addresses.

PINNED / PERMUTER-STYLE DELIVERIES (mark in results): func_00214310 (register f32 z asm("$f22"): only an f21/f22 swap of two FP temps separates the natural source (9 words)),
func_0020F6B8 (duplicate-arm `else if (&tgt->pos || b) {A} else {A}` from the permuter; not natural source).  Pinned partials kept in wip/hard/*.pin.c (func_0021035C, func_00212670, func_00206A20: 3-4 words left).

SOURCE-SHAPE LESSONS (more)
- Struct-by-value returns and `&local` quats: see ALLOCATION/CSE FACTS above (func_00202E4C family is the largest unsolved block: 202E4C, 203D4C, 2034A4, 214624 all call func_00202CA0/func_002742B4).
- Boolean `ok` values built by slot-filled `li v0,1 / move v0,zero` leaves (func_0020486C / func_00204A68): the ROM never initialises `ok` before the tests; its live ranges are only the branch edges, so `ok`
  gets v0.  Our if-chains / comma expressions keep `ok` in a2 or add 2-3 insns; not solved.
- A `static inline` classifier used twice (func_00215410: two copies of the same target classification in ROM, with `j L ; li a0,N` leaves) compiles to a different branch layout than the ROM when written as a
  function returning a value; written with nested positive tests and else-leaves it comes closer (275 vs 278 insns) but not exact.

==== r01 second pass (continuation session) ====
- BOOLEAN PREDICATE ('ok' built from slot-filled li/move v0 leaves) = a `static inline s32 pred(...)` with EARLY RETURNS (return 1 / return 0 per test), called as `if (pred(...)) {...}`.
  Each leaf then becomes `v0 = 1/0` in a delay slot and the caller's `beqz v0` follows the join (func_00204A68, func_0020486C, both EXACT).  The two (s8)state->unkCA compares must be ONE `||` expression
  (`if (a->h108 != (s8)s->unkCA || a->h10A != (s8)s->unkCA) return 0;`) so the sll/sra pair is computed once and the first bne becomes the ROM's bnel/move-v0-in-slot.
  The final float compare must be written TWICE (one `return (f32)x * scale <= field;` per arm of the if/else), not as `a = ..; b = ..; return a <= b`: otherwise gcc's scheduler hoists the second operand
  load above the mtc1/cvt/mul and the identical tails get cross-jumped (ROM has `mul.s f0 ; lwc1 f1 ; j join ; li v0,1` per arm and one shared `li v0,1 ; c.le.s ; bc1fl ; move v0,zero` tail).
- Reload after a store of another struct: `t = info->unk4.f;` copied to a local INSIDE the `if` removes the reload that gcc does after `state->f40 = 0.0f` (func_0020486C).
- Iterated permuter (wip/pdrive.py): re-base each round on the best output (measured with chk words-differ, not the permuter score); func_0021035C went 28 -> 2 words in one extra round.
- State-compare in A*-like node code: `x == 2 || x == 1` is merged by gcc into `addiu -1 ; sltiu 2`; the ROM keeps `xori v0,v1,2 ; beqz ; xori v0,v1,1 ; bnez` = write `((x ^ 2) == 0) || ((x ^ 1) == 0)` and `if ((x ^ 1) != 0) x = 1;` (func_0020C5A0, EXACT).
- Brute-force of the order of the head statements (all 40 legal permutations of 5 stores/call/add in ONE chk call) fixed the call-result forwarding (`s->unk24 = s->unk1C + s->unk20` right after `s->unk20 = f()` keeps the call result in $f0): wip/v*.txt + vx.py.
- INLINE HELPER WITH OWN LOCALS (m03 lesson confirmed, 3 more hits): func_00203D4C and func_00202E4C contain the same 4-line block "Vec3f v = state->vec130; Quat q = CA0(&v); 2742B4(&q, m)"; written as `static inline r01_mk(state, m)` that declares q and v itself,
  the &q address is re-materialised (`addiu a0,sp,N` twice) as in the ROM, where the same code written inline in the function caches it in a callee-saved reg.  The locals of the inline are allocated AFTER the caller's own locals
  (qB/vC end up above m2/m1/Q, matching the ROM frame).  func_002034A4's frame also matches with this trick (still PARTIAL, register-numbering).  Assign `info = &actor->def->info` BEFORE the inline call.
- Float literal operand in a function that already has the matching pool entry as an extern: when the first use in the ROM is a pool entry at the object's rodata address (chk prints ro=0x...:4) write the FIRST use as a literal (0.5f) and keep later
  duplicates as externs; chk does NOT compare the literal value, so check it with wip/fl.py (value of VA-0x80000000+0xC00).
- Clamp: `r = f(MAX(-1.0f, MIN(1.0f, x)))` with literal -1.0f/1.0f (NOT extern D_800C72C0/C4) reproduces the 3-compare bc1tl clamp of func_00216F44 / func_00215868 (b16 idiom; the extern version had 38 words off, the literal 6).
  The same angle-to-target body is inlined in func_00215868 / func_00215B90 / func_00214DD4 (the standalone func_00216F44 is the out-of-line copy of an `inline` function defined before its users).
- LOOP WITHOUT LOOP NOTES (goto loop): func_00210964's `for (; n < steps; n++)` hoisted `&ti` into a callee-saved reg; the ROM recomputes it each iteration.  Writing `if (n >= steps) goto done; top: ...; if (cond) goto cont; <break path>; goto done; cont: n++; if (n < steps) goto top; done:`
  gives the ROM layout (break path BEFORE the loop test, n++ merged into a branch-likely slot) and re-materialises &ti.  Also the three float params of the trace call are the RESULTS of func_0024D388/D274/E410 (m2c shows them as void calls).
- Early classification `kind` (inlined func_002169AC, b04's early-return source) in func_00214624/00215410: the result register of an inlined early-return function is a0 in the first (compare-only) copy of func_00215410; not reproduced (all source shapes give v1).
- Redundant null test after a call (`if (c) { f(c); if (c) add }`, ROM keeps the second `beqz s0`): gcc cse removes it; the permuter finds `int zero = 0; ... if (c != (T*)zero)` (func_002149C0).
- Permuter on structure-correct bases (<= 40 aligned diff lines) is cheap and effective: iterate (wip/pdrive.py) and then MINIMISE the output by hand (remove the junk `if (x || &y)`, `*(&v)`, dead copies; keep `do { } while (0)` wrappers, which matter).

==== r01 second pass: more lessons (continuation session) ====
TOOLS ADDED (wip/): pdrive.py (iterated permuter driver: `python wip/pdrive.py <secs> <rounds> fn[:startfile] ...`, re-bases each round on the best output measured with chk), bestcur.py (best candidate file per function),
 vxm.py (variants x several functions of ONE file in a chk call: needed for inline helper + out-of-line copy groups), alnv.sh (aligned/register-insensitive diff count: the right metric when the instruction count is off by one),
 loopv.py (rewrites a `for` into while / if+do-while / for(;;) shapes), fl.py (pool float value from VA), lit.py (extern pool floats -> literals; NOT always right, see below), evalall.py / finalize.py (results writer), verify_out.py.
GCC INTERNALS THAT EXPLAINED SEVERAL "UNSOLVABLE" ITEMS
- Callee-saved (fp and int) register ORDER follows allocno priority = floor_log2(n_refs)*n_refs/live_length (ties: lower pseudo number first).  n_refs is the count at local-alloc time, i.e. BEFORE combine merges `c = cosf(); c = -c;`
  (5 refs for c, 3 after rewriting `c = -cosf()`).  Dump with wip/dump.sh <file> (gcc -dg -dl): `;; N regs to allocate: ...` is the allocation order, `Register dispositions` the result.
  In func_00216F44 the ROM wants s=$f20 first although s has only 3 refs: an EMPTY asm use `__asm__("" : : "f"(s));` placed after the clamp (not before: the asm is a scheduling barrier) gives s one more ref, flips the order and emits nothing -> EXACT
  (flagged HACK in results; the same helper body then matches the three inlined copies in func_00215868 / func_00215B90 as well).
- sched1 moves register-only insns (e.g. `sub.s` of callee-saved FP pseudos, `addiu reg,sp,N`) freely ACROSS calls; loads from non-const globals stay on their side of a call, `extern const` loads can move down only.
- gcc inline expansion: every expansion of an inline function with a by-value struct param gets its own 16-byte param copy only if all expansions are in the SAME statement (temp slots are freed at statement end and reused otherwise): func_00215B90's
  three `f(a, B)` copies at sp+0x6C/0x7C/0x8C need `ang = (f(a,B) < 0.0f) ? -f(a,B) : f(a,B);`.
- "post-loop / found block placed BEFORE the loop" (func_0020D6E4, func_00215868): write the exit block textually before the loop and jump to it (`goto found`), loop as ordinary nested for loops (keeps loop notes); do not `break`.
- Literal pools: when the same constant is used several times in the ROM with separate pool entries the source needs separate externs (or per-use literals inside separate inline expansions); converting everything to literals merges them (func_002070D0: 401 insns instead of 408).
- The permuter's junk that matters: `a2 = actor;` (a copy pseudo for every access), `if (state) {A} else {A}` duplicate arms, `do { ... } while (0)` around bodies and around single blocks, a hidden zero variable to keep a redundant null test,
  changing a callee's return type (long long!) to change the temp registers of a call result.  After it finds EXACT: strip junk one item at a time (wip/vx.py with 3-4 variants) and keep what is needed.
