=== round-3 r08 findings ===
=================== FINDINGS from workspace r08 (round 3, main-image leftovers 0x29AE80..0x2AD65C + PARKED groups) ===================

TOOLING / VERIFICATION (affects every function that has a literal pool or a jump table)
- chk.py MASKS every relocation field, so for `lui at,%hi(D_800Cxxxx) ; lwc1 f,%lo(...)` the VALUE and the ORDER of the literal / jump-table
  entries are never compared.  A function whose text is "EXACT" can still have wrong literals or wrong switch-case -> label mapping.
  Example found here: func_002A9F18 (DrawTextCentered) was "EXACT" but its case bodies (24.0, 12.0, 8.0, 6.0, 16.0 ...) were in the wrong order
  (the ROM jump table maps case 3 to the 6.0 body; the draft mapped case 3 to the 8.0 body).  Fix = write the cases in the order of the ROM bodies.
- wip/lay.py <file.c> [flags]  (run through wip/lay) = pool/layout verifier.  It (1) compiles the object, (2) prints every symbol's offset and the delta
  against docs/game_funcs.txt (all deltas must be +0x0; a symbol that is not in the table = an extra out-of-line copy), (3) locates the object's
  .rodata through the hi/lo pairs that the ROM CODE encodes (not by byte search), resolves jump-table entries (R_MIPS_32 in .rodata) and compares
  the whole pool with the ROM bytes (trailing 4-byte alignment pad ignored), (4) checks every .rodata reference.  Prints LAYOUT OK / BAD.
  wip/done.py (wip/dn) = chk + lay + copy to wip/out + results line; it refuses to record a function when lay says BAD.
- ORIGINAL ASSEMBLER PLACES `li.s/li.d` LITERALS IN THE ASSEMBLY STREAM, interleaved with the `.rdata` jump tables/strings that GCC emits
  mid-function.  ROM of func_002A8A94 (ScriptStep): [37800000 3e800000 c2b40000][pad][JT1 7w][pad][JT2 6w][3f800000 ...].  Three literals used in the
  prologue come BEFORE the tables, the literal first used after the switch comes AFTER them.  tools/gas_compat.py appends the whole literal pool at the END
  of .rodata (after the tables), which cannot reproduce that.  Patch (this workspace, tools/gas_compat.py): env GAS_STREAM=1 emits each literal inline
  (`.section .rodata; .align 2|3; .LFLn: .word ..; .text`) at the li.s/li.d position; also honoured by the rodata_va path (finalize_rodata gets an
  empty Pool).  With it the group files of the UI object (func_002A8A94, func_002A9F18) match the ROM bytes exactly.  The lead's real build should use it
  (otherwise the objects that mix tables and literals come out reordered).  tools/permcc.sh passes PERM_ROVA / GAS_STREAM through.
- decomp-permuter on game code: the stock tools/perm_strip.py DROPS `static inline` helper definitions (the permuter then compiled a call to an
  undefined function: base score 2600 for func_0029BD38 and all literal-heavy functions).  Fixed (keeps static inline).  Also compare with ABSOLUTE rodata
  addresses on both sides (wip/pf2.sh: pool VRAM base from lay.py, D_800Cxxxx/jtbl_ in the target .s replaced by numbers, gas_compat given rodata_va):
  base scores become real (5..120) instead of noise.  Then the permuter solved func_0029C278 (fmod: `new_var = x - q*y; do { return new_var; } while (0);`)
  and func_002A3478 (`new_var = 4;` copy before the `if`) in minutes.  The permuter normalises branch targets, so a score of 0 can still be 1 word off
  (func_002A152C): always re-check with chk.
- wip/dd.py (wip/dd) = aligned side-by-side diff (difflib on normalised insns, relocs and branch targets masked): tells a real structural
  difference from a shifted-by-one cascade (chk's positional word count over-states diffs: func_0029C868 shows 39 words, aligned diff is 10).
  wip/vd.py (wip/vdq) ranks many source variants by the aligned diff, wip/hc.py hill-climbs on statement order, wip/fl = shortest round-trip float literal.
- git-bash cannot run python: use `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -- python3 ...`.  This tool layer turns `\\` into `\` inside heredocs: write python patch
  files with the Write tool when they contain backslashes.

COMPILER / SOURCE-SHAPE LESSONS
- -fno-schedule-insns: func_002A3478 (widget scale tree) is EXACT only with `-O2 -mcpu=r4300 -fno-schedule-insns` (aligned diff 29 -> 3).  Test this flag
  (and -fno-schedule-insns2) whenever the instruction MIX is right but the interleaving of independent FP chains / loads differs.  Recorded as flags=.
- float constants that are 255.0f / 1.0f / 1.6901408f ... and appear as `D_800Cxxxx` + `D_800Cxxxx+4` in the asm are LITERALS of the object (m2c prints them
  as externs).  With literals gcc CSEs equal constants and jump-threads compares against them (func_002A5DD8: `if (b == 255.0f) ... if (b != 255.0f)`
  jumps into the delay-slot/branch of the second compare; with 9 separate externs it can not).  Always read the pool values (wip/w/rw.py) first.
- early returns vs boolean value: `if (a != 0) return 1; if (f != 0.0f && o != 0) return 1; return 0;` gives ROM's `bnez ; li v0,1 ; move v0,zero`
  chains; a materialised boolean gives sltu (func_002A3F94).
- A backward `goto` loop is NOT a loop for gcc 2.8 (no NOTE_INSN_LOOP): the jump-table address is not hoisted (`lui at; addu; lw jt`), while a do/while hoists
  `la jt` into a callee-saved reg (func_002A5A50: the script interpreter loop needs `again: ... if (run) goto again;`).
- do { c->cur++; c->cur = c->cur % 4; ... } while: writing `cur++` and then `cur = cur % 4` (two statements) keeps both stores of a u16 field exactly like ROM
  (`sh; andi 0xFFFF; andi 3; sh`), `cur &= 3` loses the first store (func_002A80C4).
- K&R itoa (`if ((sign = n) < 0) n = -n; do { s[i++] = n % 10 + '0'; } while ((n /= 10) > 0); if (sign < 0) s[i++] = '-'; len = i; reverse...`) matches
  func_002A166C immediately: recognise the library routine, do not transcribe.
- empty `case 2: break;` + `q = o` copy before a 2-case switch reproduces the beq/slti/beqz compare tree (func_002A2FE8).
- do { c2 = lower(*b++); c1 = lower(*a++); } while (c2 && c2 == c1) = tail-recursive strcasecmp (func_002A13F4).
- Two stack-slot struct copies of 64 bytes (`mtx[a] = mtx[b]`) must be struct assignments of a 64-byte struct (lw t0..t3 / sw t0..t3), not word loops.
- `(D << 6) + (s32)who + 0x60` (sum order!) vs `&who->mtx[D]`: m2c prints the real expression order; copy it (func_002A52E4).

UNSOLVED (see results.txt notes and wip/hard)
- func_002AD65C: 1 word: the two arms `q->b = 3` / `q->b = 7` of an if/else are NOT cross-jumped in the ROM (each has its own `sb v0,0x603(v1)`), every identical-store
  shape gets merged by gcc; shapes that avoid the merge (different base expression) change the displacement.
- func_002A152C: ROM tests the OLD needle char on the mismatch path and re-reads *q on the fall-through path only (reload not CSE'd): 4 words (register swap of
  hv/w) with the right semantics, 1 word with a wrong-semantics draft.

LATER LESSONS
- chk MASKS ALL RELOCATION FIELDS: literal values, literal order and jump-table case->label mapping are never compared. wip/lay.py checks the whole pool
  (located via the ROM-encoded hi/lo pairs, jump-table entries resolved). func_002AB19C group had 2 real bugs found this way (AB854 jump-table mapping, AB794 D_800CB380 is literal 0.7f).
- The original assembler places literals in STREAM order interleaved with .rdata jump tables; tools/gas_compat.py needs GAS_STREAM=1 (patched) to reproduce it.
- K&R `s32 (*fn)()` function pointers leave a2 holding the loop terminator -1 (func_002AC6DC).
- The order of independent multiplies / stores in the ROM reveals source order (scheduler keeps source order among ready insns): func_0029D984; hill-climbing statement order solved func_0029D6A8.
- Matrix attach blocks: put in a `static inline` helper with its own `u8 m[0x40]` local so the address is re-materialised; `do{}while(0)` wrapper or duplicated if/else arms fixes callee-saved swaps (func_002A453C).
- Permuter: use `static inline` helpers (tools/perm_strip.py patched to keep them), non-static inline inflates base scores ~2600. Permuter normalises branch targets so score 0 may still be 1 word off; always re-chk.
- Backward goto vs loop, 002ABD04 inlined lookup: `{ s32 z = 0; lookup(h, z, ...) }`, struct { void *p; u8 b; } r;, and if (a0) {t=*a0; *out=NULL;} else {*out=NULL;}.
- Single biased pointer induction variable in ROM for 002A373C/002A3B48 (draft uses two pointer ivs): unsolved.

=== r08 session 2 (hard leftovers) : NEW TOOLS / LESSONS ===
SCHEDULING / ALLOCATION HAMMERS (all verified EXACT with chk + lay.py; flagged HACK in results.txt where used)
- `__asm__ __volatile__("")` is a TOTAL scheduling barrier in gcc 2.8 sched (volatile asm uses/clobbers all regs+memory).  Put right after a copy
  (`n = e; asm volatile("");`) and the copy becomes the first insn of the block, i.e. the filler of the preceding branch delay slot (func_002A35C0:
  86 -> 0 words).  An EMPTY NON-volatile `__asm__("" : "+r"(x))` only hides the value from cse (stops `move a0,a1` / copy propagation) and pins the insn
  order of x's definition before the asm (func_002A52E4: keeps an `addu` above the c.lt.s instead of in the bc1f slot).
- `register T v asm("$N")` pins (GPR $2..$23,$30 and FP $f1..$f23 all work in locals) solve pure register-role mismatches that declaration order cannot:
  func_0029BD38 (0.0 const -> $f1, inline result -> $f4), func_0029CDEC (result var -> $f1), func_0029C868 (a pinned COPY `r20 = cy` makes w = cy*cz in place),
  func_002A52E4 (arg pointer -> $5 plus a pinned temp $2), func_002A5588 (loop pointer -> $5), func_002A152C (loop char -> $3).  Pinned vars must NOT be
  loop induction variables; a pinned var in a macro/inline that is expanded several times pins every expansion (frame grew in func_002A0888 attempts).
- Delay-slot filling from the fall-through thread: when the ROM has `beqz x,L ; move a1,s0` and the target thread does NOT repeat `move a1,s0`, the
  source had the arg move FIRST in the then-arm (before the other arg): pin it (`register T *a1v asm("$5") = o; asm("" : "+r"(a1v));` inside the arm)
  and the reorg deletes the redundant move in the else arm (func_002ADA44).  Same shape: func_002A35C0 (n = e), func_002A0888.
- Identical single-insn tails of two arms are cross-jumped by jump.c even when the stored constants differ (li 3 / li 7 both end in the same sb):
  an empty `asm volatile("")` after the store in ONE arm stops the merge (func_002AD65C: ROM keeps both sb).
- local-alloc vs global-alloc: a variable that lives in one basic block only is allocated BEFORE the multi-block pseudos, so a block-local temp takes
  v0 and the global ends in v1.  Making the temp global (or pinning it) swaps them (func_002A152C).  In `v1 = 0x800FFFFF; v1 = hx & v1;` the
  user variable doubles as the mask register (`lui v1; ori v1; and v1,a0,v1`) - write the constant into the result variable first (func_0029BBA0).
- Live-range priority trick: priority = refs / live length.  A dummy `__asm__("" : : "r"(s))` placed AFTER the loops (before the join label, NOT at
  the label, which blocks branch-likely slot filling) lengthens s's life, lowers its priority and flips which of two pointers gets t0 (func_002A0888).
- Declaration order of FP locals DOES matter (func_002A5DD8: `f32 b, a;` vs `a, b`) - int locals mostly not.  Pseudo creation order decides ties.
- Statement order of ~7 independent stores / 3-5 statements: brute force all permutations in ONE chk call (60 variants ~20 s): func_002A5588 (store
  of unk34 hoisted), func_0029D4A0 (`hit = 1; tmp = out; out++; cnt += hit; inline(tmp,..)` places the addu between the FP adds).
- ROM literal-pool order is the ORDER OF FIRST USE IN THE SCHEDULED CODE: if two multiplies by different float literals are scheduled in the other order
  the pool differs even if chk (which masks relocs) says 0 words differ (func_002A8650: still open, 27.0 must precede 1.5).
TOOLING (workspace r08): wip/rv + wip/rv8.py (spec .py with BASE file + replacement lists -> one chk call), wip/hc + wip/hc8.py (decl-order hill climber,
  PERMLINES option), wip/s8.sh (side by side), wip/dump8.sh (gcc -dg/-dl dumps), wip/pmulti.sh (permuter setup+run for many functions).
  tools/perm_strip.py now keeps `static inline` helpers (before it dropped them, base scores of 2800 for every function using one).

=== r08 session 2 : more lessons (no hack needed) ===
- WORKFLOW THAT SOLVED 12 OF 20: compare with the ALIGNED diff (wip/dd.py = difflib on normalised insns, relocs/branch targets masked) after EVERY source change,
  never with chk's positional count (one missing/extra insn makes chk say 100+ words while the aligned diff says 4).  Fix the first aligned difference,
  re-run, repeat; when only reg numbers differ use pins, when only ordering differs use barriers/permutations (see above).
- func_002A373C / func_002A3B48 (vertex lerp, 259/275 insns): the draft's separate `o8 += 0x10; out += 8` pointers created a third giv.  Use ONE pointer `out`
  and re-derive `o8 = (u8 *)out;` inside the loop body; compute BOTH table-entry pointers (pa = tabA + i0*20, pb = tabC + i1*20) BEFORE the first call (the second one
  then lives in an extra callee-saved reg, which is what the ROM has: 8 saved regs); a copy `pc = (u8 *)e;` used only for the `e->a0` byte reads gives the ROM's
  `move s0,s4` and its separate base register; the colour channels are swapped in the else arm (o8[0xC] <- c[2], o8[0xE] <- c[0]), which is also why the loads
  came in the "wrong" order.  `acc = 0.0f;` must be the FIRST statement for the mtc1 position.  No pins/asm needed.
- float literal passed twice (func_002A8650): writing `s0->unk14 + sc * 27.0f` and `sc * 1.5f` as argument EXPRESSIONS at both call sites (instead of named locals) makes cse share
  them across the calls AND emits the literal loads in source order (27.0 before 1.5), which fixes the literal pool order.  Named locals reversed the pool order.
- `u16 m3 = mode - 3;` compared twice keeps the andi at the use; a pinned u32/s32 assigned as `(u16)(mode - 3)` puts the andi at the definition (func_0029B324).
  `a * 0xC0 + 4` with known-zero low bits is canonicalised into `ori`; hide the multiply result with an empty asm("+r") to get the ROM's `addiu`.
- sllv: `sll v1,v0,3` can be forced to the ROM's `sllv v1,v0,a0` (a0 = the constant 3 reg shared with a compare and a store) by pinning the constant (`register s32 k3 asm("$4") = 3`)
  and writing the multiply as `((x << k3) - x) << k3` (func_002A75D0).  A micro test showed gcc produces the register form by itself only for some uses of the product.
- A `static inline` helper that is also a real function elsewhere in the ROM must be `static` (plain `inline` emits an out-of-line copy and wip/lay.py reports a foreign symbol).
- Still open (best drafts in wip/hard): func_002A2BE0 (lw a0 should be the LAST of the four arg loads: no shape found), func_002A2270 (ROM has two extra mov.s copies of the loaded
  D_800D2BE8.cur, cse always removes them), func_0029D288 (inline-param copy of c not coalesced in the ROM), func_002AC3F0 / 002AA724 / 002A5A50 / 002A81B0 / 002ACBCC /
  002A98E0 / 002A4DC4 / 0029AE80 / 0029E510 / 002A78F0 (big, structure differs), func_002A4708 (431 insns display-list function, not attempted).

=== round-3 q04 findings ===
FINDINGS workspace q04 (overlay A, round 3: leftovers of o05/o06 workers)
=========================================================================
TOOLING (WSL, ~2 s per check instead of 30 s): tools/qc.sh (chk without the full rsync), tools/qs.sh+tools/sd.py (side-by-side with
objdump, shows only !! lines that differ), tools/vv.py (variant search: one file with @@{ alt1 @@| alt2 @@} groups expands to the product,
one chk call, best kept in wip/_best_<fn>.c), tools/perm.sh/pq.sh (decomp-permuter setup+queue, tools/perm_strip.py now keeps `inline`
functions).  The WSL box is shared with other workers (150 permuter processes), permuter runs of 5-10 minutes rarely converge.

NEW LESSONS
1. SWITCH ARMS THAT ONLY SET AN ARGUMENT FOR ONE COMMON CALL: write the CALL IN EACH ARM (f(3) / f(7) / f(10)); gcc cross-jumps only
   `jal + delay slot` and keeps the per-arm `li a0,K` blocks (even identical ones).  `a = K; ... f(a)` after the switch merges the
   identical arms (30-32 insns instead of 38) -- func_00438BD0, func_00438CBC (shape `if (c1) { if (c2) {switch} else f(10); } else f(10);`);
   case body order = ROM block order (case 1 first).  The permuter's `new_var2 = 3;` before `case 4:` hack gave a FALSE zero (branch
   offsets are not scored): always chk.
2. Load after store ordering needs the loaded object to be an array/struct member:  `extern void *D_800D74D0[]; ... D_800D74D0[0]` keeps the
   load AFTER `D_x->f14 = w` (func_004366C4, func_00437074).  A global struct pointer `D_800E5690->f14` is loaded each time anyway.
3. f32 scalar global written through a pointer (`*pf = 255.0f`): the ROM keeps the store BEFORE a struct-member load; with a plain scalar the
   store sinks into a branch delay slot.  Declare the float as the first member of a struct array (`typedef struct {f32 f; s32 pad[3];} O05F;
   extern O05F D_800D15F0[]; D_800D15F0->f = 255.0f`): MEM_IN_STRUCT stores stay in order and the symbol base register is the address
   itself (func_00438A08, 0(s0) with s0 = lui/addiu).
4. Pass-through arguments: callee prototype `s32 f(s32 a)` called as `f(a)` (the ROM keeps a0 untouched): func_00442254 only matched with
   func_0043F290(a) (K&R `f()` gave the wrong slot order).
5. Register numbering swap of two pseudos in a switch (`lw a0,4(s0); move v1,v0` vs `lw v1; move a0`): a dead duplicated branch inside one
   case (`case 3: if (p) { *(s8*)p = (s32)v; break; } else { *(s8*)p = (s32)v; break; }`) changes the pseudo priority (found by the
   permuter, func_00443120).
6. idx byte store `D_80146410[i].b7 = i` (150-byte rows) wrote the sll/addu in the wrong order; `((u8 *)D_80146410)[i * 150 + 7] = i` fixed
   the schedule (func_00436898/00436AD8).
7. FLOAT CONSTANTS D_800E1xxx..D_800E3xxx ARE LITERALS (main-image .rodata pools of the overlay objects), not externs: with externs the scheduler
   moves the loads freely and the FP register numbering never matches; with real literals (read the value at ROM = VA - 0x80000000 + 0xC00,
   print with tools/fl.py, e.g. 1.0f 1.6901408f 1.0810812f 0.0035211267f 0.0045045046f 0.21621622f) func_0043C260, 00442D18, 0043FFAC,
   00441384 matched at once.  CHK MASKS THE RELOC OF EVERY lui/lwc1: a wrong literal still says EXACT.  Always run tools/rochk.py
   (`./rochk file.c fn rom ro_rom`) which resolves jump-table words (.text + addend) and compares every rodata word with the ROM
   (found 3 wrong-but-EXACT parked sources: func_00435958/00436224/00439F38 had their jump tables permuted; case-BODY order = text order,
   the table order is the case-VALUE order, so `case 0: A; case 2: A; case 1: B;` and the like are needed).
   chk's `ro=...:24` for a 20-byte table includes 4 trailing pad bytes (junk in the ROM, e.g. 0xFF/0x1/0x3E): real pieces are 20 bytes.
8. u8-typed `(b->flags & 0x3FE0)` switches whose arms assign the same value leave a DEAD `lw v0,8(a1); andi v1,v0,0x3FE0` pair at the top (the
   compare branches are removed after flow): func_00441D50 starts with it; to reproduce, put the SAME statements in every case arm (the
   rect init `r.x0 = 0x400; ...` in 4 arms) so jump opt merges them.
9. Panel/Item family (include/q04_types.h): 0x28-byte items (Q04Item), 0x24-byte rect info (Q04Info), by-value copies `cp = *it` give the
   4-word loop + 2-word tail; locals that sit in memory are laid out FIRST-DECLARED = LOWEST ADDRESS, structs with a leading unused field are
   needed to get the ROM offsets (sz {f,w,h,g} at 0x28, r {f,x0,x1,y0,y1} at 0x38, info at 0x50).  `tp = (T*)(i*0x24 + (s32)base)` (index
   first) gives `addu a0,a2,v0`; `i = n; it = items; i--; if (i != -1) do {...; i--; it++} while (i != -1)` is the only form that
   keeps `n - 1 != -1` from folding into `n != 0`; `if (state < 3) if (state >= 0)` (nested, no braces) avoids the unsigned range fold.
10. Static inline helper taking a pointer to a local struct (`fin(&cp, ...)` reading `*(s32*)q->f14`) reads the field through the pointer
   register (`lw v0,0x14(a0)`) instead of folding to `0x2C(sp)`: func_00440618.
11. A constant address held in a callee-saved reg across calls needs the pseudo SET TWICE (`g = D_80146398;` before the first call and again
   before the second); gcc otherwise rematerialises it (func_0043AD20).  `*(&D)` second use through a separate local avoids reg reuse.
12. Permuter finds: (a) `do { ... } while (0);` around a block (func_0044632C 21 -> 12 words), (b) dup `if (c) {x} else {x}` inside a loop makes gcc
   strength-reduce a byte-stride giv (needs >= 2 address uses; func_0043BB4C, func_00444024: `for (j=21;j>=0;j--) { if (row->f) a[j]=0; else a[j]=0; }`),
   (c) `k = row->f` used as a call-argument assignment `f(.., k = row->f)` changes the arg register numbering.
13. Struct-pointer global as the loop `for (;;)` index base: `p = D_800E54A4->recs` copied into a local that is re-assigned after a call (second set) keeps a
   callee-saved copy (func_00434604: 132 insns, 4 words).
14. A constant address held in a local (u8 *g = D_80146398) that is assigned twice is rematerialised by gcc (REG_EQUIV needs 2 sets); a single set gets folded away.
15. Loops over big struct arrays want a pointer giv row (O05Q *row = &D_80146398[i]; row->b78) rather than index arithmetic; plus an explicit offset giv (off = 0x1370).
16. Single-float globals in 16-byte-stride arrays: declare typedef struct{f32 f; s32 pad[3];} O05F; extern O05F D_800D15F0[]; and write D_800D15F0->f (matches 0043 8A08, AA70 shape).
17. Permuter tricks that proved useful: new_var, (off=0) wrappers. Permuter scores are not comparable to chk word counts; always re-run chk, and a lower permuter score can be worse. A fake zero via an invalid new_var2 transform is not a match.
18. perm.sh SKIP must drop leading instruction lines (fixed); perm_strip.py must keep inline functions (fixed).
19. Functions needing one more callee-saved register than we allocate (e.g. 0043407C, frame 0x80 vs 0x88) usually hold an address-of-local (&sp38) in a saved reg across calls; passing it through several calls is not enough, needs the address used >=2 times in the loop.

===================== q04 (second session) additions =====================
TOOLING (wrappers in the workspace root and tools/; all run through WSL, ~2-4 s per call, work with the shared ~/trw_q04 tree)
- ./c.sh <list>   = chk (copies only the listed files);  ./s.sh <file.c> <fn> <rom> [flags]  = side-by-side (ours | ROM);
  ./v.sh <variants.txt> <fn> <rom> [flags] = many full-file variants separated by '//====' lines with @@{ a @@| b @@} product groups, ONE chk call,
  best variant copied back to wip/_best_<fn>.c  (CAUTION: an old v.sh bug copied a STALE _best file when the shell wrapper was wrong; always
  re-run c.sh on the file you intend to deliver);  ./gs.sh file.c = raw gcc -S;  ./rk.sh = rochk (literal/jump-table check).
- tools/ad2.py <file.c> <fn> <rom>: ALIGNED diff (difflib on mnemonics): prints "structural diff words" (insn insert/delete) and "operand diff words"
  (register/immediate differences of aligned insns).  chk's "N words differ" is useless for big functions because one early extra insn shifts
  everything (00441764: 248 words -> after fixing 5 spots EXACT); drive the work by the structural number first, then operand diffs.
- decomp-permuter: tools/pq3.sh <secs> <jobs> fn:skip ... (queue), tools/pqh.sh + perm_h.sh for HIDDEN functions (no glabel; target.s is cut from the
  asm by address, see wip/perm_func_0043C868.s).  The scores are dominated by relocation noise (base scores 1000-15000), only the relative improvement
  matters.  It solved 00434604 and 0043B03C (both after the structure was already identical: ad2 structural = 0).  Big bases (500+ insns) do not converge.

TARGET LIST FACTS
- func_0043DEDC "928 insns" was bogus (the symbol is a 40-insn function); func_0043C4F4 "1046 insns" is a SPAN of 21 functions (see results.txt): the
  splitter glued everything between two glabels.  Members: a 33-case switch returning handler pointers, cheat-code menu cursor helpers, text-entry
  buffer insert/delete (D_800E5CA0 = {cur, blink timer f32, len, char text[]}), strcmp-with-xor of the entered text.  Jump tables of the members are
  NOT contiguous in .rodata (other data in between) and the ROM has pad words after a table whose entry count is odd (0x1D etc.), so verify
  tables with rochk per member (only the pad word differs).
- Several targets start with stray head words that are scheduled-prologue-looking but are junk (0043A0A4: 3 words `addu s3,zero,zero; addu s6,s3,zero; lw s4,0x4B4(a1)`;
  00437C28: `j ..; nop`; 0043EFCC 1 word; 00444024 3 words; 0044632C 1 word): the real entry is the first `addiu sp`.

CODEGEN LESSONS (all verified EXACT)
1. PASS-THROUGH ARGS EXPLAIN REGISTER CHOICES: when a function's temporaries avoid a1/a2 (use v0,v1,a3,t0..t3) the unused params are live because they are
   passed on to the final call: func_00443298(a,b,c) { ...; func_0024A1C0(a,b,c); }.  Likewise `lw a1,0x58(a1); beqz a1` followed by `jal f` with a1
   untouched means the tested value is the 2nd argument of f (func_00444FA0: func_0028B2D4(D_8011FE88, t)).  Look at every jal whose a1..a3 are not set.
2. cse deletes the first of two ADJACENT stores to the same address (`p->x = A; p->x = B;` -> only B).  When the ROM keeps both, another store/insn sits
   between them in the source (scheduling moves it away later).  Brute force: try each following statement as the in-between one (00444024 -> 117 insns).
3. A load/store of a stack-argument constant sequence: `f(a,b,c,d,1,0,0,0,0,(u32)(x*192.0f)&0xFF)` writes the constant stack args BEFORE the FP computation of
   the last arg; computing the arg in a separate statement before the call reorders everything (00441D50).
4. `tt = sinf(...)` as a SEPARATE statement makes the call come first and the following f30*f34 loads/muls land after it (caller-saved f2) instead of
   a callee-saved f21 product before the call (00441764).  `(u32)f & 0xFF` argument inside two identical arms: `if (flags & 0x10) call(...) else call(...)`
   (identical arms are real in the ROM, gcc cross-jumps parts of them).
5. Index form `q + k * 0x64 + off` (instead of a walking pointer q += 0x64) gives the ROM's second giv register copy (`addiu v0,v0,8 ; move a0,v0`) (00444FA0).
6. `x = *(g); ... D[idx]` style: arrays of structs whose fields are read with `lui at; addu at,at,off; lw sym(at)` (byte offset giv, NOT a pointer giv)
   mean DIFFERENT extern arrays per field (D_0044FB90[k].name, D_0044FB98[k].mask, D_0044FB9C[k].snd ...) each with the full 16-byte stride struct;
   one struct array makes gcc build a pointer giv (hidden func_0043CFD8, hidden func_0043CFD8).
7. A global that the ROM addresses through a hoisted base register while the same symbol is used as scalar elsewhere: declare an alias with an asm label
   `extern u32 q4_g62CC[] __asm__("D_801462CC"); q4_g62CC[0] ^= m;` (hidden functions D2B4, D164, CFD8) - the scalar and the alias can live in one file.
8. Match flag assigned only at the exits (`goto match / nomatch` blocks that set ok right before `if (ok)`) stays in v0; assigning it before the strlen calls puts it in a
   callee-saved register (hidden func_0043CFD8).
9. Struct-pointer chains `x = o->f20; if (x != 0) {...}` that the ROM implements as `lw v1; beqz v1; move s6,v1` mean the copy is made AFTER the test:
   `if (o->f20 != 0) { x = o->f20; ...` (00440F10/004409A8).
10. goto-into-shared-tail: the three "else { func_002A125C(...); common widget block }" arms of 00437C28 are cross-jumped by gcc (write the whole arm 3 times; a goto to
   one shared label keeps `D_800E5830` in a0 wrongly).
11. permuter finds worth knowing: `unsigned char new_var = 1;` used as the constant in `s1 != new_var` / `s1 == new_var` (0043B03C, a constant that lives in a byte variable
   changes the global allocation), `new_var = r == 0; if (new_var)` (00434604, stops jump threading of a repeated test), `tab = p` assignment inside a condition.
12. Stack / literal facts: a double literal (2^32 for u32 -> double) in a pool starting at an address that is not 8-aligned relative to our one-function object shows a
   pad word in the ROM that a single-function compile cannot reproduce (00441764: chk EXACT, rochk differs only by that pad).

UNRESOLVED NEAR MISSES (best files in wip/hard/): 0043B8C8 (1 word: addu operand order of the cached base+so sum), 004438A4 (3 words, mul.s dest f0/f1), 0043BB4C (3), 00445B98 (4: lbu result v1 vs a1),
00445A58/00445CE8 (siblings, lbu v1 + move a1,v1 in the jal slot), 0043B67C (9), 004409A8 (8: order of f30/f34 loads after sinf), 00437C28 (20: evaluation order of the two index chains of the
func_002656A8 call and the move s0,v0 slot), 0044632C (12: inlined strncmp temps a2/a3), 0043F290 (14: type/p registers v1/a2 of the inlined getter), 00433AF0 (12: c1/giv s0/s1), 00441D50 (FP register
numbering of the first half), 00444024 (store order of the prologue / a1,a2 roles), 00440F10 (45: scheduling), 0043F69C (583 insns, structurally ~28 diffs), 0043407C (needs the loop rewritten).
13. (found last, solved 00445B98/00445A58/00445CE8 at once) ONE VARIABLE for the argument value and the call result:
    `s32 v = D[0]; v = f(b, v, 8, 0, 0xFF, 0); if (v == 0xF7) v = 0xF8; D[0] = v;`  gives `lbu a1,0(s0)` (or `lbu v1; ... move a1,v1` in the jal slot when
    nothing else needs a1) and keeps v in a1 afterwards; `s32 v = f(b, D[0], ...)` made v a fresh pseudo in v1.  Whenever the ROM keeps a value in an
    argument register across/after a call that consumed it, look for a variable that is both an argument and a later result (same for u8 -> `u8 v` was worse: use s32).
14. BRANCH-LIKELY loop tails (`bnezl v0,top ; addiu v1,v1,-1`, hidden func_0043C868): when the ROM has the induction update in a bnezl slot AND the same update just
    before the loop, the source has the update as the FIRST statement of the loop body (`i = start; for (n = 0; n < 11; n++) { i--; ... }`): gcc rotates the loop, copies the
    first insn into the preheader and into the likely slot.  Writing `i = start - 1; for (...; n++, i--)` gives a plain bnez with the decrement in an ordinary slot.

=== round-3 q01 findings ===
FINDINGS from workspace q01 (round 3, overlay A leftovers; gcc 2.8.1 -O2 -mcpu=r4300)
=================================================================================
New general lessons (all verified EXACT on the named functions)

1. FRAME-LOCAL STRUCT FORCES A STACK SLOT.  A local that lives in memory in the ROM although it is not address-taken in an
   obvious way (spilled "mode"/"pad" words at fixed sp offsets, e.g. 0x18(sp) reloaded after every call) is a TWO-WORD (or
   larger) local struct: `struct { s32 m; s32 pad; } mm;`.  One-field structs and 1-element arrays stay in registers.  Declaring
   all the stack scratch of the function as ONE struct reproduces the slot order too (func_00407290 pad.v/freeb/cnt = 0x20/0x24/
   0x28; func_004082FC {pad, v}; func_004085E0 {m,pad}; func_004106F4 {sp38, sp3C, buf} = 0x38/0x3C/0x40: moving three spilled
   scalars into such a struct also freed $fp for the index argument and fixed the whole prologue).
2. DO NOT REUSE ONE USER VARIABLE FOR UNRELATED LIVE RANGES when the ROM shows [li a0,N][jal; move sX,v0 in the delay slot]
   (i.e. the copy of the first call's result sits in the delay slot of the SECOND call and the arg setup is in front).
   With one pseudo used for two things (e.g. `s1 = f(x); ... s1 = g(y)` or `fs0 = lit; ... fs0 = call()`), sched1 orders
   [mov][li] and reorg then puts the `li a0` in the slot.  Distinct locals (m1, r2, fs1) give the ROM order
   (func_004069F4, func_00401980).
3. `if (0) { found: ...; goto after; }` / `else if (0) { label: ... }` PLACES A GOTO-ONLY BLOCK AT ITS TEXTUAL POSITION.  A ROM
   basic block that sits far away from its only user (func_00410B94/func_004106F4: the "palette entry found" block lies
   between the arms of an if-else chain that precedes the loop) is source text that comes before the loop; write the loop with a
   `goto found` and put the labelled block into a dead `else if (0)` arm of the preceding chain.  The loop itself must be
   a do-while (loop notes) with a normal back edge, not a goto loop (goto loops lose the invariant hoist of the mask `lui`).
4. `n = f(); s = (s32)p; ...` copies of ZERO: the ROM sometimes has `addu s6,s5,zero` instead of `addu s6,zero,zero`.  Written as
   `pal = 0; s6 = (s32)pal; s2 = (s32)pal;` (statements after the first test) gcc keeps the register copies (cse does not
   propagate the constant when the source is a pointer cast).  `s3 = 0; s2 = s3;` placed AFTER an if/else join keeps the copy too
   (func_004069F4: written inside the condition `(s3 = 0, ...)` it was folded to `li`).
5. In-place `if (t < 0) t += 7; s4 = t >> 3;` (not `t / 8`) reproduces `bgez a0,L ; sw (slot) ; addiu a0,a0,7`: the divide-by-8
   expansion otherwise allocates a second register and a branch-likely form.  A statement that must fill that delay slot
   (here `sp3C = sp38`) goes BETWEEN the multiplication and the shift.
6. A pointer-chasing `p = base + i*K` where the ROM computes `addu a1,s1,v1` (index in the first operand) needs the index term
   first AND no named copy: write `(T *)(idx * 0x1C + (s32)base)` at each use (func_00410B94: the named `off = idx*0x1C` created
   a second pseudo and a `move s1,v0` copy in the prologue; the repeated expression is CSEd into the callee-saved register).
7. Global-allocation priority (log2(refs)*refs/live_length, highest first, lowest free register first) decides s-register order;
   `gcc -dg/-dl` dumps (see wip/wdump.sh) print "regs to allocate" in that order and the dispositions.  Use it when two
   callee-saved registers are swapped: the pseudo with the higher priority gets the lower register.  Adding/removing a reference or
   shortening a live range (fresh variable) moves a pseudo up or down.
8. GROUP FILES WITH `inline` MEMBERS: to get the ROM layout ECB0, ED28(inline, open-coded into EE64/EF24), EDB8, EE64, EF24, F004
   (func_0040ECB0 group) declare EVERY member except the first `inline` (non-static): all out-of-line copies are then deferred and
   emitted in definition order; with only ED28 `inline` it was emitted last (wrong ROM span layout).
9. Dispatcher template (overlay class/mode event functions) `for (i = 0; T[i].fn != 0; i++) if (T[i].id == id) if (T[i].key == o->key
   || T[i].key == 0x7530) return T[i].fn(o,id,a2,a3,a4);` matched func_00412624 immediately (36 insns).
10. Index form vs pointer form: `for (n = 0; n < 3; n++) if (a->pool[n]) free(a->pool[n]);` with the array INSIDE the struct gives
    the ROM's `s1 counter / s0 pointer` assignment where a hand-written pointer walk had them swapped (func_00405CDC).
11. 'n = 0;' after the last use of a node pointer kills cse equivalence (func_0040170C); compound FP assignment keeps result in place.
12. Phi-copy: separate temps pv/pp assigned then copied (func_00406434).
13. Comparing against the same 'zero' variable (zero = 0; if (r != zero) ...; if (r == zero) ...) reproduces shared compare regs (func_00404958).
14. asm-label alias extern (extern s32 X[] __asm__("D_8011FECC")) yields the 'la v1; lw 0(v1)' base form (func_00405DC0).
15. Register pins (PINNED) were needed for 4070F4, 184C, 37E8, A640 only after plain-C variants failed.
16. Struct-return functions: v0 move placement remains the main residual in 401564/403658.
Near-misses left (<=10 words): 4009F4, 410B94, 6178, 7290, 7578, 9AEC, 413BD4, C11C, 401564 (scheduling/allocation).

=== q01 round-3 session 2 additions (overlay A leftovers) ===
17. HOW TO READ WHY THE SCHEDULER PUT TWO INSNS IN THE OTHER ORDER (worked for 410B94, 403458, 40C11C):
    dump the RTL (wdump: gcc -dL = after loop.c, -dS = after sched1, -dR = after sched2, -ds = after cse).  Equal-priority insns keep
    their RTL order, so the question "why is X before Y" becomes "which RTL order did the ROM have".  loop.c inserts hoisted
    constants (movables) just before the loop-start note, i.e. AFTER every source-level copy that precedes the loop, but before copies
    that loop.c itself creates (strength-reduction init of a pointer giv).  func_00410B94: ROM preheader is [lui mask][a2=limit copy][v1=ptr];
    ours was [v1][a2][lui].  Cure: declare the mask in a local BEFORE the copies (`u32 mask = 0xFF000000; n2 = t;`) so the li is a normal
    insn that precedes them, and index the array (`pal[a0]`) instead of a hand-made walking pointer so loop.c creates the pointer giv last.
18. A delay-slot insn in the ROM that belongs to the then-block ("lw s3,4(v0); beqz s3,L; addiu v1,v0,8") means the source computed it
    BEFORE the test: `cnt = n->unk4; ents = n + 8; if (cnt != 0) {...}` (func_00403458; with the add inside the if the call result was
    copied to another register first).
19. Max/min written `if (c < d) c = d; store c;` instead of `store (c > d ? c : d)` changes only the order of two loads (func_0040C11C).
20. Struct-return targets: `L.a = f()` where the destination is a struct MEMBER / array element / *p goes through a stack temp that is
    block-copied (lw/sw x3) afterwards; a plain local `a = f()` passes &a as the hidden pointer directly.  Temps are freed at the END OF
    THE ENCLOSING BLOCK, and a later struct-return temp reuses a freed slot -> `{ L.a = f(); }` in its own braces frees the slot early
    (func_00403658; frame layout a/b/dir/res are members of one local struct with 4-byte pads so that each Vec3 sits 16 bytes apart).
21. `do { ... } while (0);` around a statement can be what the ROM has even though it looks silly: it creates loop notes that cut cse
    paths (constant 1 not shared between two arms, func_00406F7C) and it changed the tail scheduling of struct copies
    (func_00401564: `do { *out = res; } while (0); return out;` puts the `addu v0,s2,zero` AFTER the block copy).  Tools: wip/hc.py
    (hill climber: adjacent statement swaps, do-while wraps of 1..3 statements at every nesting level, compare flips, commutative operand
    flips, scalar<->array toggles of D_ externs; objective = chk score) found 40C11C, 40067C progress, 408870 progress.
22. Division expands to ONE insn (div + zero/overflow checks in the template), so `div` does NOT split basic blocks for the scheduler.
23. The os.vi mode setter func_0040BE38 is an OSViMode edit (memcpy of a table entry + hStart/vStart half-word arithmetic).  Locals that the
    ROM keeps on the stack as 4-byte halfword pairs are `union { u32 w; struct { s16 s, e; } r; }` members of ONE local struct; dead stores
    to a local (`d.s = t0; ... d.s = t3`) are only kept by gcc when another store to the same object lies between them.

=== round-3 q02 findings ===
- Volatile-ness: ROM keeps 'D=0; D=1' double stores -> declare that global volatile locally (cse otherwise deletes the first store); switch tree 'beq 1; slti 2' = s32 switch var (u32 gave a flat chain)
- Cross-jump of two arms needs identical register use: a local copy 't = tex' put a mov in one arm and broke the merge (func_004173FC); write the whole push list in both arms
- switch(x){case 0:..case 1:..} with ROM tree 'beq 1; slti 2; beqz; bnez v' needs hidden 'case 2: break;' (cases 0,2,1 order irrelevant); an extra reg copy 'addu a1,a0' for one arm's arg = a local 'p = o;' used only in that arm (func_0041AB9C)
- permuter works well for pure s2/s3 swaps: it added a dead assignment 'row = t->w != 0' inside an && condition (func_00419510). perm_strip.py must be the o01-patched one (keeps static inline helpers) or the base score is garbage
- Base-register vs folded giv: access via an explicit local pointer 'p = D_80146398; p->f78 = 1; p->f7F = 0' keeps p as base (offsets 0x78/0x7F in the insns), while D_80146398[0].f78 folds the member offset into the address register; in a loop use 'p = &cfg->pl[i]' inside the loop body (func_00424128). Order of stores to a global byte vs pointer stores: write the global byte as ((u8*)ARRAY)[-0xC3] so gcc cannot reorder it with the pointer store
- addu operand order (base,index) vs (index,base): a static inline accessor 'O3Pj *pj(i){return &D[i];}' gives base-first; D[i].f gives index-first; permuter found '(u8*)D - (-(idx*0x4C8))' for one more site (func_004208C0)
- chk RELOC-MISMATCH can hide behind 'N words differ': an earlier draft that is only a few words off may still use a wrong table index (func_00420378 used e[1].id for ROM e[0]); once the words match, the reloc check fires
- Table address with constant element offset: if ROM has lui %hi(D_800E42EE) i.e. symbol+0x1A and a giv starting at idx*36, the table row is a struct with sub-arrays (row.c[i].id) so the member offset is folded into the symbol, not into the giv (e[6+i] folds +0x18 into the giv init) (func_004204A8)
- s2/s3 callee-saved swap fixed by assigning the new value back to the SAME variable then copying: 'cur = (cur+1) % n; nxt = cur;' (func_00420214); callee prototype with u16 id param gives lhu from an s16 table field
- 'p = &ARRAY[i];' written INSIDE a counted loop gives gcc a pointer giv initialised with the symbol address (lui;addiu before the loop) and keeps the counter un-reversed; a pointer walked with p++ makes gcc reverse the loop (bgez countdown), ARRAY[i].f gives lui at/addu at (func_0041EB34, func_00424128)
- Branch layout: when ROM puts the 'call' arm inline after the compare and the other arm at the end of the function, write the arms in that order with the INVERTED condition (if (idx >= count) {call...} else {...}); zero/clamp arms: positive test first, else-zero last (func_00419B8C)
- Jump table walking pointer (lw v0,0(s1); s1 += 4) with 10 entries 0..9 where the ROM table entry 0 points to the default block located LAST: 'case 0: default:' placed last in the switch (func_0042144C; the old 'ro=0x208C0E' was garbage, real ro=0xE21C0:40)
- Scheduler hoists LOADS freely but keeps STORE order of the source: when the ROM stores to unrelated globals (swc1 x4) before 'sw v1,0(a1)' (the g++ store), put 'g = D_80110634++;' after those statements (func_0041DD30)
- ROM alignment padding in .rodata/.text between Acclaim objects is junk, not zero (stray code words before functions; '%d' string followed by 0x86 at ROM 0xE2083): a per-object build can never reproduce those pad bytes; they must be emitted as raw .byte/.word (func_0041BF10)
- ROM with TWO distinct 'return 0' blocks (early bgtz -> L248 'addu v0,zero,zero', and 'bne ...,L24C; (delay v0=0)' skipping it): write 'if (c) goto ret0; ... if (x != 3) return 0; ret0: return 0;' (func_00422E54). A local 'q = D_80146894[]' pointer keeps the symbol base in a callee-saved reg (addiu a0,s0,-0x180C, sw -0x1C(s0))
- permuter 'best' score != 0 can still be chk-EXACT (score also counts non-code diffs): ALWAYS run pfix.py + chk on the best output of every perm dir. Reusing an unused parameter (a3) as the local 'key' fixed a col/key a1/a2 swap (func_0041E2F0)
- Two arms ending in the same call + a third arm falling into it: write ONE call after the if/else (not one per arm) when the ROM shows a shared tail block reached by fallthrough and 'j' (func_0041F738). Calls whose side-effect stores precede them in the ROM (sw state/timer in the jal delay slot) must be written BEFORE the call (func_0041F738 case 5)
- Value computed in two arms then passed to ONE call: write the call in BOTH arms (the arg chain then lives in a0 and gcc cross-jumps jal) - fixes register of 'r % 4' style chains and gives 'j LDEC; li a0,1' layouts instead of delay-slot tricks (func_00422C20). ad.py (aligned diff, wip/ad.py) shows the real few diffs behind big 'N words differ' counts
- Replacing goto-based shared tails ('idx = ..; goto set;') with a direct call in every arm lets gcc cross-jump exactly like the ROM (func_00419760, EXACT at first try)
- 'lh' narrowing: inline '(f18 - t*4)' narrows to lhu; split into 'r = f18 - t*4; f14 = f14 + r;' to keep lh. Constant shared between a stored value and a call arg: reuse via 'g->w0' to avoid gcc rematerialising it (0041C864 still loads twice).
- Permuter with -DNOFILL: add 'export GAS_NOFILL=1' to the perm dir compile.sh. Remaining near-misses (4245C0, 423C18, 4196C0) are pure RA/scheduling; renaming second FP block temps made 4245C0 worse (18 words).
- ROUND 3 NEW (q02): asm('') as a scheduling barrier: `__asm__("");` right after a store statement changed the order of two independent insns in the same block (sh before addiu a0,sp,N) and made func_00423C18 EXACT (permuter-style 'do{}while(0)' did not). Use when ROM keeps source order of an independent store / address setup.
- ROUND 3: gDP-style macro expansions reproduce MIPS code that looks random: CALC_DXT(w) = (2048 + MAX(1,w/div) - 1) / MAX(1,w/div) written with ternary MAX/MIN macros that RE-EVALUATE their args (D_800E32A4->w each time). The ternary diamonds defeat gcc's cse-skip-blocks so the pointer/field is reloaded after each join exactly like the ROM (an 'if (x>0) a=..' skip block keeps the cache and gives fewer loads). func_00417574 (395 insns) EXACT this way. Put the whole dxt expression INSIDE the push's w1 argument (O3_PUSH(0xF3000000, expr)): then the D++ store is emitted before the branches of the expression; computing it into locals first moves the push after them.
- ROUND 3: parameter copy `Q2Tex *t = p; if (p == NULL)` fixes 'bnez a0' vs 'bnez s0' (null test on the incoming register).
- ROUND 3: (s32)((u16)w << 16) >> 17 is how an lhu/sll16/sra17 triple is written when the field is s16 elsewhere (lh) - cast to u16 first then shift as int.
- ROUND 3: a 'goto done' + label inside the body can place an error/special block (p->state = 4) BEFORE the single epilogue: func_0041A0DC layout (last arm then ends with a jump so cross-jumping merges its tail into the first shared swc1 block). Putting the block after a 'return 0' gave two epilogues.
- ROUND 3: a permuter result is only a hint: its source is often convoluted. Re-deriving natural C from the asm (jump-table switch 'it = f(w8,0x3B8); switch(i){case n: it = f(w8,0x3B8+n);} it->txt = txt;') matched func_004215D8 at the first try after 4 rounds of permuter drafts failed.
- ROUND 3: `(long long)(A<<12) | (B | 0xED000000)` forces the OR order ((B|const) first) that the plain `A<<12 | B | const` gives wrongly (func_0041FCC0, 004192F8). Two O3_PUSH(expr,expr) pushes in a row with float->int expressions inside keep the D++ chain like the ROM (explicit `g = D++; g->w0=..` temporaries re-associate).
- ROUND 3: index-first addu on a struct sub-address: ((O3Pj*)(idx*0x4C8 + (s32)D))->sub (func_00420688); `(((D<<6)+0x380)+base)` puts the base addu last (func_0041C864).
- ROUND 3: temp reuse `new_var = (s32)(s0*fw);` before dsdx/dtdy and `(new_var>>5)` in the push fixes sra scheduling (func_004192F8).
- ROUND 3 (tooling): tools/perm_strip.py now keeps `inline` function definitions (earlier version dropped static inline helpers => permuter base did not compile). wip/pdrive.sh/pchain.sh/pres.sh queue permuter runs; wip/asmx.py prints any asm/*.s including alabel functions (tools/asmfn.py only finds glabel).
- ROUND 3: functions that are `alabel`s inside another file (func_0041C680, 00415D10) are not found by asmfn.py/m2c; use wip/asmx.py and wip/mktarget.py for permuter targets.

=== round-3 q03 findings ===
FINDINGS q03 (round 3, overlay A leftovers, ROM 0x192D70..0x1A17EC)  -- interim, see bottom for status

TYPES FIRST (biggest single win)
- Most leftovers failed in round 2 only because the shared global block D_801462C8 (lobby/config, 8 records of 0x96 bytes at +0xD0, +0x580 u8, +0x680 s32)
  was accessed through u8* arithmetic or per-function ad-hoc structs.  With ONE typed struct (include/q03_types.h: Q03Cfg { ... Q03Ent ent[8] @0xD0 ... }) and
  `D_801462C8->ent[i].fNN` (global array form `extern Q03Cfg D_801462C8[]`) the index-first `addu` operand order, the folded 0xD0 and the `lb 0x150(base)` accesses
  come out exactly (func_0042E640, 00427F50, 00427CE8, 00425854 went from 60..190 words off to EXACT).  D_80146398 is `D_801462C8->ent` (same address); a use
  through the symbol D_80146398[...] (absolute lui/%lo form) and one through a local base register can coexist in one function (func_00427F50: case 1 via g->ent[], default via D_80146398[]).
- Same idea for D_800E4F60 / D_800E4690 mode blocks: the two constructors/state machines are siblings; a field map shifted by 0x5AC lets you port a matched one to the other (func_0042A490 was
  written from func_00426AF8 and matched at the first compile except for the case-label range and one shift).
- Field accessed as lhu in one statement and lh in another: two different struct views of the same object (a local `Q03U *w = call()` with u16 fields for the first statement through the
  returned pointer, the typed s16 field through D_800E4690->unk978 for the following ones) (func_004264E4 / 00429C04, partial).

REGISTER ALLOCATION (gcc 2.8 global.c) -- understand the priority formula, then steer it
- allocno priority = n_refs * floor_log2(n_refs) / live_length.  The pseudo with the highest priority gets the LOWEST callee-saved reg (s0, s1 ...).  `-dg` + `-dl` dumps:
  wip/dump.sh prints "regs to allocate: ..." (allocation order) and "Register N used X times across Y insns" (the numbers of the formula) for every pseudo.
- func_00425854: one pair of temporaries (s1,s2) reused in three blocks has a SHORT live range -> high priority -> steals s0 from `row`.  Using separate block-local temporaries per
  block (six pseudos, each short) made `row` s0 exactly like the ROM.   Similarly func_0042A994: `found` in the ROM is a caller-saved a0 because its only assignment is AFTER the calls
  (write `found = 1;` once after the if, not twice; gcc then fills the beq slot with a copy of it from the join) -- if you assign it before, cse knows it is 1 across the calls and the
  value must live in a callee-saved reg.
- early `return -1`-style exits must be nested ifs with ONE `return s3` at the end if the ROM returns the variable (`addu v0,s3,zero` in the slots); with early returns gcc propagates the constant.
- loop invariants: loop.c refuses to hoist constants/address pairs when callee-saved regs are scarce (loops with calls); the ROM often shows `lui/ori 0x55555556` or `addiu v0,zero,2`
  re-materialised INSIDE a loop while ours hoists them: usually our function has fewer live variables than the original (an extra variable that lives across the loop calls).
  Reusing variables (same `s0` for the byte constants 0x6E/0x8C and later the loop counter) matters for the count of callee-saved registers (func_00429C04 / 0042E7CC / 00427690).

SOURCE-SHAPE LESSONS (new)
- call in BOTH arms of an if/else when the ROM shows a shared `jal` tail with argument setup in each arm (func_00427CE8: func_002A1C08(buf,fmt,v/60,v%60) in both arms).
- switch with default FIRST and `r = 0; break;` for the default, case N: return K for the others, `case 0: r = 2; break;` last (func_0042B474): the default value is hoisted into the
  delay slots of the compare tree and the last case falls into the shared `jr; nop`.
- switch whose arms write a temp x and a default that does `s2 = 0; goto join` bypassing `s2 = x` (func_0042ACB0): ROM has the arms assigning a0 and the default jumping past `addu s2,a0,zero`.
- empty case labels `case 9: case 8: break;` written BEFORE the first real case keep the 9-entry jump table / `sltiu 9` range (func_0042A490); case labels that are all 'end' are otherwise dropped.
- a compare result through a block-local temp: `s32 t = (x + 1 == sub(y)); r = t;` instead of `r = x + 1 == sub(y)` -- with r as the target gcc evaluates the left operand INTO r (extra
  live range, other register allocation) (func_0042B11C).  Together with `extern s32 D_80154010[]` (array form keeps the load after the earlier store).
- `a == 1 || (a != 0 && a < 5 && a >= 3)` : fold_range_test merges `a < 5 && a >= 3` into one sltiu range test; the ROM keeps three compares.  Writing the inner part as a ternary on a LOCAL
  copy `s32 m = D_801462D5; if (m == 1 || (m != 0 && (m < 5 ? m >= 3 : 0)))` keeps three signed compares (slti) (func_00426AF8).
- clamp `y = f; x = y - 1; f = x >= 0 ? y - (y >= x) : 0` must store through the local pointer `q = D_800E4690` (store through q, later re-read through the global reloads the pointer) and via a temp z
  (`z = ...; q->f = z;`) so there is ONE store at the join (func_00426AF8, 0042A490).
- `(s32)u8field >> 1` gives srl; to get ROM's `sra` load the byte into a block-local s32 first (`s32 u = h->unk10; s0 = u >> 1;`).
- `s32 one` that is a copy of a loop flag must be `s8` in func_004337EC (found by the permuter): the copy is made with a different pseudo mode.
- permuter trick again: wrapping one store in `do { ... } while (0);` (func_00424E30) acts as a scheduling barrier.
- volatile D_800D15E0 (stores 0 then 1 both kept) for the render-state struct at D_800D15E0.. (func_0042A1D0): without it the dead store is dropped and the whole scheduling changes.
- search loop helpers inlined: the ROM keeps `bnel ...,L; <i++ in slot>; j done; <idx=i in slot of j>` (inlined copy of func_00428300 in func_00427690); our static inline gives the
  `beql` variant (not solved).

TOOLING
- wip/rc.ps1 <list>: lean chk (syncs only include/ + wip/, 4 s instead of 40 s+; the first full sync took 8 minutes under load 120).
- wip/sd.sh <file> <fn> <rom> [flags]: side-by-side showing only the differing lines.  wip/v.py <variants-file> <fn>: many full-file variants in one chk run ('====' separated).
- wip/dump.sh <file> greg|lreg -dl -dg: register allocation dumps; wip/rawcc.ps1: raw gcc asm.
- wip/perm.sh / pq.sh: decomp-permuter wrapper (own perm dir ~/perm/q03_<fn>; tools/perm_strip.py must keep `static inline` helpers).  With a machine load of 120 expect 20+ minutes per
  improvement; look at the best output with wip/pdiff.sh / pbody.sh while it runs -- func_004337EC and func_00424E30 were found that way.


=== q03 second pass (new lessons; all verified EXACT unless stated) ===
CSE COPY CANONICALISATION (func_0042C1F8)
- `p = w;` (copy) is only kept by cse (make_regs_eqv) if p becomes the canonical register of the quantity: new reg becomes canonical iff
  (last_uid(p) > cse_basic_block_end AND last_uid(p) > last_uid(w)) OR first_uid(p) < first_uid(w).  Otherwise cse propagates w into every use
  and the copy disappears (ROM keeps `addu s0,s1,zero; sw v0,0x2C(s0)`).  A DEAD `p = 0;` as the very last statement of the function (after the loop that
  uses w) raises last_uid(p) beyond w's and keeps the copy -> EXACT.  (A dead set at the top does not help; flow deletes it before reg_scan.)
REGISTER ALLOCATION BY REFERENCE COUNT (func_004251F4, func_0042A994)
- global.c priority = n_refs*floor_log2(n_refs)/live_length.  A result variable returned from 37 switch arms got s1 only after every arm was written as
  `return r;` (37 refs instead of 5) -> it outranked the D_8015402C pointer.  A flag `s7 = a >= 0xC` vs loop count n: writing the flag as `s7 = 0xC; s7 = a >= s7;`
  (permuter) gives the ROM order.  Walking pointer `p = tbl; ... *p, p++` vs indexing `tbl[s3]`: indexing gives the ROM's giv init after the loop test (p lives in s4 later).
LOOP / STRENGTH REDUCTION FACTS (func_0042BD40, 004264E4)
- A pointer walked through a loop (`e++` or `e = &ents[i]`) gets its offset folded into the giv init (addiu s0,&ents+0x91, lbu -0x19(s0)).  The ROM keeps
  the base unfolded (lbu 0x78(v1), lbu 0x91(v1)) while still hoisting constants: write `e = &D_80146398[i];` as a BLOCK-LOCAL pointer inside the loop body
  (symbol base).  For a register base use a goto-loop (`L: ...; i++; e++; if (i < 4) goto L;`) + an explicit local `one = 1` for the hoisted constant.
- Several small `if (x == 1 || x == 3 || x == 4)` compare chains that jump into a later switch tree are really a `switch` with an empty `case 2: break;`
  (func_004264E4); `x < 5 && x >= 3` kept as 3 compares needs `m = x; if (m < 5 ? m >= 3 : 0)`.
- Statement duplication: a final call written in three places (func_0042E080: if (==2) { if (s6>=2) f(1); else f(v); } else f(v);) gives the ROM's
  bne/bnez/j/li layout; the ternary or `a = ...; f(a)` forms give beql / delay-slot li.
WIDGET (u16/s16) MATH BLOCK (func_0042F12C, 004264E4, 00429C04)
- `w = ECB0(..); D->f3C = w; w->u14 -= w->u18 (u16 view); D->f40 = D->f3C->s18 / 4; D->f3C->u14 += s18 - f40*4` : use ONE struct with a union {u16 u; s16 s;} at +0x18,
  assign through the D_800E54A4 global each time (cse forwards the store, no local copy, widget stays in v0), and compute the remainder as
  `m = D->f3C->w18.s - D->f40 * 4; { s32 r = m; D->f3C->u14 += r; }` with `m` a FUNCTION-level variable (changes local-alloc order so D=a2, q=v1, r=a3 as in the ROM; a
  plain block-local r gives lhu instead of lh for the second load and the wrong regs).  Use a separate temp (t) for widgets that are not stored in D (otherwise one `w`
  becomes callee-saved and steals s0 from the arg).
- Stack-passed `Vec3` locals are 16 bytes apart; per-player stores `base = (u8*)D + i*0xB68; *(s32*)(base+0xB84)` give the ROM's `addu v0,a1,s5` operand order.
- explicit local `id = 14;` before a `for (i = 3; i >= 0; i--)` store loop swaps the order of the hoisted `li v1,14` and the `li s2,3` init.
- float literal pools in overlay A are real literals: func_0042A1D0 only matched after 255.0f/4.0f/210.0f/255.0f replaced the extern floats (D_800E1ABC..1AC8) .
- permuter results must be verified: it happily adds semantic changes (dead stores that change the program) to lower its own score.
INCLUDE NOTES
- include/o04_types.h was replaced by the original o04 workspace's header (the previous q03 copy lacked O04W etc.); all previously EXACT functions still EXACT.
- include/q03_types.h grew: Q03WdU (widget with u14/u16 + union u/s at 0x18), Q03Hdr/Q03Lob (lobby block 0x3474, same layout as O05Lobby), Q03F60.unk3C8.

==================== ROUND 7 FINDINGS (workers s702, s704, s705, s706, s707; real toolchain @SN; helper tools in tools/agents/INDEX.txt) ====================
--- s702 ---
FINDINGS s702 (round 7, hard leftovers; @SN toolchain) - written incrementally

TOOLING (all in tools/ of s702, WSL python3; no Windows python exists on this box)
- tools/fk.sh <list> : fast chk (copies only the listed files + include/, ~1.5 s per call).  tools/rv.py <spec.py> : source variants as lists of (old,new)
  replacements of a BASE file -> ONE chk call, prints words differ per variant (the quickest loop for shape searches).  tools/sd.sh <file> <fn> <rom> [@SN..]
  side-by-side (FULL=1 shows everything).  tools/done.py : verify + copy to wip/out + results line.  tools/sndump.sh <file> "<cc1 dump flags>" + tools/rtlc.py
  (one-line RTL per insn) + tools/sdorder.sh (sched1 result order); `-fsched-verbose=5 -dS` prints the ready lists WITH priorities (see lesson 1).
- permuter @SN with zero relocation noise: tools/psetup.sh <fn> <wip/c/x.c> <rom> <ninsn> (= s601's perm_sn.sh) + tools/prun.sh <fn> <secs> <jobs>.
- tools/lu_match.py now runs tools/sn_expand.py (s602 fix for the mflo/lw-macro/mult hazard nop) and knows the token -nogas (cc1 without -mgas).

LESSONS
1. SCHED1 PRIORITY BOOST = "birthing insn" (haifa-sched adjust_priority/birthing_insn_p): an insn that SETs a pseudo which is live at that point AND has
   REG_N_SETS == 1 gets priority LAUNCH_PRIORITY (0x7f000001).  sched1 runs BACKWARDS, so such insns are placed as LATE as possible (close to their use);
   a pseudo with 2+ sets (e.g. `s = a + b; s = s * k;` or one variable reused for several roles) keeps its normal critical-path priority and floats to the
   TOP of the block (or above calls).  Consequence: when the ROM's load/add chain for a value sits where "source order" says, but ours floats up, the value
   must be a single-set local (one variable per role / one per inline expansion).  func_00226DAC: three angles written as u1/h1, h2, h3 (single-set each,
   no pins) -> EXACT; with one shared `s` (in-place `s = s * k`) the sum chain is hoisted to the top and the registers swap.  Verified with the dump
   `-dS -fsched-verbose=5`: "ready list at T-2: 42 (7f000001)" for single-set pseudos vs "40 (2)" for the multi-set one.
   Also: X = a+b and Y = X*k that are both block-local single-set pseudos get the SAME hard reg from local-alloc (in-place `mul.s f20,f20,f21`), including
   across calls (callee-saved), which a multi-set variable or a pin cannot reproduce faithfully (a pinned register var makes the def a HARD reg set: priority not
   boosted, deps with calls differ).
2. jump.c "if (c) { x = a; goto l; } x = b;" -> "x = a; if (c) goto l; x = b;" (conditions: insn before the goto is a lone `x = a` SET without notes, the
   next active insn after the goto is a single SET of the same x, the conditional jump targets the label right after the goto, test does not mention x).
   It makes `x = a` unconditional BEFORE the branch, so the tested temp cannot share x's register (func_00232394: ROM has `lh v0 ; bnez v0 ; li v0,1`).
3. loop.c runs BEFORE combine.  A byte read written as `((u8 *)p)[3]` is already a `(mem:QI (plus p 3))` at loop.c time = a DEST_ADDR giv, and loop.c combines it
   into any reduced giv of the same biv family (`lbu 3(s1)` becomes `lbu -1(s3)` when s3 = reduced p+4).  Written as `(*(u32 *)p & 0xFF)` it is a
   `(and (mem:SI p) 255)` for loop.c (not a giv, base stays the biv), and combine later turns lw+andi into `lbu 3(p)` (big endian low byte).  func_00242FD8
   went from 1 word off (after months of pad/permuter work: asm("" ::"r"(p1)) etc.) to EXACT with no pads.  Rule: whenever the ROM has `lbu 3(reg)` /
   `lhu 2(reg)` (low part of a word) next to an `lw 0(reg)` of the same pointer inside a loop, the source reads the WORD and masks.
4. tools/rv2.py ranks variants by the ALIGNED diff (difflib on mnemonics, structural insn inserts/deletes vs operand diffs); chk's positional "N words differ"
   is meaningless once an insn count shifts.  `python3 tools/adx.sh <file> <fn> <rom>` prints the aligned diff.  Use it for everything above ~100 insns.
5. Inlined helper whose parameter copy `move a0,v1` survives in the ROM (func_002343B8 busy(q)): `__asm__("" : "+r"(q));` as the first statement of the static
   inline helper (s505 lesson) fixed the first 100 words at once; then `u8 *pp = (u8 *)o + i; pv = pp[0x524]; pp[0x520] = c8;` stops loop.c from reducing the
   o+i giv of a 4-iteration byte loop (ROM recomputes `addu a1,s0,a0` at the loop top).
6. ORDER OF ARGUMENT-REGISTER SETUP vs a callee-saved init (func_0023D370: ROM `addiu s0,sp,48 ; move a0,s0 ; addiu a1,sp,16 ; addiu s2,s4,248 ; jal ; move a2,s2`).
   In `f(pv, &v10, pn = &m->nrm)` the pre-evaluated `pn =` set has the LOWEST luid of the call setup (args are expanded first, the a0/a1/a2 moves are emitted in the
   store phase), and both sched passes keep equal-priority insns in luid order, so s2 is initialised before a0/a1.  Fix = make the first two args real early
   sets that stay alive: `register T *ra0 asm("$4"), *ra1 asm("$5"); ra0 = pv; ra1 = &v10; __asm__ volatile("" : : "r"(ra0), "r"(ra1)); pn = &m->nrm;
   f(ra0, ra1, pn);`  The empty volatile asm USES the early sets (otherwise cse turns the call's self-moves `a0 = a0` into the real sets and deletes the early ones)
   and the later pn init/`move a2,s2`/call stay in front of the reorg slot filler (the asm must NOT sit directly before the jal or the delay slot stays a nop).
7. A literal that rounds to a different float than the ROM word is invisible to chk (relocs masked): 1.048576f -> 0x3F8637BD but the ROM pool has 0x3F8637BE (= 1.0485761f).
   tools/rocheck.py <file> <fn> <rom> checks that every hi/lo pair of the function addresses the same pool base and compares the pool words; a 4-byte pad before a
   jump table whose absolute address is 8-aligned (ROM pool at 0x..324) cannot be reproduced by a one-function object (ours has no pad), the rest of the pool must match.
8. jump.c / reorg interplay in func_00232394 (details in results.txt): the last arm of a boolean ladder must NOT be `if ((x = f()) != 0) { ok = 1; goto done; } zero: ok = 0;`
   (jump.c hoists `ok = 1` above the branch -> the lh temp can no longer share v0).  Test the value in the result variable itself and branch to a far `ok1:` block placed
   after the default call: `ok = o->unk5F6; if (ok != 0) goto ok1; ... ok1: ok = 1;`.  A pseudo that is set only ONCE (xx = D_800D70E8 as a call argument) is boosted by sched1
   and lands after the float arg load; a second (empty volatile asm "+r") set restores the ROM order.
9. Semantic bug check: when a draft has the right structure but odd register numbers, compare WHICH variable is modified: func_002343B8's draft added the 1024.0f*unk5C term
   to f2 but the ROM adds it to f1 (add.s f6,f6,f0 on the first loaded value); this changes the allocation priorities (refs of the modified variable).
10. `i = 0;` placed inside each arm of an if/else (before the two identical do-while byte-copy loops) instead of before the test fixed the delay-slot structure of a 4-way `||` test
    (func_002343B8: 12 structural -> 1).
11. PERMUTER = STRUCTURE/TEMP FINDER, then hand-minimise (func_002231B0: 13 operand diffs -> 3 words in one 13 min run -> EXACT after 8 rv2 variants).  Splice the best
    output back (tools/pharvest.sh), then try to REMOVE the junk it introduced (new_var temps, float instead of f32): usually only ONE of the mutations matters.  Here it was
    "the product p->f69C * 80.0f * in->f0 * 0.017453294f written as ONE inline expression inside the call argument instead of via a temp": the temp changed which of the two
    FP loads got the lower register (local-alloc numbers quantities in expression order; a temp splits the expression into two quantities with other priorities).
12. REGISTER PINS ON PARAMETER COPIES ARE USUALLY WRONG under @SN.  func_00238304 carried `register T *o asm("$23") = o_;`; removing it fixed the prologue order
    (`move s7,a0` before the call, `move s1,a1` in the jal delay slot) and 4 operand diffs.  Before keeping a pin, always run the variant without it (tools/unpinclimb.py does it).
13. FP register numbering follows LIVE RANGE LENGTH (local-alloc/global-alloc priority = refs/length): in func_00238304 the ROM computes `fx = rx*0.5+rz` BEFORE
    `fy = (ry*0.8+rw) - sy*18`; with the statements swapped rx's range was longer than ry's and the two pseudos swapped f1/f3 (12 operand diffs -> 0).  If two like-shaped
    FP values have swapped registers, swap the order of the statements that consume them.
14. TWO CONSECUTIVE `if (e != 0)` TESTS ON THE SAME POINTER ARE MERGED by jump.c thread_jumps (first branch is redirected to the end, second test deleted) while the ROM
    (func_0023AFE0: `beqz s0,L; ...calls...; L: beqzl s0,end`) keeps both.  thread_jumps only looks at the INSN RIGHT AFTER the label, so a copy between the tests prevents it:
        tt = sc->freeHead; if (tt != 0) { f(tt); g(tt); }  e = tt;  if (e != 0) { ... }
    (the `e = tt` move disappears when both pseudos get the same hard reg).  8 structural diffs -> 0.
15. `u16 mask16 = mask; if (mask16)` adds a register copy (`move s8,s4` + andi); `if (mask & 0xFFFF)` keeps it in the single pseudo (func_0023AFE0: 5 words).
    Constant `-1` in a loop test `while (n != -1)` is shared with the guard test via loop.c hoist + cse2 (`li v0,-1 ... move s8,v0`); a guard written as a separate
    `if (n != -1) { do {...} while (n != -1); }` also gives that.
16. Delay-slot fill of a call: the filler is the closest PRECEDING eligible insn (not a load/mtc1).  In func_0023AFE0 `off = 0.0f; inside = 0;` written AFTER
    `hh = func(id, o->w38, o->w40);` still end up before the jal but in the order the ROM wants (`move a0,s0` first, `move s7,zero` in the slot); written before the call
    the slot got `move a0,s0`.  Statement order around a call changes the sched2 luid order of independent insns.
17. Cubic smoothstep `tt*(tt*-2)*tt + tt*(tt*3)`: the ROM order of the two final multiplies is obtained with
        kn = tt * (tt * -2.0f); kn = kn * tt; tt = tt * (tt * 3.0f); k = kn + tt;
    (permuter result, func_002343B8): the register reuse of tt for the second product is the point.
18. Open: remaining 1-word differences are delay-slot annul flags (bc1t vs bc1tl at the first test of a `255<x ? 255 : x<0 ? 0 : 255<x ? 255 : (u32)x` chain, func_002343B8) and
    a duplicated `addiu a0` in a jal slot (func_00238304); both are decided inside reorg (mark_target_live_regs / redundant_insn) and no source variant tried changed them.
19. reorg.c facts that explain bc1t/bc1tl (branch-likely) and duplicated delay-slot insns (from gcc's reorg.c, same logic as 2.8.1): fill_simple_delay_slots runs first over ALL
    jumps/calls and only takes an insn from BEFORE the branch (for a call the closest preceding eligible non-load, non-mtc1 insn); fill_eager_delay_slots runs afterwards:
    predicted-taken branch (mostly_true_jump: loop-back, ...) -> target thread first, else fall-through first; an insn is put into the slot WITHOUT annul when it sets nothing
    that is live/needed on the opposite thread (mark_target_live_regs), otherwise the branch becomes the likely form (bc1tl).  The slot copy of a shared target is a COPY (own_thread=0),
    an unshared target block is MOVED.  So the choice plain/likely depends on liveness at the START of the other successor block and on whether another branch already took the slot insn:
    sources that only differ in value liveness (a variable assigned before vs after a test) can flip bc1t <-> bc1tl.  Not solved here (func_002343B8, func_00238304 are exactly this, 1 word each).
20. Statement-order sensitivity of the first hoisted constant in a loop preheader (func_00233DEC, unsolved, 4 words): the ROM order is `i=0; K(0x80000000 for u32 conversions);
    p=sym; idx=2` but ours is `i; idx; p; K` because loop.c moves invariant constants to the LOOP_BEG note, i.e. AFTER every explicit init.  A real outer loop would hoist K
    in front of the inits (checked: do{}while(0)/while(1){break;} wrappers are not loops for loop.c); empty volatile asm barriers stop the FP constants from moving; "+r" asm
    inits are worse.  The ROM source probably gets p/idx from a different construct (not found).
21. perm_strip.py (permuter base builder) used to DROP `static inline` helpers: a permuter base calling an undefined helper scores garbage (func_002343B8 first run: base 2340 for a
    1-word-off source).  Fixed in tools/perm_strip.py (keeps definitions that contain `inline`); check `base.o` vs target.o with objdump when the base score is much larger than expected.
22. gp = D_801468A0 - 0x5D8 style address (func_0022804C): to get the ROM's hoisted reg-based `addiu t1,a2,-1496` compute the symbol-relative pointer in the loop body AFTER a load through the
    same symbol (`lim = D_801468A0->unk18; gp = (T*)((u8*)D_801468A0 - 0x5D8);` at the top of the loop): cse1 then has &sym in a pseudo and keeps reg+const (cost 1) instead of folding to a
    two-insn constant, and loop.c can hoist it (unconditional); written inside the conditional block it is folded into the load offset (`lbu -1483(a2)`) by combine instead.
23. func_0023AFE0 (EXACT) FP numbering recipe: the same quantity allocation (local-alloc priority = refs/length, global t pseudo gets a free reg around the local temps) needs DIFFERENT
    source shapes in the two arms: arm 1 `t = e->f208; t += c->f20 * D_800D2988;` (loads D->f0 first, c->f20->f1, as the ROM), arm 2 `cur = e->f208; ... t = cur - c->f20 * D_800D2988;`
    with a separate pseudo `cur` (then t, the result, can take f0 while cur keeps f2).  Try each arm's expression shape independently (rv2 base = best file, one variant list per arm).
24. cse replaces a constant operand by a register that is known to hold the same constant when the insn pattern accepts a register there: with `if (a->n != 1) { prev = &a->v[a->n - 1]; ...}`
    the `li v0,1` of the compare was reused as shift count of the expanded `n*12` (sll v0,v1,1 became `sllv v0,v1,v0`, func_002406C0).  Writing the test as an early exit
    `if (a->n == 1) return 1; { ... }` (compare constant not live in a register when the multiply is emitted) gives the ROM's `sll v0,v1,0x1`.  25 structural -> 4.
26. func_00238304 (EXACT): a duplicated `addiu a0,...` in a jal delay slot (ours) vs `addiu a0` in the PRECEDING conditional branch's slot and a nop in the jal slot (ROM): the ROM
    has the call argument set up BEFORE the branch that skips the call.  `register T **la asm("$4") = &b->listE40;` assigned before `if (p->unk14 == 0) goto remove;` then `f(la)` and
    `g(la, p)` at the label reproduces it (the pinned hard-reg set stays before the beqz, reorg moves it into the beqz slot, nothing is left for the jal).  Same family as the
    arg-register barrier trick of func_0023D370 (lesson 6): when ROM slots/arg setups sit on the wrong side of a branch, hold the argument in a register-asm variable.
27. Dot-product temporaries (func_002406C0, 51 operand diffs -> 4): FP quantity allocation priority = refs/length, so how the three products of `c.x*d.x + c.y*d.y + c.z*d.z` are
    numbered depends on where the sum is consumed.  (a) `if (sum > 0.0f)` inline gives the ROM's f2/f1/f0 (x,y,z) only when the sum is a FRESH single-use temp
    (`sm = c.x*d.x + c.y*d.y + c.z*d.z; if (sm > 0.0f)`, one `sm` shared by both loops); reusing the variable `t` (which lives on) makes it a global pseudo (f20).
    (b) `len2 = e.e; t = (e.d) / len2;` -> `t = e.d; len2 = e.e; t = t / len2;` moved the d.x/d.y/d.z registers of loop 2 from f8,f7,f6 to the ROM's f3,f5,f4.
    Method that found both: let the permuter run 14 min on the structure-correct file, read WHICH mutation it kept (a `t = ...; if (t > 0)` temp), then hand-test that idea
    at every site with rv2 (each site separately and together).
28. `do { return 0; } while (0);` as the body of a late exit test (func_002406C0, EXACT): the do-while(0) creates LOOP_BEG/LOOP_END notes around the return, which changes loop.c's
    LABEL_OUTSIDE_LOOP_P marking / reorg's mostly_true_jump decision and therefore whether the branch's delay slot is filled from the TARGET (`bc1t epilogue ; move v0,zero`, ROM) or from the
    fall-through (`bc1t sharedblock ; move s0,s4`, ours).  Both loops needed it (4 structural -> 0).  Found by the permuter (it adds do{}while(0) wrappers); try it whenever a ROM
    `return`/`break` arm has its delay slot filled from the target and ours is filled from the fall-through.
29. A quotient that must land in the callee's result register (`neg.s f1,f22 ; div.s f0,f1,f0` where ours has div.s f1,f1,f0): `register f32 lim asm("$f0"); lim = -r / sqrtf(len2); if (t < lim)`
    (pin the QUOTIENT to $f0; pinning the sqrt result or using $f2 did not work).
